class StrictPromptContractError(ValueError):
    def __init__(self, code: str, message: str) -> None:
        super().__init__(message)
        self.code = code


@dataclass(frozen=True)
class PromptSlimmingAudit:
    old_text: str
    new_text: str
    old_bytes: int
    new_bytes: int
    removed_markers: frozenset[str]
    preserved_markers: frozenset[str]
    schema_hash: str

    def as_dict(self) -> dict[str, Any]:
        return {
            "old_bytes": self.old_bytes,
            "new_bytes": self.new_bytes,
            "removed_markers": sorted(self.removed_markers),
            "preserved_markers": sorted(self.preserved_markers),
            "schema_hash": self.schema_hash,
        }


def closed_object(
    properties: Mapping[str, Any],
    *,
    required: Sequence[str] | None = None,
) -> dict[str, Any]:
    ordered = {key: deepcopy(value) for key, value in properties.items()}
    return {
        "type": "object",
        "properties": ordered,
        "required": list(required) if required is not None else list(ordered),
        "additionalProperties": False,
    }


def _string(
    *,
    enum: Sequence[str] | None = None,
    const: str | None = None,
    min_length: int | None = None,
    max_length: int | None = None,
) -> dict[str, Any]:
    schema: dict[str, Any] = {"type": "string"}
    if enum is not None:
        schema["enum"] = list(enum)
    if const is not None:
        schema["const"] = const
    if min_length is not None:
        schema["minLength"] = min_length
    if max_length is not None:
        schema["maxLength"] = max_length
    return schema


def _number(*, minimum: float = 0, maximum: float = 1) -> dict[str, Any]:
    return {"type": "number", "minimum": minimum, "maximum": maximum}


def _integer(*, minimum: int, maximum: int) -> dict[str, Any]:
    return {"type": "integer", "minimum": minimum, "maximum": maximum}


def _array(
    items: Mapping[str, Any],
    *,
    min_items: int | None = None,
    max_items: int | None = None,
) -> dict[str, Any]:
    schema: dict[str, Any] = {"type": "array", "items": deepcopy(dict(items))}
    if min_items is not None:
        schema["minItems"] = min_items
    if max_items is not None:
        schema["maxItems"] = max_items
    return schema


def _evidence_array(*, required: bool = True) -> dict[str, Any]:
    return _array(
        _string(min_length=1),
        min_items=1 if required else 0,
    )


def _inspiration_call_a_schema(
    evaluation_contract: Mapping[str, Any],
    *,
    contract_profile: str | None = None,
) -> dict[str, Any]:
    signals = (
        [(key, list(reasons)) for key, reasons in LATEST_INSPIRATION_REDLINE_SIGNALS]
        if contract_profile == LATEST_INSPIRATION_CONTRACT_PROFILE
        else inspiration_redline_signals(evaluation_contract.get("redline_policy"))
    )
    if not signals:
        raise StrictPromptContractError(
            "strict_prompt_redline_contract_invalid",
            "灵感图调用 A 没有可冻结的红线信号",
        )
    redline_keys = tuple(key for key, _reasons in signals)
    reasons = tuple(dict.fromkeys(reason for _key, values in signals for reason in values))
    redline_flags = closed_object({key: {"type": "boolean"} for key in redline_keys})
    # 紧凑形状：只列命中项的 {key, evidence}，未触发的红线不再各占一个空数组
    # （8 条红线的空骨架约 200 字/次，是调用 A 输出里最大的固定开销）。
    # 适配器入口把它展开成逐 key 数组的规范形状，判定逻辑不变。
    redline_evidence = _array(
        closed_object(
            {
                "key": _string(enum=redline_keys),
                "evidence": _string(min_length=1),
            }
        )
    )
    hard_defect_values = (
        LATEST_INSPIRATION_HARD_DEFECT_VALUES
        if contract_profile == LATEST_INSPIRATION_CONTRACT_PROFILE
        else tuple(sorted(INSPIRATION_HARD_DEFECT_VALUES))
    )
    defect_evidence = closed_object(
        {
            "key": _string(enum=hard_defect_values),
            "evidence": _string(min_length=1),
        }
    )
    image_defects_enabled = inspiration_image_defects_enabled(evaluation_contract)
    top_properties: dict[str, Any] = {
        "redline_triggered": redline_flags,
        "reason": _array(_string(enum=reasons), max_items=len(reasons)),
        "hard_defects": _array(
            _string(enum=hard_defect_values),
            max_items=len(hard_defect_values),
        ),
    }
    decisive_properties: dict[str, Any] = {
        "redline_triggered": redline_evidence,
        "hard_defects": _array(defect_evidence),
    }
    if image_defects_enabled:
        image_defect_evidence = closed_object(
            {
                "key": _string(enum=_INSPIRATION_IMAGE_DEFECT_VALUES),
                "evidence": _string(min_length=1),
            }
        )
        top_properties["image_defects"] = _array(
            _string(enum=_INSPIRATION_IMAGE_DEFECT_VALUES),
            max_items=len(_INSPIRATION_IMAGE_DEFECT_VALUES),
        )
        decisive_properties["image_defects"] = _array(image_defect_evidence)
    top_properties["decisive_evidence"] = closed_object(decisive_properties)
    top_properties.update(
        {
            "decision_status": _string(enum=("complete", "uncertain")),
            "uncertain_fields": _array(_string(min_length=1)),
            "track_classification": _string(
                enum=("class_one", "class_two", "class_three")
            ),
            "track_confidence": _number(),
            "media_type": _string(
                enum=("real_photo", "3d_render", "ai_generated", "other")
            ),
            "media_confidence": _number(),
            "trait": _string(enum=("实景照片", "3D数字效果图", "AI图", "其它")),
            "primary_category": _string(enum=_INSPIRATION_PRIMARY_CATEGORIES),
            "secondary_category": _string(),
            "classification_confidence": _number(),
        }
    )
    if contract_profile == LATEST_INSPIRATION_CONTRACT_PROFILE:
        # 运营新机制要求调用A同时产出生产字段 title / seotitle（业务上 10/28 字内，
        # 由正文约束；schema 只保证非空与上限，避免模型多几个字就整条被拒）。
        top_properties["title"] = _string(min_length=1, max_length=40)
        top_properties["seotitle"] = _string(min_length=1, max_length=80)
    return closed_object(top_properties)


def _inspiration_call_b_schema(
    *, contract_profile: str | None = None,
    dimension_keys: tuple[str, ...] = DIMENSION_KEYS,
    dimension_descriptions: Mapping[str, str] | None = None,
) -> dict[str, Any]:
    # 紧凑形状：逐维证据与不足各一句话（字符串，不足可为空串），基座校验入口展开成数组。
    dimension = closed_object(
        {
            "grade": _integer(minimum=1, maximum=5),
            "evidence": _string(min_length=1),
            "shortcomings": _string(),
        }
    )
    dimensions: dict[str, Any] = {}
    for key in dimension_keys:
        item = deepcopy(dimension)
        description: str | None = None
        if dimension_descriptions is not None:
            description = dimension_descriptions.get(key)
        elif contract_profile == LATEST_INSPIRATION_CONTRACT_PROFILE:
            description = _LATEST_INSPIRATION_DIMENSION_DESCRIPTIONS.get(key)
        if description:
            item["description"] = description
        dimensions[key] = item
    properties: dict[str, Any] = {
        "contract_version": _string(
            const=(
                LATEST_FOUNDATION_VERSION
                if contract_profile == LATEST_INSPIRATION_CONTRACT_PROFILE
                else FOUNDATION_VERSION
            )
        ),
        "aesthetic_score": _integer(minimum=0, maximum=100),
        "dimensions": closed_object(dimensions),
        "overall_evidence": _string(min_length=1),
        "confidence": _number(),
    }
    if contract_profile == LATEST_INSPIRATION_CONTRACT_PROFILE:
        # 紧凑形状：只列命中的来源扣分项 {key, evidence}；三项全未命中时为空数组。
        properties["source_deductions"] = _array(
            closed_object(
                {
                    "key": _string(enum=LATEST_SOURCE_DEDUCTION_KEYS),
                    "evidence": _string(min_length=1),
                }
            )
        )
    return closed_object(properties)


def _model_3d_su_call_a_schema() -> dict[str, Any]:
    risk_strings = {
        key: _string()
        for key in (
            "watermark",
            "unrendered",
            "brand_trademark",
            "brand_name",
            "chinese",
            "film_ip",
            "celebrities",
            "game_ip",
            "other_ip",
            "qr_code",
            "landmark",
            "religion",
        )
    }
    return closed_object(
        {
            "classification": closed_object(
                {
                    "scope_status": _string(
                        enum=("in_scope", "boundary", "out_of_scope")
                    ),
                    "primary_category": _string(enum=_MODEL_3D_SU_PRIMARY_CATEGORIES),
                    "primary_confidence": _number(),
                    "evidence": _evidence_array(),
                    # V29 分类定级表短路：平台规则按 doc_primary/doc_secondary 精确匹配
                    "doc_primary": _string(),
                    "doc_secondary": _string(),
                    "doc_tertiary": _string(),
                    "doc_group": _string(enum=_MODEL_3D_SU_DOC_GROUPS),
                }
            ),
            "production_fields": closed_object(
                {
                    "title": _string(max_length=10),
                    "seotitle": _string(max_length=28),
                    "category": _string(),
                    "style": _string(),
                    "tags": _array(_string(min_length=1), min_items=4),
                    "cons": _string(),
                    "design": _string(max_length=50),
                    "score": _integer(minimum=0, maximum=100),
                    # v15/v29 提示词要求写入 cg_model 等机器标记，不再限定为灵感图业务枚举
                    "reason": _array(_string(min_length=1)),
                    # 允许 CG_GAME_ANIMATION_REDLINE / UNRENDERED_WHITE_MODEL 等机器标记与自由描述
                    "image_defects": _string(max_length=400),
                    "trait": _string(enum=("3D数字效果图", "其它")),
                }
            ),
            "image_quality": closed_object(
                {"quality_severity": _string(enum=sorted(QUALITY_SEVERITY_VALUES))}
            ),
            "media_form": closed_object(
                {
                    "rendered_status": closed_object(
                        {
                            "status": _string(enum=("yes", "no", "uncertain")),
                            "confidence": _number(),
                            "evidence": _evidence_array(),
                        }
                    )
                }
            ),
            "model_3d_su_fields": closed_object(
                {**risk_strings, "high_risk": _array(_string(min_length=1))}
            ),
        }
    )


def _model_3d_su_call_b_schema() -> dict[str, Any]:
    dimension = closed_object(
        {
            "grade": _integer(minimum=1, maximum=5),
            "evidence": _evidence_array(),
        }
    )
    return closed_object(
        {
            "aesthetic_score": _integer(minimum=0, maximum=100),
            "aesthetic_evidence": _evidence_array(),
            "aesthetic_confidence": _number(),
            "dimensions": closed_object(
                {key: dimension for key in MODEL_3D_SU_DIMENSION_KEYS}
            ),
            "overall_note": _string(max_length=200),
        }
    )


def _contract(name: str, description: str, schema: Mapping[str, Any]) -> dict[str, Any]:
    return {
        "mode": "json_schema",
        "name": name,
        "description": description,
        "strict": True,
        "schema": deepcopy(dict(schema)),
    }


def strict_output_contract(
    category_key: str,
    stage: str,
    *,
    evaluation_contract: Mapping[str, Any],
    route: str | None = None,
    contract_profile: str | None = None,
) -> dict[str, Any]:
    if not isinstance(evaluation_contract, Mapping):
        raise StrictPromptContractError(
            "strict_prompt_evaluation_contract_invalid",
            "评测合同必须是对象",
        )
    normalized_stage = stage.strip().upper() if isinstance(stage, str) else ""
    if contract_profile not in {None, LATEST_INSPIRATION_CONTRACT_PROFILE}:
        raise StrictPromptContractError(
            "strict_prompt_contract_profile_unknown",
            f"严格输出合同配置不存在：{contract_profile}",
        )
    if contract_profile is not None and category_key != "inspiration_image":
        raise StrictPromptContractError(
            "strict_prompt_contract_profile_unexpected",
            f"{category_key} 不接受灵感图合同配置",
        )
    if category_key == "knowledge_graph":
        if normalized_stage != "A":
            raise StrictPromptContractError(
                "strict_prompt_stage_unknown",
                f"知识图谱不支持调用阶段 {stage}",
            )
        if not route:
            raise StrictPromptContractError(
                "strict_prompt_route_required",
                "知识图谱必须指定冻结路线",
            )
        try:
            return knowledge_graph_strict_output_contract(route)
        except ValueError as exc:
            raise StrictPromptContractError(
                "strict_prompt_route_unknown",
                f"知识图谱严格输出路线不存在：{route}",
            ) from exc
    if route is not None:
        raise StrictPromptContractError(
            "strict_prompt_route_unexpected",
            f"{category_key} 不接受知识图谱路线参数",
        )
    if category_key == "inspiration_image" and normalized_stage == "A":
        return _contract(
            "inspiration_call_a",
            "灵感图红线、缺陷、赛道、媒介与分类前检",
            _inspiration_call_a_schema(
                evaluation_contract,
                contract_profile=contract_profile,
            ),
        )
    if category_key == "inspiration_image" and normalized_stage == "B":
        from .inspiration_aesthetic_foundation import _resolve_dimension_keys
        # 模型被要求输出的维度集必须与后续校验/固化同源（都来自评测合同），
        # 缺省仍回落冻结八维；否则模型吐六维而校验要八维会整批被拒。
        dimension_keys = _resolve_dimension_keys(evaluation_contract)
        return _contract(
            "inspiration_call_b",
            "灵感图锚点美感评分",
            _inspiration_call_b_schema(
                contract_profile=contract_profile,
                dimension_keys=dimension_keys,
            ),
        )
    if category_key == "model_3d_su" and normalized_stage == "A":
        return _contract(
            "model_3d_su_call_a",
            "3D/SU 分类、生产字段、媒介与风险前检",
            _model_3d_su_call_a_schema(),
        )
    if category_key == "model_3d_su" and normalized_stage == "B":
        return _contract(
            "model_3d_su_call_b",
            "3D/SU 五维美感评分",
            _model_3d_su_call_b_schema(),
        )
    if category_key not in {"inspiration_image", "model_3d_su"}:
        raise StrictPromptContractError(
            "strict_prompt_category_unknown",
            f"不支持严格输出合同的评测类目：{category_key}",
        )
    raise StrictPromptContractError(
        "strict_prompt_stage_unknown",
        f"{category_key} 不支持调用阶段 {stage}",
    )


def merged_strict_output_contract(
    category_key: str,
    stages: Sequence[str],
    *,
    evaluation_contract: Mapping[str, Any],
    name: str,
    description: str = "",
    contract_profile: str | None = None,
) -> dict[str, Any]:
    if (
        not isinstance(stages, Sequence)
        or isinstance(stages, (str, bytes))
        or not stages
    ):
        raise StrictPromptContractError(
            "strict_prompt_stages_invalid",
            "合并严格输出合同至少需要一个调用阶段",
        )
    contracts = [
        strict_output_contract(
            category_key,
            stage,
            evaluation_contract=evaluation_contract,
            contract_profile=contract_profile,
        )
        for stage in stages
    ]
    return merge_strict_prompt_contracts(
        contracts,
        name=name,
        description=description,
    )


_SHAPE_MARKERS = {
    "只输出一个严格JSON对象": ("只输出一个严格JSON对象", "只输出一个严格 JSON 对象"),
    "顶层和逐维字段必须严格遵循": ("顶层和逐维字段必须严格遵循",),
    "必须返回以下结构": ("必须返回以下结构",),
    "输出结构": ("输出结构",),
    "Preserve every key": ("Preserve every key",),
}
_BUSINESS_MARKERS = {
    "75分边界",
    "90分边界",
    "第五张才是待评对象",
    "相邻锚点比较",
}


def prompt_slimming_audit(
    old_text: str,
    new_text: str,
    contract: Mapping[str, Any],
) -> PromptSlimmingAudit:
    if not isinstance(old_text, str) or not isinstance(new_text, str):
        raise StrictPromptContractError(
            "strict_prompt_audit_text_invalid",
            "提示词审计输入必须是文本",
        )
    schema = contract.get("schema") if isinstance(contract, Mapping) else None
    if not isinstance(schema, Mapping):
        raise StrictPromptContractError(
            "strict_prompt_audit_contract_invalid",
            "提示词审计必须绑定严格 Schema",
        )
    removed = frozenset(
        name
        for name, variants in _SHAPE_MARKERS.items()
        if any(marker in old_text for marker in variants)
        and not any(marker in new_text for marker in variants)
    )
    preserved = frozenset(
        marker
        for marker in _BUSINESS_MARKERS
        if marker in old_text and marker in new_text
    )
    return PromptSlimmingAudit(
        old_text=old_text,
        new_text=new_text,
        old_bytes=len(old_text.encode("utf-8")),
        new_bytes=len(new_text.encode("utf-8")),
        removed_markers=removed,
        preserved_markers=preserved,
        schema_hash=hashlib.sha256(canonical_json(schema).encode("utf-8")).hexdigest(),
    )

===== FILE backend/app/strict_prompt_release.py =====
"""Materialize immutable strict-schema, high-fidelity workflow candidates.

Preview is deliberately the default and performs no writes.  Apply mode creates
new draft PromptVersion and WorkflowVersion rows, binds them to existing assets,
and records their rollback lineage.  It never activates a candidate or enables
physical downstream projection.
"""

class StrictPromptReleaseError(ValueError):
    def __init__(self, code: str, message: str) -> None:
        super().__init__(message)
        self.code = code


@dataclass(frozen=True)
class PromptCandidateReceipt:
    node_instance_id: str
    asset_key: str
    stage: str
    version: str
    source_prompt_id: int
    candidate_prompt_id: int | None
    rollback_prompt_id: int
    source_content_hash: str
    candidate_content_hash: str
    source_bytes: int
    candidate_bytes: int
    byte_delta: int
    output_contract_hash: str
    vision_policy_hash: str


@dataclass(frozen=True)
class RuleCandidateReceipt:
    node_instance_id: str
    asset_key: str
    module_type_key: str
    source_rule_module_id: int
    candidate_rule_module_id: int | None
    rollback_rule_module_id: int
    source_content_hash: str
    candidate_content_hash: str


@dataclass(frozen=True)
class WorkflowCandidateReceipt:
    category_key: str
    version: str
    status: str
    source_workflow_id: int
    source_workflow_version: str
    source_canonical_hash: str
    rollback_workflow_id: int
    candidate_workflow_id: int | None
    canonical_hash: str | None
    plan_hash: str
    model_id: str
    prompts: tuple[PromptCandidateReceipt, ...]
    rules: tuple[RuleCandidateReceipt, ...]


@dataclass(frozen=True)
class StrictPromptReleaseReceipt:
    schema_version: str
    mode: str
    workflows: tuple[WorkflowCandidateReceipt, ...]

    def as_dict(self) -> dict[str, Any]:
        return asdict(self)

class _RulePlan:

def _sha256(value: Any) -> str:
    return hashlib.sha256(canonical_json(value).encode("utf-8")).hexdigest()


def _inspiration_terminal_rules() -> list[dict[str, Any]]:
    complete = {
        "path": "output.decision_status",
        "op": "eq",
        "value": "complete",
    }
    redline_any = [
        {
            "path": f"output.redline_triggered.{key}",
            "op": "eq",
            "value": True,
        }
        for key, _reasons in LATEST_INSPIRATION_REDLINE_SIGNALS
    ]
    defect_values = list(LATEST_INSPIRATION_HARD_DEFECT_VALUES)
    hard_defect_any = [
        {
            "path": f"output.hard_defects.{index}",
            "op": "in",
            "value": defect_values,
        }
        for index in range(len(defect_values))
    ]
    shared = {
        "rule_version": "short-circuit-rule-v1",
        "skip_roles": list(_INSPIRATION_SKIP_ROLES),
    }
    return [
        {
            **shared,
            "rule_key": "inspiration-redline-l5-v2",
            "when": {"all": [complete, {"any": redline_any}]},
            "target_level": "L5",
            "priority": 1000,
        },
        {
            **shared,
            "rule_key": "inspiration-class-two-l3-v2",
            "when": {
                "all": [
                    complete,
                    {
                        "path": "output.track_classification",
                        "op": "eq",
                        "value": "class_two",
                    },
                ]
            },
            "target_level": "L3",
            "priority": 800,
        },
        {
            **shared,
            "rule_key": "inspiration-class-three-l4-v2",
            "when": {
                "all": [
                    complete,
                    {
                        "path": "output.track_classification",
                        "op": "eq",
                        "value": "class_three",
                    },
                ]
            },
            "target_level": "L4",
            "priority": 900,
        },
        {
            **shared,
            "rule_key": "inspiration-hard-defect-l4-v2",
            "when": {"all": [complete, {"any": hard_defect_any}]},
            "target_level": "L4",
            "priority": 900,
        },
    ]


def _release_transform_plan(category_key: str) -> dict[str, Any] | None:
    if category_key != "inspiration_image":
        return None
    return {
        "contract_profile": LATEST_INSPIRATION_CONTRACT_PROFILE,
        "node_roles": deepcopy(_INSPIRATION_NODE_ROLES),
        "short_circuit_rules": _inspiration_terminal_rules(),
    }


def _prompt_content(
    *,
    stage: str,
    system_prompt: str,
    user_prompt: str,
    rubric_version: str,
) -> dict[str, Any]:
    return {
        "stage": stage,
        "system_prompt": system_prompt,
        "user_prompt": user_prompt,
        "rubric_version": rubric_version,
    }


def _validation_ok(workflow: WorkflowVersion) -> bool:
    try:
        report = json.loads(workflow.validation_report_json or "{}")
    except json.JSONDecodeError:
        return False
    return (
        workflow.status == "active"
        and workflow.workflow_schema_version == "workflow-v3"
        and isinstance(report, Mapping)
        and report.get("ok") is True
    )


def _manifest_uses_current_seed_model(
    db: Session,
    *,
    manifest: Mapping[str, Any],
) -> bool:
    """Return true only when every prompt node uses the frozen Seed 2.0 model.

    Source selection may encounter newer workflows owned by another runtime
    (for example the isolated Qwen validation chain).  Those workflows remain
    valid and active, but cannot be used as lineage for the Doubao release.
    Full registry/runtime capability validation still happens later in
    ``_current_model_policy_hash`` so malformed Seed registrations fail closed.
    """

    has_prompt = False
    for node in manifest.get("nodes", []):
        if not isinstance(node, Mapping) or node.get("kind") != "prompt":
            continue
        has_prompt = True
        config = node.get("config")
        registry_id = (
            config.get("model_registry_id") if isinstance(config, Mapping) else None
        )
        registry = (
            db.get(ModelRegistryEntry, registry_id)
            if isinstance(registry_id, int) and not isinstance(registry_id, bool)
            else None
        )
        if (
            registry is None
            or not registry.active
            or registry.model_id != CURRENT_SEED_20_MODEL_ID
        ):
            return False
    return has_prompt


def _source_workflow(
    db: Session,
    *,
    category_key: str,
    release_version: str,
) -> WorkflowVersion:
    completed_runs = db.scalars(
        select(BaselineRegressionRun)
        .where(
            BaselineRegressionRun.category_key == category_key,
            BaselineRegressionRun.status == "completed",
            BaselineRegressionRun.workflow_version_id.is_not(None),
        )
        .order_by(BaselineRegressionRun.id.desc())
    ).all()
    for run in completed_runs:
        workflow = db.get(WorkflowVersion, run.workflow_version_id)
        if workflow is not None and _validation_ok(workflow):
            manifest = persisted_workflow_manifest(workflow)
            if (
                manifest.get("category_key") == category_key
                and _manifest_uses_current_seed_model(db, manifest=manifest)
            ):
                return workflow

    definition = db.scalar(
        select(WorkflowDefinition).where(
            WorkflowDefinition.workflow_key == f"{category_key}.evaluation"
        )
    )
    if definition is None:
        raise StrictPromptReleaseError(
            "strict_release_source_missing",
            f"{category_key} 不存在可解析的工作流定义",
        )
    candidates = db.scalars(
        select(WorkflowVersion)
        .where(
            WorkflowVersion.workflow_definition_id == definition.id,
            WorkflowVersion.version != release_version,
            WorkflowVersion.status == "active",
        )
        .order_by(WorkflowVersion.id.desc())
    ).all()
    for workflow in candidates:
        if not _validation_ok(workflow):
            continue
        manifest = persisted_workflow_manifest(workflow)
        if (
            manifest.get("category_key") == category_key
            and "stock_regression" in (manifest.get("run_modes") or [])
            and _manifest_uses_current_seed_model(db, manifest=manifest)
        ):
            return workflow
    raise StrictPromptReleaseError(
        "strict_release_source_missing",
        f"{category_key} 没有通过验证的冻结回归工作流",
    )


def _evaluation_contract(
    db: Session,
    *,
    category_key: str,
    source_manifest: Mapping[str, Any],
) -> dict[str, Any]:
    policy = source_manifest.get("resource_policy")
    identity = policy.get("migration_identity") if isinstance(policy, Mapping) else None
    revision_id = identity.get("legacy_revision_id") if isinstance(identity, Mapping) else None
    revision = (
        db.get(CategoryEvaluationV3Revision, revision_id)
        if isinstance(revision_id, int) and not isinstance(revision_id, bool)
        else None
    )
    if revision is None or revision.category_key != category_key:
        projection = (
            policy.get("legacy_projection_snapshot")
            if identity is None and isinstance(policy, Mapping)
            else None
        )
        workflow_fields = (
            projection.get("workflow_fields")
            if isinstance(projection, Mapping)
            and projection.get("available") is True
            else None
        )
        workflow_fields_hash = (
            projection.get("workflow_fields_hash")
            if isinstance(projection, Mapping)
            else None
        )
        projected_contract = (
            workflow_fields.get("contract")
            if isinstance(workflow_fields, Mapping)
            else None
        )
        if (
            isinstance(workflow_fields, Mapping)
            and isinstance(workflow_fields_hash, str)
            and workflow_fields_hash == _sha256(workflow_fields)
            and isinstance(projected_contract, Mapping)
            and projected_contract.get("category_key") == category_key
        ):
            return deepcopy(dict(projected_contract))
        raise StrictPromptReleaseError(
            "strict_release_lineage_contract_missing",
            f"{category_key} 的冻结源工作流缺少唯一历史评测合同",
        )
    bundle = revision_bundle(revision)
    contract = bundle.get("contract")
    if not isinstance(contract, Mapping):
        raise StrictPromptReleaseError(
            "strict_release_lineage_contract_invalid",
            f"{category_key} 的冻结历史评测合同损坏",
        )
    return deepcopy(dict(contract))


def _current_model_policy_hash(
    db: Session,
    *,
    source_config: Mapping[str, Any],
) -> tuple[str, str]:
    model_id = source_config.get("model_registry_id")
    model = (
        db.get(ModelRegistryEntry, model_id)
        if isinstance(model_id, int) and not isinstance(model_id, bool)
        else None
    )
    if model is None or not model.active:
        raise StrictPromptReleaseError(
            "strict_release_model_missing",
            "候选提示词节点没有可冻结的现役模型",
        )
    if model.model_id != CURRENT_SEED_20_MODEL_ID:
        raise StrictPromptReleaseError(
            "strict_release_model_version_mismatch",
            (
                "候选快照必须使用 doubao-seed-2-0-lite-260428，"
                f"当前节点为 {model.model_id}"
            ),
        )
    source_model = (
        db.get(ModelConfig, model.source_model_config_id)
        if model.source_model_config_id is not None
        else None
    )
    if source_model is None or source_model.model_id != CURRENT_SEED_20_MODEL_ID:
        raise StrictPromptReleaseError(
            "strict_release_source_model_version_mismatch",
            "模型注册表与运行模型没有共同冻结到 260428",
        )
    try:
        registry_profile = json.loads(model.vision_capability_profile_json or "{}")
        runtime_profile = json.loads(
            source_model.vision_capability_profile_json or "{}"
        )
        capability = parse_vision_capability_profile(
            registry_profile,
            provider=model.provider,
            model_id=model.model_id,
        )
        runtime_capability = parse_vision_capability_profile(
            runtime_profile,
            provider=source_model.provider,
            model_id=source_model.model_id,
        )
    except (json.JSONDecodeError, ValueError) as exc:
        raise StrictPromptReleaseError(
            "strict_release_vision_capability_invalid",
            "260428 模型图片能力档案缺失或损坏",
        ) from exc
    if canonical_json(capability.as_dict()) != canonical_json(
        runtime_capability.as_dict()
    ):
        raise StrictPromptReleaseError(
            "strict_release_vision_capability_drift",
            "模型注册表与运行模型的图片能力档案不一致",
        )
    policy = parse_vision_input_policy(
        STRICT_HIGH_POLICY,
        capability=capability,
        legacy_if_missing=False,
    )
    return model.model_id, vision_policy_hash(policy, capability)


def _strict_prompt_text(category_key: str, stage: str) -> str:
    filename = _PROMPT_FILES.get((category_key, stage))
    if filename is None:
        raise StrictPromptReleaseError(
            "strict_release_prompt_stage_unsupported",
            f"{category_key} 的提示词阶段 {stage} 不在本次候选范围",
        )
    try:
        text = (_PROMPT_ROOT / filename).read_text(encoding="utf-8").strip()
    except OSError as exc:
        raise StrictPromptReleaseError(
            "strict_release_prompt_source_missing",
            f"严格提示词源文件缺失：{filename}",
        ) from exc
    if not text:
        raise StrictPromptReleaseError(
            "strict_release_prompt_source_empty",
            f"严格提示词源文件为空：{filename}",
        )
    return text


def _prompt_plans(
    db: Session,
    *,
    category_key: str,
    release_version: str,
    source_manifest: Mapping[str, Any],
) -> tuple[tuple[_PromptPlan, ...], str]:
    evaluation_contract = (
        _evaluation_contract(
            db,
            category_key=category_key,
            source_manifest=source_manifest,
        )
        if category_key != "knowledge_graph"
        else {}
    )
    kg_sources = knowledge_graph_prompt_sources() if category_key == "knowledge_graph" else {}
    plans: list[_PromptPlan] = []
    model_ids: set[str] = set()
    for node in source_manifest.get("nodes", []):
        if not isinstance(node, Mapping) or node.get("kind") != "prompt":
            continue
        node_id = node.get("node_instance_id")
        config = node.get("config")
        if not isinstance(node_id, str) or not isinstance(config, Mapping):
            raise StrictPromptReleaseError(
                "strict_release_prompt_node_invalid",
                "冻结源工作流存在无效提示词节点",
            )
        prompt_id = config.get("prompt_version_id")
        source = (
            db.get(PromptVersion, prompt_id)
            if isinstance(prompt_id, int) and not isinstance(prompt_id, bool)
            else None
        )
        asset_key = config.get("asset_key")
        if source is None or not isinstance(asset_key, str) or not asset_key:
            raise StrictPromptReleaseError(
                "strict_release_prompt_lineage_missing",
                f"提示词节点 {node_id} 缺少冻结版本或资产绑定",
            )
        model_name, policy_hash = _current_model_policy_hash(
            db,
            source_config=config,
        )
        model_ids.add(model_name)
        if category_key == "knowledge_graph":
            contract_key = config.get("output_contract_key")
            if not isinstance(contract_key, str):
                raise StrictPromptReleaseError(
                    "strict_release_kg_contract_missing",
                    f"知识图谱节点 {node_id} 缺少路由输出合同",
                )
            route_key = node_id.removeprefix("prompt_")
            system_prompt = kg_sources.get(route_key, "")
            contract = strict_output_contract(
                "knowledge_graph",
                "A",
                evaluation_contract={},
                route=contract_key,
            )
        else:
            system_prompt = _strict_prompt_text(category_key, source.stage)
            contract = strict_output_contract(
                category_key,
                source.stage,
                evaluation_contract=evaluation_contract,
                contract_profile=(
                    LATEST_INSPIRATION_CONTRACT_PROFILE
                    if category_key == "inspiration_image"
                    else None
                ),
            )
        if not system_prompt:
            raise StrictPromptReleaseError(
                "strict_release_prompt_source_missing",
                f"提示词节点 {node_id} 没有严格候选正文",
            )
        candidate_version = f"{release_version}-{asset_key}"
        plans.append(
            _PromptPlan(
                node_instance_id=node_id,
                asset_key=asset_key,
                source=source,
                candidate_version=candidate_version,
                system_prompt=system_prompt,
                user_prompt=source.user_prompt,
                output_contract=contract,
                output_contract_hash=_sha256(contract),
                vision_policy_hash=policy_hash,
            )
        )
    if not plans:
        raise StrictPromptReleaseError(
            "strict_release_prompt_nodes_missing",
            f"{category_key} 的冻结源工作流不含提示词节点",
        )
    if len(model_ids) != 1:
        raise StrictPromptReleaseError(
            "strict_release_model_lineage_ambiguous",
            f"{category_key} 候选节点没有冻结到唯一 260428 模型",
        )
    return tuple(plans), next(iter(model_ids))


def _candidate_rule_content(
    module_type_key: str,
    source_content: Mapping[str, Any],
) -> dict[str, Any]:
    candidate = deepcopy(dict(source_content))
    if module_type_key == "media_adjustment":
        candidate.update(
            {
                "enabled": True,
                "baseline": "real_photo",
                "penalties": {
                    "real_photo": 0,
                    "render_3d": -5,
                    "ai_image": -10,
                    "other": -10,
                },
                "media_penalty_enabled": True,
            }
        )
    elif module_type_key == "score_level_matcher":
        candidate["version"] = "category-level-scale-v1"
        candidate["levels"] = [
            {"level": "L1", "enabled": True, "min_score": 90},
            {"level": "L2", "enabled": True, "min_score": 80},
            {"level": "L3", "enabled": True, "min_score": 60},
            {"level": "L4", "enabled": True, "min_score": 0},
            {"level": "L5", "enabled": False},
        ]
        thresholds = [deepcopy(item) for item in _INSPIRATION_LATEST_THRESHOLDS]
        candidate["legacy_level_thresholds"] = thresholds
        candidate["legacy_score_thresholds"] = deepcopy(thresholds)
    else:
        raise StrictPromptReleaseError(
            "strict_release_rule_transform_unknown",
            f"未注册的灵感图候选规则模块：{module_type_key}",
        )
    return validate_module_content(module_type_key, candidate)


def _rule_plans(
    db: Session,
    *,
    category_key: str,
    source_manifest: Mapping[str, Any],
) -> tuple[_RulePlan, ...]:
    if category_key != "inspiration_image":
        return ()
    plans: list[_RulePlan] = []
    for node in source_manifest.get("nodes", []):
        if not isinstance(node, Mapping) or node.get("kind") != "rule_module":
            continue
        config = node.get("config")
        config = config if isinstance(config, Mapping) else {}
        module_type_key = config.get("module_type_key")
        if module_type_key not in _INSPIRATION_CANDIDATE_RULE_KEYS:
            continue
        node_id = node.get("node_instance_id")
        asset_key = config.get("asset_key")
        source_id = config.get("rule_module_version_id")
        source = (
            db.get(RuleModuleVersion, source_id)
            if isinstance(source_id, int) and not isinstance(source_id, bool)
            else None
        )
        if (
            not isinstance(node_id, str)
            or not isinstance(asset_key, str)
            or source is None
            or source.module_type_key != module_type_key
        ):
            raise StrictPromptReleaseError(
                "strict_release_rule_lineage_missing",
                f"灵感图规则节点 {node_id or module_type_key} 缺少冻结版本",
            )
        try:
            source_content = json.loads(source.content_json)
        except json.JSONDecodeError as exc:
            raise StrictPromptReleaseError(
                "strict_release_rule_source_invalid",
                f"灵感图规则节点 {node_id} 的冻结内容损坏",
            ) from exc
        if not isinstance(source_content, Mapping):
            raise StrictPromptReleaseError(
                "strict_release_rule_source_invalid",
                f"灵感图规则节点 {node_id} 的冻结内容不是对象",
            )
        content = _candidate_rule_content(str(module_type_key), source_content)
        plans.append(
            _RulePlan(
                node_instance_id=node_id,
                asset_key=asset_key,
                source=source,
                content=content,
                content_hash=canonical_component_hash(content),
            )
        )
    found = {plan.asset_key for plan in plans}
    expected = set(_INSPIRATION_CANDIDATE_RULE_KEYS)
    if found != expected:
        raise StrictPromptReleaseError(
            "strict_release_rule_topology_invalid",
            f"灵感图候选缺少确定性规则模块：{sorted(expected - found)}",
        )
    return tuple(plans)


def _plan_hash(
    *,
    category_key: str,
    release_version: str,
    source: WorkflowVersion,
    prompt_plans: tuple[_PromptPlan, ...],
    rule_plans: tuple[_RulePlan, ...],
    model_id: str,
) -> str:
    return _sha256(
        {
            "schema_version": "strict-prompt-release-plan-v1",
            "category_key": category_key,
            "release_version": release_version,
            "source_workflow_id": source.id,
            "source_workflow_hash": source.canonical_hash,
            "model_id": model_id,
            "workflow_transform": _release_transform_plan(category_key),
            "prompts": [
                {
                    "node_instance_id": plan.node_instance_id,
                    "asset_key": plan.asset_key,
                    "source_prompt_id": plan.source.id,
                    "source_content_hash": canonical_component_hash(
                        _prompt_content(
                            stage=plan.source.stage,
                            system_prompt=plan.source.system_prompt,
                            user_prompt=plan.source.user_prompt,
                            rubric_version=plan.source.rubric_version,
                        )
                    ),
                    "candidate_version": plan.candidate_version,
                    "candidate_content_hash": canonical_component_hash(
                        _prompt_content(
                            stage=plan.source.stage,
                            system_prompt=plan.system_prompt,
                            user_prompt=plan.user_prompt,
                            rubric_version=plan.source.rubric_version,
                        )
                    ),
                    "output_contract_hash": plan.output_contract_hash,
                    "vision_policy_hash": plan.vision_policy_hash,
                }
                for plan in prompt_plans
            ],
            "rules": [
                {
                    "node_instance_id": plan.node_instance_id,
                    "asset_key": plan.asset_key,
                    "source_rule_module_id": plan.source.id,
                    "source_content_hash": plan.source.content_hash,
                    "candidate_content_hash": plan.content_hash,
                }
                for plan in rule_plans
            ],
        }
    )


def _apply_inspiration_v2_execution_contract(
    manifest: dict[str, Any],
) -> None:
    resource_policy = manifest.setdefault("resource_policy", {})
    if not isinstance(resource_policy, dict):
        raise StrictPromptReleaseError(
            "strict_release_inspiration_resource_policy_invalid",
            "灵感图候选工作流的资源策略不是对象",
        )
    resource_policy["level_scale"] = {
        "schema_version": "workflow-level-scale-v1",
        "enabled_levels": ["L1", "L2", "L3", "L4", "L5"],
    }
    expected_roles = set(_INSPIRATION_NODE_ROLES)
    assigned: set[str] = set()
    for node in manifest.get("nodes", []):
        if not isinstance(node, dict):
            continue
        config = node.get("config")
        if not isinstance(config, dict):
            continue
        config.pop("short_circuit_rules", None)
        role_key: str | None = None
        if node.get("kind") == "prompt":
            asset_key = config.get("asset_key")
            if asset_key in {"prompt_a", "prompt_b"}:
                role_key = str(asset_key)
        elif node.get("kind") == "rule_module":
            module_type = config.get("module_type_key")
            if module_type in _INSPIRATION_NODE_ROLES:
                role_key = str(module_type)
        if role_key is None:
            continue
        config["execution_role"] = _INSPIRATION_NODE_ROLES[role_key]
        assigned.add(role_key)
        if role_key == "prompt_a":
            config["short_circuit_rules"] = _inspiration_terminal_rules()
    if assigned != expected_roles:
        missing = sorted(expected_roles - assigned)
        unexpected = sorted(assigned - expected_roles)
        raise StrictPromptReleaseError(
            "strict_release_inspiration_topology_invalid",
            (
                "灵感图候选工作流无法冻结最新短路拓扑："
                f"missing={missing}, unexpected={unexpected}"
            ),
        )


def _asset(db: Session, *, category_key: str, asset_key: str) -> WorkflowNodeAsset:
    row = db.scalar(
        select(WorkflowNodeAsset).where(
            WorkflowNodeAsset.category_key == category_key,
            WorkflowNodeAsset.asset_key == asset_key,
            WorkflowNodeAsset.kind == "prompt",
        )
    )
    if row is None or row.status != "active":
        raise StrictPromptReleaseError(
            "strict_release_prompt_asset_missing",
            f"提示词资产 {category_key}/{asset_key} 不存在或已停用",
        )
    return row


def _materialize_prompt(
    db: Session,
    *,
    category_key: str,
    plan: _PromptPlan,
    actor: str,
) -> PromptVersion:
    rows = db.scalars(
        select(PromptVersion).where(
            PromptVersion.category_key == category_key,
            PromptVersion.stage == plan.source.stage,
            PromptVersion.version == plan.candidate_version,
        )
    ).all()
    expected = {
        "pipeline_scope": plan.source.pipeline_scope,
        "system_prompt": plan.system_prompt,
        "user_prompt": plan.user_prompt,
        "rubric_version": plan.source.rubric_version,
        "status": "draft",
        "source": "strict_release",
        "rollback_prompt_id": plan.source.id,
    }
    if len(rows) > 1:
        raise StrictPromptReleaseError(
            "strict_release_prompt_duplicate",
            f"候选提示词版本重复：{plan.candidate_version}",
        )
    if rows:
        row = rows[0]
        actual = {key: getattr(row, key) for key in expected}
        if actual != expected:
            raise StrictPromptReleaseError(
                "strict_release_prompt_version_conflict",
                f"候选提示词版本已存在但内容漂移：{plan.candidate_version}",
            )
    else:
        row = PromptVersion(
            category_key=category_key,
            pipeline_scope=plan.source.pipeline_scope,
            stage=plan.source.stage,
            name=(f"{plan.source.name} 严格高保真候选")[:120],
            version=plan.candidate_version,
            system_prompt=plan.system_prompt,
            user_prompt=plan.user_prompt,
            rubric_version=plan.source.rubric_version,
            status="draft",
            source="strict_release",
            rollback_prompt_id=plan.source.id,
            change_note=(
                "严格 json_schema、target/anchor high、原图语义、逐图无损传输；"
                f"回退提示词 ID {plan.source.id}"
            ),
            created_by=actor,
        )
        db.add(row)
        db.flush()
    bind_prompt_version(
        db,
        asset=_asset(db, category_key=category_key, asset_key=plan.asset_key),
        prompt_version=row,
        created_by=actor,
    )
    return row


def _materialize_rule(
    db: Session,
    *,
    plan: _RulePlan,
    actor: str,
) -> RuleModuleVersion:
    row = db.scalar(
        select(RuleModuleVersion).where(
            RuleModuleVersion.asset_id == plan.source.asset_id,
            RuleModuleVersion.content_hash == plan.content_hash,
        )
    )
    if row is None:
        maximum = db.scalar(
            select(func.max(RuleModuleVersion.version)).where(
                RuleModuleVersion.asset_id == plan.source.asset_id
            )
        )
        row = RuleModuleVersion(
            asset_id=plan.source.asset_id,
            version=int(maximum or 0) + 1,
            module_type_key=plan.source.module_type_key,
            content_json=canonical_json(plan.content),
            content_hash=plan.content_hash,
            source_working_copy_revision=plan.source.source_working_copy_revision,
            created_by=actor,
        )
        db.add(row)
        db.flush()
    if (
        row.module_type_key != plan.source.module_type_key
        or row.content_json != canonical_json(plan.content)
    ):
        raise StrictPromptReleaseError(
            "strict_release_rule_version_conflict",
            f"灵感图候选规则 {plan.asset_key} 已存在但内容漂移",
        )
    asset = db.get(WorkflowNodeAsset, plan.source.asset_id)
    if asset is None:
        raise StrictPromptReleaseError(
            "strict_release_rule_asset_missing",
            f"灵感图规则资产 {plan.asset_key} 不存在",
        )
    bind_rule_module_version(
        db,
        asset=asset,
        rule_module_version=row,
        created_by=actor,
    )
    return row


def _candidate_manifest(
    db: Session,
    *,
    source: WorkflowVersion,
    source_manifest: Mapping[str, Any],
    category_key: str,
    release_version: str,
    prompt_plans: tuple[_PromptPlan, ...],
    prompts: Mapping[str, PromptVersion],
    rule_plans: tuple[_RulePlan, ...],
    rules: Mapping[str, RuleModuleVersion],
    plan_hash: str,
    model_id: str,
) -> dict[str, Any]:
    manifest = deepcopy(dict(source_manifest))
    plans_by_node = {plan.node_instance_id: plan for plan in prompt_plans}
    rule_plans_by_node = {plan.node_instance_id: plan for plan in rule_plans}
    for node in manifest["nodes"]:
        config = node["config"]
        kind = node["kind"]
        if kind == "prompt":
            node_id = node["node_instance_id"]
            plan = plans_by_node[node_id]
            prompt = prompts[node_id]
            for key in (
                "component_snapshot",
                "model_snapshot",
                "vision_capability_snapshot",
                "vision_policy_hash",
                "output_contract_snapshot",
            ):
                config.pop(key, None)
            config["prompt_version_id"] = prompt.id
            config["input_modality"] = "image"
            config["thinking_level"] = "none"
            config["thinking_enabled"] = False
            config["structured_output"] = True
            config["output_contract"] = deepcopy(plan.output_contract)
            config["vision_input_policy"] = deepcopy(STRICT_HIGH_POLICY)
            node["output_schema"] = deepcopy(plan.output_contract["schema"])
        elif kind == "rule_module" and node["node_instance_id"] in rule_plans_by_node:
            node_id = node["node_instance_id"]
            config.pop("component_snapshot", None)
            config["rule_module_version_id"] = rules[node_id].id
        elif kind in {"upstream_connector", "downstream_projection"}:
            connector_key = config.get("connector_key")
            if not isinstance(connector_key, str) or not connector_key.startswith(
                "logical."
            ):
                raise StrictPromptReleaseError(
                    "strict_release_physical_connector_forbidden",
                    f"候选工作流禁止物理连接器：{connector_key}",
                )
            if kind == "downstream_projection":
                config["completion_mode"] = "continue"
    if category_key == "inspiration_image":
        _apply_inspiration_v2_execution_contract(manifest)
    resource_policy = manifest.setdefault("resource_policy", {})
    resource_policy["physical_production_projection_enabled"] = False
    resource_policy["candidate_release"] = {
        "schema_version": "strict-prompt-candidate-release-v1",
        "status": "candidate",
        "activated": False,
        "release_version": release_version,
        "source_workflow_id": source.id,
        "source_workflow_version": source.version,
        "source_workflow_hash": source.canonical_hash,
        "rollback_workflow_id": source.id,
        "plan_hash": plan_hash,
        "model_id": model_id,
        "physical_production_projection_enabled": False,
        "rule_rollbacks": [
            {
                "node_instance_id": plan.node_instance_id,
                "asset_key": plan.asset_key,
                "source_rule_module_id": plan.source.id,
                "rollback_rule_module_id": plan.source.id,
                "candidate_content_hash": plan.content_hash,
            }
            for plan in rule_plans
        ],
    }
    if category_key == "inspiration_image":
        resource_policy["candidate_release"]["business_rule_baseline"] = {
            "schema_version": "inspiration-business-rule-baseline-v2",
            "source": "alidocs",
            "document_node": "vy20BglGWOeMOGOEFvRpq5g5JA7depqY",
            "contract_profile": LATEST_INSPIRATION_CONTRACT_PROFILE,
            # 命中优先级按等级严重度：L5 > L4 > L3（同级并列，等级一致不冲突）
            "terminal_order": [
                "redline:L5",
                "class_three:L4",
                "hard_defect:L4",
                "class_two:L3",
            ],
        }
    frozen = freeze_component_references(db, manifest)
    assert_native_workflow_executable(frozen)
    return frozen


def _workflow_candidate(
    db: Session,
    *,
    source: WorkflowVersion,
    category_key: str,
    release_version: str,
    manifest: Mapping[str, Any],
    plan_hash: str,
    actor: str,
) -> WorkflowVersion:
    existing = db.scalar(
        select(WorkflowVersion).where(
            WorkflowVersion.workflow_definition_id
            == source.workflow_definition_id,
            WorkflowVersion.version == release_version,
        )
    )
    expected_hash = _sha256(manifest)
    if existing is None:
        existing = create_workflow_version(
            db,
            definition=source.definition,
            version=release_version,
            manifest=manifest,
            created_by=actor,
        )
    if existing.canonical_hash != expected_hash or existing.status != "draft":
        raise StrictPromptReleaseError(
            "strict_release_workflow_version_conflict",
            f"候选工作流 {release_version} 已存在但内容或状态漂移",
        )
    existing.validation_report_json = canonical_json(
        {
            "ok": True,
            "errors": [],
            "candidate": True,
            "activated": False,
            "category_key": category_key,
            "source_workflow_id": source.id,
            "rollback_workflow_id": source.id,
            "plan_hash": plan_hash,
            "physical_production_projection_enabled": False,
        }
    )
    db.flush()
    return existing


def _prompt_receipt(
    plan: _PromptPlan,
    candidate: PromptVersion | None,
) -> PromptCandidateReceipt:
    source_content = _prompt_content(
        stage=plan.source.stage,
        system_prompt=plan.source.system_prompt,
        user_prompt=plan.source.user_prompt,
        rubric_version=plan.source.rubric_version,
    )
    candidate_content = _prompt_content(
        stage=plan.source.stage,
        system_prompt=plan.system_prompt,
        user_prompt=plan.user_prompt,
        rubric_version=plan.source.rubric_version,
    )
    source_bytes = len(
        (plan.source.system_prompt + plan.source.user_prompt).encode("utf-8")
    )
    candidate_bytes = len((plan.system_prompt + plan.user_prompt).encode("utf-8"))
    return PromptCandidateReceipt(
        node_instance_id=plan.node_instance_id,
        asset_key=plan.asset_key,
        stage=plan.source.stage,
        version=plan.candidate_version,
        source_prompt_id=plan.source.id,
        candidate_prompt_id=candidate.id if candidate is not None else None,
        rollback_prompt_id=plan.source.id,
        source_content_hash=canonical_component_hash(source_content),
        candidate_content_hash=canonical_component_hash(candidate_content),
        source_bytes=source_bytes,
        candidate_bytes=candidate_bytes,
        byte_delta=candidate_bytes - source_bytes,
        output_contract_hash=plan.output_contract_hash,
        vision_policy_hash=plan.vision_policy_hash,
    )


def _rule_receipt(
    plan: _RulePlan,
    candidate: RuleModuleVersion | None,
) -> RuleCandidateReceipt:
    return RuleCandidateReceipt(
        node_instance_id=plan.node_instance_id,
        asset_key=plan.asset_key,
        module_type_key=plan.source.module_type_key,
        source_rule_module_id=plan.source.id,
        candidate_rule_module_id=candidate.id if candidate is not None else None,
        rollback_rule_module_id=plan.source.id,
        source_content_hash=plan.source.content_hash,
        candidate_content_hash=plan.content_hash,
    )


def materialize_strict_vision_candidates(
    db: Session,
    *,
    actor: str,
    apply: bool = False,
    category_keys: Sequence[str] | None = None,
) -> StrictPromptReleaseReceipt:
    """Preview or materialize all three frozen candidates.

    The function never commits.  Callers own the transaction and may therefore
    combine apply mode with their deployment audit transaction.
    """

    normalized_actor = actor.strip() if isinstance(actor, str) else ""
    if apply and (not normalized_actor or len(normalized_actor) > 80):
        raise StrictPromptReleaseError(
            "strict_release_actor_required",
            "应用候选版本时必须提供 1 至 80 字符的审计 actor",
        )
    # 各类目候选彼此独立：按需只物化指定类目，避免别的类目漂移把本次发布连坐。
    selected_specs = tuple(
        spec for spec in _RELEASE_SPECS
        if category_keys is None or spec[0] in set(category_keys)
    )
    if category_keys is not None and not selected_specs:
        raise StrictPromptReleaseError(
            "strict_release_category_unknown",
            "指定的类目没有可物化的严格候选",
        )
    workflows: list[WorkflowCandidateReceipt] = []
    with db.no_autoflush:
        for category_key, release_version in selected_specs:
            source = _source_workflow(
                db,
                category_key=category_key,
                release_version=release_version,
            )
            source_manifest = persisted_workflow_manifest(source)
            prompt_plans, model_id = _prompt_plans(
                db,
                category_key=category_key,
                release_version=release_version,
                source_manifest=source_manifest,
            )
            rule_plans = _rule_plans(
                db,
                category_key=category_key,
                source_manifest=source_manifest,
            )
            plan_hash = _plan_hash(
                category_key=category_key,
                release_version=release_version,
                source=source,
                prompt_plans=prompt_plans,
                rule_plans=rule_plans,
                model_id=model_id,
            )
            prompt_rows: dict[str, PromptVersion] = {}
            rule_rows: dict[str, RuleModuleVersion] = {}
            candidate_workflow: WorkflowVersion | None = None
            if apply:
                for plan in prompt_plans:
                    prompt_rows[plan.node_instance_id] = _materialize_prompt(
                        db,
                        category_key=category_key,
                        plan=plan,
                        actor=normalized_actor,
                    )
                for plan in rule_plans:
                    rule_rows[plan.node_instance_id] = _materialize_rule(
                        db,
                        plan=plan,
                        actor=normalized_actor,
                    )
                manifest = _candidate_manifest(
                    db,
                    source=source,
                    source_manifest=source_manifest,
                    category_key=category_key,
                    release_version=release_version,
                    prompt_plans=prompt_plans,
                    prompts=prompt_rows,
                    rule_plans=rule_plans,
                    rules=rule_rows,
                    plan_hash=plan_hash,
                    model_id=model_id,
                )
                candidate_workflow = _workflow_candidate(
                    db,
                    source=source,
                    category_key=category_key,
                    release_version=release_version,
                    manifest=manifest,
                    plan_hash=plan_hash,
                    actor=normalized_actor,
                )
            workflows.append(
                WorkflowCandidateReceipt(
                    category_key=category_key,
                    version=release_version,
                    status="candidate",
                    source_workflow_id=source.id,
                    source_workflow_version=source.version,
                    source_canonical_hash=source.canonical_hash,
                    rollback_workflow_id=source.id,
                    candidate_workflow_id=(
                        candidate_workflow.id if candidate_workflow is not None else None
                    ),
                    canonical_hash=(
                        candidate_workflow.canonical_hash
                        if candidate_workflow is not None
                        else None
                    ),
                    plan_hash=plan_hash,
                    model_id=model_id,
                    prompts=tuple(
                        _prompt_receipt(
                            plan,
                            prompt_rows.get(plan.node_instance_id),
                        )
                        for plan in prompt_plans
                    ),
                    rules=tuple(
                        _rule_receipt(
                            plan,
                            rule_rows.get(plan.node_instance_id),
                        )
                        for plan in rule_plans
                    ),
                )
            )
    return StrictPromptReleaseReceipt(
        schema_version="strict-prompt-release-receipt-v1",
        mode="apply" if apply else "preview",
        workflows=tuple(workflows),
    )

===== FILE backend/app/subcategory_resolver.py =====
"""ADR-0033 Phase 3.7 deterministic subcategory resolver (framework-first phase).

The authoritative pipeline is 红线 → **分类器(类目→子类目)** → 子类目 A/B →
维度(共性+特有) → 产出.  This module is the *classifier node*: it maps 调用A's
classification signal (``classification.primary_category`` etc.) onto a v3
contract **subcategory key (track key)** so the aggregator's ``track_key`` can
consume it.

Pure function: no IO, no network, no database, no model calls.  Deterministic
and JSON-serializable for a given input.  It does not touch the worker path,
does not flip L-levels and does not reach the frontend.

The classification map is carried as a standalone optional input (not baked
into the frozen contract) so the frozen contract stays untouched this phase.
"""

from __future__ import annotations

import math
from typing import Any


CLASSIFICATION_MAP_FORMAT_VERSION = "subcategory-classification-map-v1"

_VALID_SCOPE_STATUS = frozenset({"in_scope", "boundary", "out_of_scope"})


class SubcategoryResolverError(ValueError):
    """Raised when the resolver cannot proceed (fail-closed).

    Carries a stable ``code`` for programmatic branching independent of the
    (localized) message text, matching the earlier-phase error convention.
    """

    def __init__(self, code: str, message: str) -> None:
        super().__init__(message)
        self.code = code


def _is_number(value: Any) -> bool:
    return isinstance(value, (int, float)) and not isinstance(value, bool)


def _is_finite_unit(value: Any) -> bool:
    """True iff ``value`` is a finite real number in the closed range [0, 1]."""
    return (
        _is_number(value)
        and math.isfinite(float(value))
        and 0.0 <= float(value) <= 1.0
    )


def _track_keys(track_classification: Any) -> set[str]:
    """Extract the set of defined track keys from a v3 ``track_classification``.

    Only structurally sound entries are collected; this is a lightweight read,
    not a full contract validation (the aggregator/contract validator own that).
    Returns an empty set for a malformed block so callers fail closed.
    """
    if not isinstance(track_classification, dict):
        return set()
    tracks = track_classification.get("tracks")
    if not isinstance(tracks, list):
        return set()
    keys: set[str] = set()
    for track in tracks:
        if isinstance(track, dict) and isinstance(track.get("key"), str):
            keys.add(track["key"])
    return keys


def validate_classification_map(
    classification_map: Any,
    *,
    valid_track_keys: set[str],
) -> None:
    """Fail-closed validation of the standalone classification map.

    Every mapping target and the ``out_of_scope_subcategory`` target must be a
    track key that is defined in the contract (``valid_track_keys``); a wrong
    ``format_version`` or a malformed ``min_confidence`` fails closed too.
    """
    if not isinstance(classification_map, dict):
        raise SubcategoryResolverError(
            "classification_map_not_object", "classification_map 必须是对象"
        )
    if classification_map.get("format_version") != CLASSIFICATION_MAP_FORMAT_VERSION:
        raise SubcategoryResolverError(
            "classification_map_version",
            f"classification_map 版本必须是 {CLASSIFICATION_MAP_FORMAT_VERSION}",
        )

    min_confidence = classification_map.get("min_confidence")
    if not _is_finite_unit(min_confidence):
        raise SubcategoryResolverError(
            "min_confidence_invalid",
            "classification_map.min_confidence 必须是 0 至 1 的数值",
        )

    mapping = classification_map.get("category_to_subcategory")
    if not isinstance(mapping, dict) or not mapping:
        raise SubcategoryResolverError(
            "category_map_invalid",
            "classification_map.category_to_subcategory 必须是非空对象",
        )
    for category, target in mapping.items():
        if not isinstance(category, str) or not category:
            raise SubcategoryResolverError(
                "category_map_key_invalid",
                "category_to_subcategory 的类目 key 必须是非空字符串",
            )
        if target not in valid_track_keys:
            raise SubcategoryResolverError(
                "map_target_unknown",
                f"类目 {category} 映射到未定义的子类目 track：{target}",
            )

    out_of_scope_target = classification_map.get("out_of_scope_subcategory")
    if out_of_scope_target not in valid_track_keys:
        raise SubcategoryResolverError(
            "out_of_scope_target_unknown",
            "out_of_scope_subcategory 必须是合同已定义的 track key",
        )


def resolve_subcategory(
    precheck: Any,
    *,
    classification_map: dict,
    track_classification: dict,
) -> dict:
    """Resolve 调用A's classification signal to a subcategory (track) key.

    Pure function, deterministic.  Resolve order (each step appended to
    ``notes``):

    1. Validate ``classification_map`` (against the contract's track keys) and
       ``track_classification`` (must carry a ``default_track`` inside its keys).
    2. Read ``precheck.classification`` — ``scope_status`` / ``primary_category``
       / ``primary_confidence``.  Missing or illegal → ``default_track``,
       ``resolved_by="invalid_classification"``, ``needs_review=True``.
    3. ``scope_status == "out_of_scope"`` → ``out_of_scope_subcategory``,
       ``resolved_by="out_of_scope"``.
    4. ``primary_confidence < min_confidence`` → ``default_track``,
       ``resolved_by="low_confidence"``, ``needs_review=True``.
    5. ``primary_category`` hits ``category_to_subcategory`` → that subcategory,
       ``resolved_by="mapped"``.
    6. No mapping hit → ``default_track``, ``resolved_by="unmapped_category"``,
       ``needs_review=True``.

    ``boundary`` scope_status is treated as in-scope and continues through
    steps 4/5/6 (annotated in ``notes``).  Every default-fallback branch yields
    a ``track_key`` that exists in the contract (``default_track`` is validated).

    Returns ``{"track_key", "resolved_by", "needs_review", "primary_category",
    "confidence", "notes"}`` — a fixed-shape, JSON-serializable dict.
    """
    notes: list[str] = []

    # Step 1 — validate both inputs (fail-closed).
    valid_track_keys = _track_keys(track_classification)
    if not valid_track_keys:
        raise SubcategoryResolverError(
            "track_classification_invalid",
            "track_classification 必须携带至少一个合法 track key",
        )
    validate_classification_map(
        classification_map, valid_track_keys=valid_track_keys
    )

    default_track = (
        track_classification.get("default_track")
        if isinstance(track_classification, dict)
        else None
    )
    if default_track not in valid_track_keys:
        raise SubcategoryResolverError(
            "default_track_unknown",
            "track_classification.default_track 必须是已定义的 track key",
        )
    notes.append(f"contract default_track={default_track}")

    out_of_scope_target = classification_map["out_of_scope_subcategory"]
    mapping = classification_map["category_to_subcategory"]
    min_confidence = float(classification_map["min_confidence"])

    def _result(
        *,
        track_key: str,
        resolved_by: str,
        needs_review: bool,
        primary_category: Any,
        confidence: Any,
    ) -> dict:
        return {
            "track_key": track_key,
            "resolved_by": resolved_by,
            "needs_review": needs_review,
            "primary_category": primary_category if isinstance(primary_category, str) else None,
            "confidence": float(confidence) if _is_number(confidence) else None,
            "notes": list(notes),
        }

    # Step 2 — read + validate the classification signal.
    classification = precheck.get("classification") if isinstance(precheck, dict) else None
    scope_status = classification.get("scope_status") if isinstance(classification, dict) else None
    primary_category = (
        classification.get("primary_category") if isinstance(classification, dict) else None
    )
    primary_confidence = (
        classification.get("primary_confidence") if isinstance(classification, dict) else None
    )

    if (
        not isinstance(classification, dict)
        or scope_status not in _VALID_SCOPE_STATUS
        or not isinstance(primary_category, str)
        or not primary_category
        or not _is_finite_unit(primary_confidence)
    ):
        notes.append("classification 缺失或非法，落 default_track")
        return _result(
            track_key=default_track,
            resolved_by="invalid_classification",
            needs_review=True,
            primary_category=primary_category,
            confidence=primary_confidence,
        )

    confidence = float(primary_confidence)
    notes.append(
        f"classification scope_status={scope_status} "
        f"primary_category={primary_category} confidence={confidence}"
    )
    if scope_status == "boundary":
        notes.append("scope_status=boundary 视为在范围内继续解析")

    # Step 3 — explicit out-of-scope routing.
    if scope_status == "out_of_scope":
        notes.append(f"scope_status=out_of_scope → {out_of_scope_target}")
        return _result(
            track_key=out_of_scope_target,
            resolved_by="out_of_scope",
            needs_review=False,
            primary_category=primary_category,
            confidence=confidence,
        )

    # Step 4 — low-confidence fallback.
    if confidence < min_confidence:
        notes.append(
            f"confidence {confidence} < min_confidence {min_confidence}，落 default_track"
        )
        return _result(
            track_key=default_track,
            resolved_by="low_confidence",
            needs_review=True,
            primary_category=primary_category,
            confidence=confidence,
        )

    # Step 5 — mapped category hit.
    mapped_track = mapping.get(primary_category)
    if mapped_track is not None:
        notes.append(f"命中映射 {primary_category} → {mapped_track}")
        return _result(
            track_key=mapped_track,
            resolved_by="mapped",
            needs_review=False,
            primary_category=primary_category,
            confidence=confidence,
        )

    # Step 6 — unmapped category fallback.
    notes.append(f"类目 {primary_category} 未命中映射，落 default_track")
    return _result(
        track_key=default_track,
        resolved_by="unmapped_category",
        needs_review=True,
        primary_category=primary_category,
        confidence=confidence,
    )

===== FILE backend/app/test_environment.py =====
from __future__ import annotations

import hashlib
import json
import re
import sys
from argparse import ArgumentParser
from dataclasses import dataclass
from pathlib import Path
from typing import Sequence, TextIO

from sqlalchemy import create_engine, event, inspect, text
from sqlalchemy.engine import Connection, make_url

from .database import configure_mysql_dbapi_connection, engine_options


class TestEnvironmentError(RuntimeError):
    """Raised when a production-derived test copy cannot be neutralized safely."""

    __test__ = False


@dataclass(frozen=True)
class TestEnvironmentNeutralizationReport:
    __test__ = False

    target_schema: str
    affected_rows: dict[str, int]
    evidence_hash: str


_SAFE_TEST_SCHEMA = re.compile(r"labelsystem_test(?:_[a-z0-9_]+)?")


def _configure_mysql_event_connection(
    dbapi_connection: object,
    _connection_record: object,
) -> None:
    configure_mysql_dbapi_connection(dbapi_connection)


def _columns(connection: Connection, table: str) -> set[str]:
    return {str(item["name"]) for item in inspect(connection).get_columns(table)}


def _update_live_rows(
    connection: Connection,
    *,
    table: str,
    live_statuses: tuple[str, ...],
    terminal_status: str,
) -> int:
    columns = _columns(connection, table)
    assignments = ["status = :terminal_status"]
    for field in (
        "lease_owner",
        "lease_token",
        "leased_at",
        "lease_expires_at",
        "heartbeat_at",
        "next_retry_at",
    ):
        if field in columns:
            assignments.append(f"{field} = NULL")
    placeholders = ", ".join(f":status_{index}" for index in range(len(live_statuses)))
    parameters = {"terminal_status": terminal_status}
    parameters.update(
        {f"status_{index}": status for index, status in enumerate(live_statuses)}
    )
    result = connection.execute(
        text(
            f"UPDATE {table} SET {', '.join(assignments)} "
            f"WHERE status IN ({placeholders})"
        ),
        parameters,
    )
    return int(result.rowcount or 0)


def neutralize_test_environment(
    connection: Connection,
    *,
    target_schema: str,
) -> TestEnvironmentNeutralizationReport:
    """Make a copied schema inert before any test service can start."""

    if _SAFE_TEST_SCHEMA.fullmatch(target_schema) is None:
        raise TestEnvironmentError("只允许中和 labelsystem_test 命名空间内的测试 schema")
    tables = set(inspect(connection).get_table_names())
    required = {
        "production_runs",
        "production_step_attempts",
        "runtime_dispatch_items",
        "runtime_outbox_events",
        "session_tokens",
        "model_quota_leases",
        "automation_worker_statuses",
        "incremental_automation_controls",
        "automation_policies",
        "model_quota_pools",
        "incremental_routing_policies",
        "connector_pause_states",
    }
    missing = sorted(required - tables)
    if missing:
        raise TestEnvironmentError(
            "测试 schema 缺少中和所需表：" + ",".join(missing)
        )

    affected = {
        "production_runs": _update_live_rows(
            connection,
            table="production_runs",
            live_statuses=(
                "planned",
                "queued",
                "running",
                "waiting_external",
                "paused",
                "retryable",
                "blocked",
            ),
            terminal_status="canceled",
        ),
        "production_step_attempts": _update_live_rows(
            connection,
            table="production_step_attempts",
            live_statuses=("pending", "leased", "running", "retryable"),
            terminal_status="skipped",
        ),
        "runtime_dispatch_items": _update_live_rows(
            connection,
            table="runtime_dispatch_items",
            live_statuses=("queued", "leased"),
            terminal_status="canceled",
        ),
        "runtime_outbox_events": _update_live_rows(
            connection,
            table="runtime_outbox_events",
            live_statuses=("queued", "leased"),
            terminal_status="canceled",
        ),
    }
    for table in (
        "session_tokens",
        "model_quota_leases",
        "automation_worker_statuses",
    ):
        result = connection.execute(text(f"DELETE FROM {table}"))
        affected[table] = int(result.rowcount or 0)
    for table in (
        "incremental_automation_controls",
        "automation_policies",
        "model_quota_pools",
    ):
        result = connection.execute(text(f"UPDATE {table} SET enabled = 0 WHERE enabled = 1"))
        affected[table] = int(result.rowcount or 0)
    result = connection.execute(
        text(
            "UPDATE incremental_routing_policies SET admission_paused = 1 "
            "WHERE admission_paused = 0"
        )
    )
    affected["incremental_routing_policies"] = int(result.rowcount or 0)
    for table in sorted(tables):
        if "encrypted_api_key" not in _columns(connection, table):
            continue
        result = connection.execute(
            text(
                f"UPDATE {table} SET encrypted_api_key = NULL "
                "WHERE encrypted_api_key IS NOT NULL"
            )
        )
        affected[f"{table}.encrypted_api_key"] = int(result.rowcount or 0)
    result = connection.execute(
        text(
            "UPDATE connector_pause_states SET state = 'paused', "
            "reason_code = 'test_environment_default_closed', "
            "paused_by = 'test_environment_prepare' WHERE state != 'paused'"
        )
    )
    affected["connector_pause_states"] = int(result.rowcount or 0)

    payload = {
        "target_schema": target_schema,
        "affected_rows": dict(sorted(affected.items())),
    }
    evidence_hash = hashlib.sha256(
        json.dumps(payload, sort_keys=True, separators=(",", ":")).encode()
    ).hexdigest()
    return TestEnvironmentNeutralizationReport(
        target_schema=target_schema,
        affected_rows=payload["affected_rows"],
        evidence_hash=evidence_hash,
    )


def _private_mysql_url(path: Path, *, target_schema: str):
    resolved = path.expanduser().absolute()
    if resolved.is_symlink() or not resolved.is_file():
        raise TestEnvironmentError("测试数据库 URL 必须是非符号链接普通文件")
    if resolved.stat().st_mode & 0o077:
        raise TestEnvironmentError("测试数据库 URL 文件权限必须为 0600")
    raw = resolved.read_text(encoding="utf-8").strip()
    if not raw or "\n" in raw or "\r" in raw:
        raise TestEnvironmentError("测试数据库 URL 文件必须只包含一行")
    url = make_url(raw)
    if url.get_backend_name() != "mysql" or url.database != target_schema:
        raise TestEnvironmentError("测试数据库 URL 必须指向目标测试 schema")
    if not url.username or url.password is None:
        raise TestEnvironmentError("测试数据库 URL 必须包含独立测试身份")
    return url


def main(
    argv: Sequence[str] | None = None,
    *,
    output: TextIO = sys.stdout,
) -> int:
    parser = ArgumentParser(description="Neutralize a copied label-system test schema")
    parser.add_argument("--database-url-file", type=Path, required=True)
    parser.add_argument("--target-schema", required=True)
    args = parser.parse_args(argv)
    url = _private_mysql_url(args.database_url_file, target_schema=args.target_schema)
    engine = create_engine(url, **engine_options(url))
    event.listen(engine, "connect", _configure_mysql_event_connection)
    try:
        with engine.begin() as connection:
            report = neutralize_test_environment(
                connection,
                target_schema=args.target_schema,
            )
    finally:
        engine.dispose()
    print(
        json.dumps(
            {
                "status": "ready",
                "target_schema": report.target_schema,
                "affected_rows": report.affected_rows,
                "evidence_hash": report.evidence_hash,
            },
            ensure_ascii=False,
            sort_keys=True,
        ),
        file=output,
    )
    return 0


if __name__ == "__main__":
    raise SystemExit(main())

===== FILE backend/app/three_d_profile.py =====
from __future__ import annotations

import json
import re
from typing import Any, Mapping

from sqlalchemy.orm import Session

from .models import FieldDemandContract


THREE_D_PROFILE = "3d-asset-quality-v1"
THREE_D_CATEGORY_KEY = "model_3d_su"
_SHA256 = re.compile(r"^[0-9a-f]{64}$")


class ThreeDProfileError(ValueError):
