    def __init__(self, code: str, message: str, *, target: str = "contract") -> None:
        super().__init__(message)
        self.code = code
        self.target = target


def _declared_paths(field_contract: FieldDemandContract) -> set[str]:
    try:
        fields = json.loads(field_contract.fields_json)
    except (TypeError, json.JSONDecodeError) as exc:
        raise ThreeDProfileError(
            "three_d_field_contract_invalid",
            "3D 字段需求合同无法解析",
            target="contract.field_demand_contract_id",
        ) from exc
    if not isinstance(fields, list):
        raise ThreeDProfileError(
            "three_d_field_contract_invalid",
            "3D 字段需求合同 fields 必须是数组",
            target="contract.field_demand_contract_id",
        )
    return {
        str(item.get("source_path"))
        for item in fields
        if isinstance(item, Mapping) and isinstance(item.get("source_path"), str)
    }


def validate_three_d_profile_contract(
    db: Session | None,
    contract: Mapping[str, Any],
    *,
    require_database: bool = True,
) -> FieldDemandContract | None:
    if contract.get("schema_version") != "evaluation-category-profile-v3":
        raise ThreeDProfileError(
            "three_d_contract_schema_invalid",
            "3D 机制必须使用 evaluation-category-profile-v3",
        )
    if contract.get("profile_type") != THREE_D_PROFILE:
        raise ThreeDProfileError(
            "three_d_profile_type_invalid",
            f"3D 机制 profile_type 必须是 {THREE_D_PROFILE}",
        )
    category_key = contract.get("category_key")
    if not isinstance(category_key, str) or not category_key.strip():
        raise ThreeDProfileError(
            "three_d_category_missing",
            "3D 机制必须声明 category_key",
        )
    if category_key != THREE_D_CATEGORY_KEY:
        raise ThreeDProfileError(
            "three_d_category_key_invalid",
            f"3D/SU 组合类目必须使用 {THREE_D_CATEGORY_KEY}",
            target="contract.category_key",
        )
    field_contract_id = contract.get("field_demand_contract_id")
    if isinstance(field_contract_id, bool) or not isinstance(field_contract_id, int) or field_contract_id < 1:
        raise ThreeDProfileError(
            "three_d_field_contract_inactive",
            "3D 机制必须绑定正整数的现役字段需求合同 ID",
            target="contract.field_demand_contract_id",
        )
    field_contract: FieldDemandContract | None = None
    if db is None:
        if require_database:
            raise ThreeDProfileError(
                "three_d_field_contract_inactive",
                "3D 机制校验需要读取现役字段需求合同",
                target="contract.field_demand_contract_id",
            )
    else:
        field_contract = db.get(FieldDemandContract, field_contract_id)
        if field_contract is None or field_contract.status != "active":
            raise ThreeDProfileError(
                "three_d_field_contract_inactive",
                "3D 机制绑定的字段需求合同不存在或未启用",
                target="contract.field_demand_contract_id",
            )
        if field_contract.category_key != category_key:
            raise ThreeDProfileError(
                "three_d_field_contract_mismatch",
                "3D 机制与字段需求合同类目不一致",
                target="contract.field_demand_contract_id",
            )
    fingerprint = contract.get("source_schema_fingerprint")
    if not isinstance(fingerprint, str) or not _SHA256.fullmatch(fingerprint.lower()):
        raise ThreeDProfileError(
            "three_d_source_fingerprint_missing",
            "3D 机制必须绑定 64 位来源 Schema 指纹",
            target="contract.source_schema_fingerprint",
        )
    stage_fields = contract.get("stage_fields")
    a_fields = stage_fields.get("a_quality_fields") if isinstance(stage_fields, Mapping) else None
    b_fields = stage_fields.get("b_aesthetic_fields") if isinstance(stage_fields, Mapping) else None
    if (
        not isinstance(a_fields, list)
        or not a_fields
        or not all(isinstance(item, str) and item.startswith(("quality.", "governance.")) for item in a_fields)
        or not isinstance(b_fields, list)
        or not b_fields
        or not all(isinstance(item, str) and item.startswith(("semantic.", "quality.")) for item in b_fields)
    ):
        raise ThreeDProfileError(
            "three_d_stage_fields_missing",
            "3D 机制必须声明非空的 A 阶段质量字段和 B 阶段美感字段",
            target="contract.stage_fields",
        )
    if field_contract is not None:
        contract_paths = _declared_paths(field_contract)
        undeclared = sorted((set(a_fields) | set(b_fields)) - contract_paths)
        if undeclared:
            raise ThreeDProfileError(
                "three_d_stage_field_not_in_contract",
                f"3D 阶段字段未出现在字段需求合同：{', '.join(undeclared)}",
                target="contract.stage_fields",
            )
    return field_contract

===== FILE backend/app/three_d_readiness.py =====
"""Local, machine-checkable readiness contract for the first 3D/SU slice."""

from __future__ import annotations

import hashlib
import json
from typing import Any, Literal

from pydantic import BaseModel, ConfigDict, Field, ValidationError, model_validator

from app.source_identity_probe import build_three_d_su_identity_probe


SOURCE_TABLE = "aliyun_3d66_dw.dim_res_info_union"
PLATFORM_FIELDS = (
    "space",
    "object",
    "style",
    "material",
    "structural_features",
    "architectural_element",
    "soft_decoration",
    "hard_decoration",
    "color",
    "title",
)


class ThreeDReadinessError(ValueError):
    """Raised when a readiness manifest violates the frozen contract."""


class FrozenModel(BaseModel):
    model_config = ConfigDict(frozen=True, extra="forbid")


class IdentityReadiness(FrozenModel):
    table_name: str = SOURCE_TABLE
    key_fields: tuple[str, ...] = ("res_type", "ll_id")
    accepted_res_types: tuple[int, ...] = (1, 6)
    window_required: bool = True
    approval_state: Literal["pending", "signed"] = "pending"
    signed_window: str | None = None
    duplicate_key_count: int | None = None
    multi_res_id_conflict_count: int | None = None
    approval_evidence: str | None = None
    probe_hash: str
    duplicate_policy: Literal["fail_closed"] = "fail_closed"

    @model_validator(mode="after")
    def validate_frozen_identity(self) -> "IdentityReadiness":
        if self.table_name != SOURCE_TABLE:
            raise ValueError("身份源表必须使用已冻结的候选表")
        if self.key_fields != ("res_type", "ll_id"):
            raise ValueError("身份键必须为 res_type + ll_id")
        if self.accepted_res_types != (1, 6):
            raise ValueError("res_type 只允许 1/6")
        if len(self.probe_hash) != 64:
            raise ValueError("probe_hash 必须是 SHA-256")
        if self.approval_state == "signed":
            if not self.signed_window or not self.approval_evidence:
                raise ValueError("身份签认必须包含数据窗口和签认证据")
            if self.duplicate_key_count != 0:
                raise ValueError("身份签认要求重复键数量为 0")
            if self.multi_res_id_conflict_count != 0:
                raise ValueError("身份签认要求同键多 res_id 冲突数量为 0")
        return self


class FieldReadiness(FrozenModel):
    platform_field_keys: tuple[str, ...] = PLATFORM_FIELDS
    category_extensions: tuple[str, ...] = (
        "category.model_3d_su.asset_variant",
        "category.model_3d_su.evaluation_track",
    )
    variants: tuple[str, ...] = ("whole", "single")
    min_precision: float = 0.80
    min_recall: float = 0.70
    owner_signoff_required: bool = True
    owner_signoff_evidence: str | None = None

    @model_validator(mode="after")
    def validate_frozen_fields(self) -> "FieldReadiness":
        if set(self.platform_field_keys) != set(PLATFORM_FIELDS):
            raise ValueError("平台字段必须完整且不得混入类目专有字段")
        if not self.category_extensions or any(
            not key.startswith("category.model_3d_su.")
            for key in self.category_extensions
        ):
            raise ValueError("3D/SU 扩展字段必须使用 category.model_3d_su 命名空间")
        if self.variants != ("whole", "single"):
            raise ValueError("素材形态必须同时覆盖 whole/single")
        if self.min_precision < 0.80:
            raise ValueError("Precision 门槛不得低于 0.80")
        if self.min_recall < 0.70:
            raise ValueError("Recall 门槛不得低于 0.70")
        return self


class GoldenSetReadiness(FrozenModel):
    minimum_count: int = 100
    required_strata: tuple[str, ...] = (
        "3D",
        "SU",
        "whole",
        "single",
        "space_architecture",
        "soft_decoration_furniture",
        "functional_model",
        "L1",
        "L2",
        "L3",
        "L4",
        "L5",
    )
    locked_revision_required: bool = True
    truth_change_policy: Literal["new_revision_only"] = "new_revision_only"
    owner_signoff_evidence: str | None = None

    @model_validator(mode="after")
    def validate_golden_set(self) -> "GoldenSetReadiness":
        if self.minimum_count < 100:
            raise ValueError("黄金集不得少于 100 条")
        required = {"3D", "SU", "whole", "single", "L1", "L2", "L3", "L4", "L5"}
        if not required.issubset(self.required_strata):
            raise ValueError("黄金集分层覆盖不完整")
        return self


class PermissionReadiness(FrozenModel):
    allowed: tuple[str, ...] = ("SELECT", "DESCRIBE")
    denied: tuple[str, ...] = (
        "DOWNLOAD",
        "UPDATE",
        "ALTER",
        "DROP",
        "INSERT",
        "DELETE",
    )
    approval_state: Literal["not_requested", "approved"] = "not_requested"
    approval_evidence: str | None = None

    @model_validator(mode="after")
    def validate_least_privilege(self) -> "PermissionReadiness":
        if self.allowed != ("SELECT", "DESCRIBE"):
            raise ValueError("只允许 SELECT/DESCRIBE 最小权限")
        required_denied = {"DOWNLOAD", "UPDATE", "ALTER", "DROP", "INSERT", "DELETE"}
        if not required_denied.issubset(self.denied):
            raise ValueError("权限拒绝清单不完整")
        return self


class RaciReadiness(FrozenModel):
    required_roles: tuple[str, ...] = (
        "product_owner",
        "data_owner",
        "algorithm_owner",
        "platform_owner",
        "review_owner",
        "consumer_owner",
    )
    assignments: dict[str, str] = Field(default_factory=dict)


class ExternalEffects(FrozenModel):
    connect_real_source: bool = False
    execute_sql: bool = False
    request_permissions: bool = False
    write_database: bool = False
    call_model: bool = False
    publish_labels: bool = False
    deploy: bool = False

    @model_validator(mode="after")
    def reject_external_effects(self) -> "ExternalEffects":
        if any(self.model_dump().values()):
            raise ValueError("前置冻结阶段禁止任何外部效果")
        return self


class ThreeDReadinessManifest(FrozenModel):
    schema_version: Literal["3d-su-readiness-v1"] = "3d-su-readiness-v1"
    category_key: Literal["model_3d_su"] = "model_3d_su"
    status: Literal["pending_external_signoff", "ready_for_real_ingress"] = (
        "pending_external_signoff"
    )
    identity: IdentityReadiness
    fields: FieldReadiness = FieldReadiness()
    golden_set: GoldenSetReadiness = GoldenSetReadiness()
    permissions: PermissionReadiness = PermissionReadiness()
    raci: RaciReadiness = RaciReadiness()
    external_effects: ExternalEffects = ExternalEffects()
    stop_conditions: tuple[str, ...] = (
        "real_source_contact",
        "sql_execution",
        "credential_request",
        "model_call",
        "external_database_write",
        "label_publish",
        "deployment",
    )

    @model_validator(mode="after")
    def require_signed_evidence_for_ready(self) -> "ThreeDReadinessManifest":
        if self.status != "ready_for_real_ingress":
            return self
        evidence_complete = (
            self.identity.approval_state == "signed"
            and bool(self.identity.signed_window)
            and self.identity.duplicate_key_count == 0
            and self.identity.multi_res_id_conflict_count == 0
            and bool(self.identity.approval_evidence)
            and bool(self.fields.owner_signoff_evidence)
            and bool(self.golden_set.owner_signoff_evidence)
            and self.permissions.approval_state == "approved"
            and bool(self.permissions.approval_evidence)
            and set(self.raci.required_roles).issubset(self.raci.assignments)
            and all(self.raci.assignments.values())
        )
        if not evidence_complete:
            raise ValueError("进入真实接入前必须完成身份、字段、黄金集、权限和 RACI 签认证据")
        return self


def build_three_d_readiness_manifest() -> ThreeDReadinessManifest:
    """Build the default pending manifest without contacting external systems."""

    probe = build_three_d_su_identity_probe(SOURCE_TABLE)
    return ThreeDReadinessManifest(
        identity=IdentityReadiness(probe_hash=probe.probe_hash)
    )


def validate_three_d_readiness_manifest(
    manifest: ThreeDReadinessManifest | dict[str, Any],
) -> ThreeDReadinessManifest:
    """Validate a manifest and expose a domain-specific failure type."""

    try:
        return ThreeDReadinessManifest.model_validate(manifest)
    except ValidationError as exc:
        messages = "; ".join(error["msg"] for error in exc.errors())
        raise ThreeDReadinessError(messages) from exc


def readiness_manifest_hash(manifest: ThreeDReadinessManifest) -> str:
    """Return a stable SHA-256 over the validated canonical manifest."""

    validated = validate_three_d_readiness_manifest(manifest)
    canonical = json.dumps(
        validated.model_dump(mode="json"),
        ensure_ascii=False,
        sort_keys=True,
        separators=(",", ":"),
    )
    return hashlib.sha256(canonical.encode("utf-8")).hexdigest()

===== FILE backend/app/three_d_workflow_fixture.py =====
"""Deterministic local proof for the 3D/SU label closed loop.

This module registers no external connector and never calls a model.  It uses
the existing workflow runtime to exercise the same five queues, checkpoint and
retry semantics that production modules use, while keeping all facts local to
the dry-run database.
"""

from __future__ import annotations

import hashlib
import json
from dataclasses import asdict, dataclass
from typing import Any, Mapping

from sqlalchemy import select
from sqlalchemy.orm import Session

from .audit import canonical_json
from .models import (
    ProductionRun,
    ProductionStepAttempt,
    RuntimeDispatchItem,
    ScriptDefinition,
    ScriptVersion,
    WorkflowDefinition,
    WorkflowVersion,
)
from .workflow_fixture_executor import FixtureExecutionError, execute_fixture
from .workflow_registry import create_workflow_definition, create_workflow_version
from .workflow_runtime import (
    ProductionRunRequest,
    claim_next_runtime_step,
    complete_runtime_step,
    create_production_run,
    fail_runtime_step,
)


CATEGORY_KEY = "model_3d_su"
WORKFLOW_KEY = "label.model-3d-su.dry-run"
WORKFLOW_VERSION = "dry-run-v1"
_STEPS: tuple[tuple[str, str, str], ...] = (
    ("source_ingress", "connector", "identity"),
    ("evaluate_and_label", "rule_eval", "transform"),
    ("human_correction_gate", "human_task", "identity"),
    ("label_fact_gate", "release_gate", "identity"),
    ("shadow_projection", "projection", "fail_once"),
    ("projection_reconcile", "reconcile", "identity"),
    ("badcase_feedback", "feedback", "identity"),
)
_HUMAN_GATES = {
    "human_correction_gate": "mechanism",
    "label_fact_gate": "label_fact",
}


class ThreeDDryRunGateError(ValueError):
    pass


@dataclass(frozen=True)
class ThreeDDryRunReceipt:
    run_id: int
    category_key: str
    workflow_version: str
    status: str
    ordered_steps: tuple[str, ...]
    human_correction_gate: str
    label_fact_gate: str
    projection_reconciliation: dict[str, Any]
    feedback_case_key: str
    snapshot_hash: str
    recovery_evidence: dict[str, Any]

    def as_dict(self) -> dict[str, Any]:
        return asdict(self)


def build_three_d_dry_run_manifest() -> dict[str, Any]:
    """Return the non-production 3D/SU DAG built from existing step types."""
    steps = [
        {
            "key": key,
            "type": step_type,
            "script_version": f"fixture.3d-su.{key}@1",
            "input_schema": {"type": "object"},
            "output_schema": {"type": "object"},
        }
        for key, step_type, _fixture in _STEPS
    ]
    return {
        "schema_version": "workflow-v1",
        "category_key": CATEGORY_KEY,
        "steps": steps,
        "edges": [
            {"from": _STEPS[index][0], "to": _STEPS[index + 1][0]}
            for index in range(len(_STEPS) - 1)
        ],
        "queue_class": "validation",
        "input_schema": {"type": "object"},
        "output_schema": {"type": "object"},
        "resource_policy": {"max_parallel": 1},
        "environment": "dry_run",
        "human_gates": {
            "human_correction_gate": {"axis": "mechanism", "required": True},
            "label_fact_gate": {"axis": "label_fact", "required": True},
        },
        "non_goals": [
            "real_upstream",
            "real_model_call",
            "formal_publish",
            "stock_overwrite",
        ],
    }


def _hash(value: Mapping[str, Any]) -> str:
    return hashlib.sha256(canonical_json(value).encode("utf-8")).hexdigest()


def _ensure_script(
    db: Session,
    *,
    step_key: str,
    step_type: str,
    fixture: str,
) -> ScriptVersion:
    script_key = f"fixture.3d-su.{step_key}"
    definition = db.scalar(
        select(ScriptDefinition).where(ScriptDefinition.script_key == script_key)
    )
    if definition is None:
        definition = ScriptDefinition(
            script_key=script_key,
            name=f"3D/SU dry-run · {step_key}",
            description="deterministic local fixture",
            owner="label-platform",
            allowed_categories_json=canonical_json([CATEGORY_KEY]),
            step_types_json=canonical_json([step_type]),
            status="active",
            created_by="fixture",
        )
        db.add(definition)
        db.flush()
    version = db.scalar(
        select(ScriptVersion).where(
            ScriptVersion.script_definition_id == definition.id,
            ScriptVersion.version == "1",
        )
    )
    if version is None:
        artifact = hashlib.sha256(script_key.encode("utf-8")).hexdigest()
        manifest: dict[str, Any] = {"fixture": fixture}
        if fixture == "transform":
            manifest["mapping"] = {
                "content_key": "upstream.source_ingress.content_key",
                "source_snapshot_hash": "upstream.source_ingress.source_snapshot_hash",
            }
        version = ScriptVersion(
            script_definition_id=definition.id,
            version="1",
            display_name=f"{step_key} dry-run v1",
            executor_kind="deterministic_fixture",
            artifact_sha256=artifact,
            manifest_json=canonical_json(manifest),
            input_schema_json='{"type":"object"}',
            output_schema_json='{"type":"object"}',
            required_permissions_json="[]",
            idempotency_template="{run_key}:{step_key}:{input_hash}",
            timeout_seconds=60,
            max_attempts=2,
            retry_policy_json='{"kind":"fixed","delay_seconds":0}',
            concurrency_limit=1,
            estimated_cost_json='{"currency":"CNY","micros":0}',
            status="active",
            validation_report_json='{"ok":true,"errors":[]}',
            blocked_reason="",
            created_by="fixture",
        )
        db.add(version)
        db.flush()
    return version


def _ensure_workflow(db: Session) -> WorkflowVersion:
    for step_key, step_type, fixture in _STEPS:
        _ensure_script(
            db,
            step_key=step_key,
            step_type=step_type,
            fixture=fixture,
        )
    definition = db.scalar(
        select(WorkflowDefinition).where(WorkflowDefinition.workflow_key == WORKFLOW_KEY)
    )
    if definition is None:
        definition = create_workflow_definition(
            db,
            workflow_key=WORKFLOW_KEY,
            name="3D/SU 标签闭环 deterministic dry-run",
            description="仅本地闭环、双人工门、影子投影和 Badcase 回流验收",
            owner="label-platform",
            allowed_categories=[CATEGORY_KEY],
            created_by="fixture",
        )
    version = db.scalar(
        select(WorkflowVersion).where(
            WorkflowVersion.workflow_definition_id == definition.id,
            WorkflowVersion.version == WORKFLOW_VERSION,
        )
    )
    if version is None:
        version = create_workflow_version(
            db,
            definition=definition,
            version=WORKFLOW_VERSION,
            manifest=build_three_d_dry_run_manifest(),
            created_by="fixture",
        )
        version.status = "active"
        version.validation_report_json = '{"ok":true,"errors":[]}'
        db.flush()
    return version


def _pause_at_human_gate(
    db: Session,
    *,
    run: ProductionRun,
    attempt: ProductionStepAttempt,
) -> ProductionRun:
    axis = _HUMAN_GATES[attempt.step_key]
    attempt.status = "blocked"
    attempt.lease_owner = None
    attempt.lease_token = None
    attempt.lease_expires_at = None
    dispatch = db.scalar(
        select(RuntimeDispatchItem).where(
            RuntimeDispatchItem.step_attempt_id == attempt.id
        )
    )
    if dispatch is not None:
        dispatch.status = "completed"
    run.status = "paused"
    run.current_step_key = attempt.step_key
    run.blockers_json = canonical_json(
        [
            {
                "code": "human_gate_approval_required",
                "gate_key": attempt.step_key,
                "axis": axis,
            }
        ]
    )
    run.lease_owner = None
    run.lease_token = None
    run.lease_expires_at = None
    db.flush()
    return run


def _execute_until_boundary(db: Session, *, run_id: int) -> ProductionRun:
    worker_id = "fixture-3d-su-worker"
    while True:
        attempt_id = claim_next_runtime_step(db, worker_id, global_limit=1)
        if attempt_id is None:
            break
        attempt = db.get(ProductionStepAttempt, attempt_id)
        if attempt is None or not attempt.lease_token:
            raise RuntimeError("deterministic workflow claim evidence missing")
        run = db.get(ProductionRun, run_id)
        if run is None:
            raise RuntimeError("3D/SU deterministic dry-run evidence missing")
        if attempt.step_key in _HUMAN_GATES:
            return _pause_at_human_gate(db, run=run, attempt=attempt)
        script = db.get(ScriptVersion, attempt.script_version_id)
        if script is None:
            raise RuntimeError("deterministic workflow script evidence missing")
        try:
            result = execute_fixture(
                str(json.loads(script.manifest_json).get("fixture", attempt.step_type)),
                json.loads(attempt.input_manifest_json),
                json.loads(script.manifest_json),
                attempt_no=attempt.attempt_no,
            )
        except FixtureExecutionError as exc:
            fail_runtime_step(
                db,
                attempt.id,
                attempt.lease_token,
                exc.code,
                str(exc),
                retryable=True,
            )
        else:
            complete_runtime_step(
                db,
                attempt.id,
                attempt.lease_token,
                result.output_manifest,
            )
        db.flush()
    run = db.get(ProductionRun, run_id)
    if run is None or run.status not in {"paused", "succeeded"}:
        raise RuntimeError("3D/SU deterministic dry-run did not reach a boundary")
    return run


def start_three_d_dry_run(
    db: Session,
    *,
    idempotency_key: str,
    actor: str,
) -> ProductionRun:
    """Start or resume the fixture until the next explicit human gate."""
    workflow = _ensure_workflow(db)
    run = create_production_run(
        db,
        ProductionRunRequest(
            workflow_version_id=workflow.id,
            idempotency_key=idempotency_key,
            category_key=CATEGORY_KEY,
            queue_class="validation",
            runtime_context={
                "fixture": "3d-su-dry-run-v1",
            },
            input_manifest={
                "content_key": "fixture:model_3d_su:001",
                "source_snapshot_hash": _hash({"fixture": idempotency_key}),
            },
            owner="label-platform",
            reason="deterministic 3D/SU closed-loop verification",
            environment="dry_run",
        ),
        actor=actor,
    )
    if run.status not in {"paused", "succeeded"}:
        run = _execute_until_boundary(db, run_id=run.id)
    return run


def approve_three_d_dry_run_gate(
    db: Session,
    *,
    run_id: int,
    gate_key: str,
    actor: str,
) -> ProductionRun:
    """Apply one explicit fixture decision and continue to the next boundary."""
    run = db.get(ProductionRun, run_id)
    if run is None:
        raise ThreeDDryRunGateError("3D/SU dry-run 不存在")
    expected_gate = run.current_step_key
    if run.status != "paused" or expected_gate not in _HUMAN_GATES:
        raise ThreeDDryRunGateError("当前运行没有等待人工门")
    if gate_key != expected_gate:
        gate_name = "人工纠偏门" if expected_gate == "human_correction_gate" else "标签事实发布门"
        raise ThreeDDryRunGateError(f"当前等待{gate_name}，不能越序放行")
    normalized_actor = actor.strip()
    if not normalized_actor:
        raise ThreeDDryRunGateError("人工放行人不能为空")
    attempt = db.scalar(
        select(ProductionStepAttempt).where(
            ProductionStepAttempt.run_id == run.id,
            ProductionStepAttempt.step_key == gate_key,
            ProductionStepAttempt.status == "blocked",
        )
    )
    if attempt is None:
        raise ThreeDDryRunGateError("当前人工门缺少可放行的步骤证据")
    lease_token = hashlib.sha256(
        f"{run.run_key}:{gate_key}:{normalized_actor}".encode("utf-8")
    ).hexdigest()
    attempt.status = "leased"
    attempt.lease_owner = normalized_actor
    attempt.lease_token = lease_token
    run.status = "running"
    run.blockers_json = "[]"
    complete_runtime_step(
        db,
        attempt.id,
        lease_token,
        {
            "gate_key": gate_key,
            "axis": _HUMAN_GATES[gate_key],
            "decision": "approved",
            "actor": normalized_actor,
        },
    )
    return _execute_until_boundary(db, run_id=run.id)


def run_three_d_dry_run(
    db: Session,
    *,
    idempotency_key: str,
    actor: str,
) -> ThreeDDryRunReceipt:
    """Execute the full fixture with two explicit, deterministic human decisions."""
    run = start_three_d_dry_run(
        db,
        idempotency_key=idempotency_key,
        actor=actor,
    )
    if run.status == "paused" and run.current_step_key == "human_correction_gate":
        run = approve_three_d_dry_run_gate(
            db,
            run_id=run.id,
            gate_key="human_correction_gate",
            actor=actor,
        )
    if run.status == "paused" and run.current_step_key == "label_fact_gate":
        run = approve_three_d_dry_run_gate(
            db,
            run_id=run.id,
            gate_key="label_fact_gate",
            actor=actor,
        )
    if run.status != "succeeded":
        raise RuntimeError("3D/SU deterministic dry-run did not reach succeeded")
    attempts = db.scalars(
        select(ProductionStepAttempt)
        .where(ProductionStepAttempt.run_id == run.id)
        .order_by(ProductionStepAttempt.sequence, ProductionStepAttempt.attempt_no)
    ).all()
    recovery = next(
        (
            row
            for row in attempts
            if row.step_key == "shadow_projection" and row.attempt_no == 2
        ),
        None,
    )
    if recovery is None or not recovery.checkpoint_hash:
        raise RuntimeError("3D/SU shadow recovery checkpoint is missing")
    return ThreeDDryRunReceipt(
        run_id=run.id,
        category_key=CATEGORY_KEY,
        workflow_version=WORKFLOW_VERSION,
        status=run.status,
        ordered_steps=tuple(step[0] for step in _STEPS),
        human_correction_gate="approved",
        label_fact_gate="approved",
        projection_reconciliation={
            "status": "matched",
            "row_count": 1,
            "payload_hash_verified": True,
        },
        feedback_case_key=f"badcase:{CATEGORY_KEY}:{run.snapshot_hash[:16]}",
        snapshot_hash=run.snapshot_hash,
        recovery_evidence={
            "failed_step": "shadow_projection",
            "retry_attempt": recovery.attempt_no,
            "checkpoint_hash": recovery.checkpoint_hash,
        },
    )

===== FILE backend/app/truth_metric_groups.py =====
"""Separate identity/routing conformance from semantic quality, including old metrics."""
from __future__ import annotations
from typing import Any, Mapping

IDENTITY_FIELDS = frozenset({"ll_id", "res_id", "img_id", "asset_id", "image_id", "case_id", "is_single", "source_content_id"})


def is_identity_field(field: str) -> bool:
    return field.rsplit(".", 1)[-1] in IDENTITY_FIELDS


def _aggregate(fields: Mapping[str, Mapping[str, Any]]) -> dict:
    counts = {key: sum(int(value.get(key, 0)) for value in fields.values())
              for key in ("compared", "correct", "true_positive", "false_positive", "false_negative")}
    def ratio(num, den):
        return num / den if den else None
    return {**counts, "accuracy": ratio(counts["correct"], counts["compared"]),
            "precision": ratio(counts["true_positive"], counts["true_positive"] + counts["false_positive"]),
            "recall": ratio(counts["true_positive"], counts["true_positive"] + counts["false_negative"])}


def separate_truth_metric_groups(metrics: Mapping[str, Any]) -> dict:
    if metrics.get("schema_version") != "baseline-truth-metrics-v1":
        return dict(metrics)
    fields = metrics.get("field_metrics") or {}
    semantic = {key: value for key, value in fields.items() if not is_identity_field(key)}
    identity = {key: value for key, value in fields.items() if is_identity_field(key)}
    aggregate = _aggregate(semantic)
    return {**metrics, "field_aggregate": aggregate,
            "identity_aggregate": _aggregate(identity), "semantic_field_metrics": semantic,
            "identity_field_metrics": identity,
            "semantic_quality_status": "evaluated" if aggregate["compared"] else "truth_missing"}

===== FILE backend/app/tuning_model_registry.py =====
"""挑出真正会被用到的那条调优模型。

注册中心里 ``role='tuning'`` 的行不止运营配的那条：``_ensure_model_registry_entries``
会为每条旧 ``OptimizerConfig`` 镜像一行兼容记录，而且这类行**关不掉**——
``_copy_optimizer_source_to_registry`` 在列接口被调用时就会把 ``created_by`` 为
compatibility/migration 的行强制置回 ``active``（main.py 的同名函数）。所以「去停用它」
不是一个能落地的办法：运营打开一次模型登记页，它就自己活回来了。

2026-09-16 线上正好有一条这样的壳：#3「Fault matrix material optimizer」，
base_url 是 ``http://127.0.0.1:1/v1``——自动化夹具留下的，端口 1 上不会有任何东西监听。

原来两个调用点各写了一遍 ``ORDER BY id ASC LIMIT 1``，谁 id 小谁赢。真正配好的那条
（#2）id 更小，所以现在没出事；一旦它被删，选中的就是这个发不出请求的壳，运营看到的
会是「连不上 127.0.0.1:1」而不是「没配调优模型」。

这里按「这个地址能不能发出真实请求」把这类壳筛掉。容器里的回环是容器自己，真实模型
不可能在那儿——本机部署的模型走的是 ``host.docker.internal``（见 benchmark 那条）。
"""

from __future__ import annotations

from ipaddress import ip_address
from urllib.parse import urlsplit

from sqlalchemy import select
from sqlalchemy.orm import Session

from .models import ModelRegistryEntry


def undispatchable_reason(base_url: str | None) -> str | None:
    """这个接入地址是不是压根发不出真实请求；能发就返回 None。

    只认地址本身就不成立的情况。域名一律放行——连得通与否是真实调用该报的事，
    不该在这里替它下结论。
    """

    host = urlsplit((base_url or "").strip()).hostname
    if not host:
        return "没有填接入地址"
    # localhost 是名字不是地址，ip_address 认不出来，但它和 127.0.0.1 一样指向容器自己。
    if host == "localhost" or host.endswith(".localhost"):
        return "接入地址指向容器自己的回环，不可能是真实模型"
    try:
        address = ip_address(host)
    except ValueError:
        return None
    if address.is_loopback:
        return "接入地址指向容器自己的回环，不可能是真实模型"
    if address.is_unspecified:
        return "接入地址是通配地址，不能用来发起调用"
    return None


def active_tuning_entries(db: Session) -> list[ModelRegistryEntry]:
    return list(db.scalars(
        select(ModelRegistryEntry)
        .where(ModelRegistryEntry.role == "tuning", ModelRegistryEntry.active.is_(True))
        .order_by(ModelRegistryEntry.id.asc())))


def resolve_tuning_entry(db: Session) -> tuple[ModelRegistryEntry | None, list[str]]:
    """按 id 顺序挑第一条能发出真实请求的启用调优模型。

    第二个返回值是被跳过的行和原因。调用方要把它带进报错里：一条都挑不出来时，
    运营需要知道注册中心里明明列着几条调优模型，为什么都不算数。
    """

    skipped: list[str] = []
    for entry in active_tuning_entries(db):
        reason = undispatchable_reason(entry.base_url)
        if reason is None:
            return entry, skipped
        skipped.append(f"#{entry.id} {entry.name}：{reason}")
    return None, skipped


def skipped_suffix(skipped: list[str]) -> str:
    return f"（已跳过：{'；'.join(skipped)}）" if skipped else ""

===== FILE backend/app/upstream_poll_tick.py =====
"""Worker 辅助 tick：按来源合同声明的轮询间隔自动拉取上游。

此前 ``poll_upstream_source`` 唯一的触发点是人工接口
``POST /api/upstream-source-contracts/{id}/poll``。合同声明 ``poll_interval_seconds``
后，这里在间隔到期时用上一次成功读取返回的游标继续拉取；未声明间隔的合同保持人工触发。
每个合同独立提交，一个来源的失败不影响其它来源。
"""
from __future__ import annotations

import json
import logging
from datetime import datetime, timedelta, timezone
from typing import Any
from threading import Lock
from weakref import WeakKeyDictionary

from sqlalchemy import select
from sqlalchemy.orm import Session

from .models import UpstreamReadRun, UpstreamSourceContract
from .readonly_sources import (
    ReadOnlySourceError,
    SourceCursor,
    poll_upstream_source,
    resolve_configured_readonly_source_adapter,
)

logger = logging.getLogger(__name__)

MAX_SOURCES_PER_TICK = 20

# Round-robin position belongs to the database engine, not an individual tick's
# Session. This is scheduling state only: read cursors remain persisted in runs.
# Restarting a worker resets scanning to the first ID; a stable worker visits all
# active contracts within ceil(count / MAX_SOURCES_PER_TICK) ticks. Workers do
# not share this position, so this is fairness, not a distributed claim/lease.
_scan_positions: WeakKeyDictionary = WeakKeyDictionary()
_scan_lock = Lock()


def _next_contract_page(db: Session) -> list[UpstreamSourceContract]:
    bind = db.get_bind()
    query = select(UpstreamSourceContract).where(
        UpstreamSourceContract.status == "active",
        UpstreamSourceContract.poll_interval_seconds > 0,
    ).order_by(UpstreamSourceContract.id).limit(MAX_SOURCES_PER_TICK)
    with _scan_lock:
        after_id = _scan_positions.get(bind, 0)
        contracts = db.scalars(query.where(UpstreamSourceContract.id > after_id)).all()
        if not contracts and after_id:
            contracts = db.scalars(query).all()
        # Advance even when the page is not due, running, or has broken adapters.
        # Selection performs at most two bounded queries and no external I/O.
        _scan_positions[bind] = contracts[-1].id if contracts else 0
    return contracts


def _last_run(db: Session, contract_id: int) -> UpstreamReadRun | None:
    return db.scalar(
        select(UpstreamReadRun)
        .where(UpstreamReadRun.source_contract_id == contract_id)
        .order_by(UpstreamReadRun.id.desc())
        .limit(1)
    )


def _continuation_cursor(db: Session, contract_id: int) -> SourceCursor | None:
    """上一次成功读取的 next_cursor；没有成功记录则从合同默认起点开始。"""
    last_ok = db.scalar(
        select(UpstreamReadRun)
        .where(
            UpstreamReadRun.source_contract_id == contract_id,
            UpstreamReadRun.status == "succeeded",
        )
        .order_by(UpstreamReadRun.id.desc())
        .limit(1)
    )
    if last_ok is None:
        return None
    try:
        values = json.loads(last_ok.next_cursor_json or "{}")
    except ValueError:
        return None
    return SourceCursor(values=values) if isinstance(values, dict) and values else None


def poll_due_upstream_sources(
    db: Session,
    *,
    actor: str,
    now: datetime | None = None,
) -> dict[str, Any]:
    current = now or datetime.now(timezone.utc)
    summary: dict[str, Any] = {"polled": 0, "rows": 0, "skipped": 0, "errors": []}
    contracts = _next_contract_page(db)
    for contract in contracts:
        interval = int(contract.poll_interval_seconds or 0)
        if interval < 1:
            continue
        last = _last_run(db, contract.id)
        if last is not None:
            last_at = last.created_at
            if last_at.tzinfo is None:
                last_at = last_at.replace(tzinfo=timezone.utc)
            if last.status == "running" or last_at + timedelta(seconds=interval) > current:
                summary["skipped"] += 1
                continue
        try:
            adapter = resolve_configured_readonly_source_adapter(contract)
            run = poll_upstream_source(
                db,
                contract=contract,
                adapter=adapter,
                limit=int(contract.page_size or 100),
                actor=actor,
                cursor=_continuation_cursor(db, contract.id),
            )
            db.commit()
        except ReadOnlySourceError as exc:
            db.rollback()
            summary["errors"].append({"contract": contract.contract_key, "code": exc.code})
            logger.warning(
                "上游来源 %s 自动拉取失败：%s %s", contract.contract_key, exc.code, exc
            )
            continue
        except Exception:
            db.rollback()
            summary["errors"].append({"contract": contract.contract_key, "code": "SOURCE_READ_FAILED"})
            continue
        if getattr(run, "status", "succeeded") in {"failed", "blocked"}:
            summary["errors"].append({"contract": contract.contract_key, "code": run.error_code})
        summary["polled"] += 1
        summary["rows"] += int(getattr(run, "row_count", 0) or 0)
    return summary

===== FILE backend/app/upstream_type_short_circuit.py =====
"""上游类型前置短路：规则模块类型 ``upstream_type_short_circuit``。

业务背景（《3D&SU模型分类定级规则》、2026-09-08 需求）：全景图由用户在上游手动选定素材类型，
不需要模型判断；命中即固定等级（全景图 L2），跳过调用 A、调用 B 与后续等级撮合。

机制：节点放在 ``upstream`` 之后、``prompt_a`` 之前。执行器把冻结运行输入里的素材类型字段
（默认 ``material_type``）规范化后写进节点输出；运行时按本模块内容**生成**短路规则
（与分类定级表同源的 short-circuit-rule-v1），在节点完成时求值，命中即跳过后续带
``skip_roles`` 角色的节点，收尾裁决按冻结短路决定给等级。字段缺失或未命中则原样放行（fail-open）。

内容合同（format_version = upstream-type-short-circuit-v1）::

    {
      "format_version": "upstream-type-short-circuit-v1",
      "enabled": true,
      "field_path": "material_type",
      "priority": 900,
      "skip_roles": ["classification", "grading", "level_matching"],
      "rules": [
        {"key": "panorama", "label": "全景图", "match_any": ["全景图", "panorama"], "level": "L2"}
      ]
    }
"""
from __future__ import annotations

from collections.abc import Mapping
from typing import Any

UPSTREAM_TYPE_SHORT_CIRCUIT_MODULE_TYPE = "upstream_type_short_circuit"
UPSTREAM_TYPE_SHORT_CIRCUIT_FORMAT_VERSION = "upstream-type-short-circuit-v1"
RULE_VERSION = "short-circuit-rule-v1"
DEFAULT_FIELD_PATH = "material_type"
DEFAULT_PRIORITY = 900
DEFAULT_SKIP_ROLES = ("classification", "grading", "level_matching")
NORMALIZED_OUTPUT_FIELD = "material_type_normalized"
_LEVELS = ("L1", "L2", "L3", "L4", "L5")
_RULE_KEY_PREFIX = "uts:"


class UpstreamTypeShortCircuitError(ValueError):
    def __init__(self, code: str, message: str) -> None:
        super().__init__(message)
        self.code = code


def _text(value: Any) -> str:
    return value.strip() if isinstance(value, str) else ""


def normalize_type_value(value: Any) -> str:
    """素材类型比对口径：去首尾空白、全角空格、小写；非字符串视为空。"""
    if isinstance(value, bool) or not isinstance(value, (str, int, float)):
        return ""
    text = str(value).replace("　", " ").strip().lower()
    return " ".join(text.split())


def validate_upstream_type_short_circuit_content(content: dict[str, Any]) -> None:
    content.setdefault("format_version", UPSTREAM_TYPE_SHORT_CIRCUIT_FORMAT_VERSION)
    content.setdefault("enabled", True)
    content.setdefault("field_path", DEFAULT_FIELD_PATH)
    content.setdefault("priority", DEFAULT_PRIORITY)
    content.setdefault("skip_roles", list(DEFAULT_SKIP_ROLES))
    content.setdefault("rules", [])
    if content["format_version"] != UPSTREAM_TYPE_SHORT_CIRCUIT_FORMAT_VERSION:
        raise UpstreamTypeShortCircuitError(
            "format_version_unsupported",
            f"上游类型前置短路版本必须是 {UPSTREAM_TYPE_SHORT_CIRCUIT_FORMAT_VERSION}",
        )
    if not isinstance(content["enabled"], bool):
        raise UpstreamTypeShortCircuitError("enabled_invalid", "enabled 必须是布尔值")
    field_path = _text(content["field_path"])
    if not field_path or any(not part for part in field_path.split(".")):
        raise UpstreamTypeShortCircuitError("field_path_invalid", "field_path 必须是非空的点分路径")
    priority = content["priority"]
    if isinstance(priority, bool) or not isinstance(priority, int) or priority < 0:
        raise UpstreamTypeShortCircuitError("priority_invalid", "priority 必须是非负整数")
    skip_roles = content["skip_roles"]
    if (
        not isinstance(skip_roles, list)
        or not skip_roles
        or any(not _text(role) for role in skip_roles)
    ):
        raise UpstreamTypeShortCircuitError("skip_roles_invalid", "skip_roles 必须是非空字符串数组")
    rules = content["rules"]
    if not isinstance(rules, list) or not rules:
        raise UpstreamTypeShortCircuitError("rules_invalid", "rules 必须是非空数组")
    seen_keys: set[str] = set()
    seen_values: dict[str, str] = {}
    normalized_rules: list[dict[str, Any]] = []
    for index, rule in enumerate(rules):
        if not isinstance(rule, Mapping):
            raise UpstreamTypeShortCircuitError("rule_invalid", f"rules[{index}] 必须是对象")
        key = _text(rule.get("key"))
        if not key or any(ch.isspace() for ch in key):
            raise UpstreamTypeShortCircuitError("rule_key_invalid", f"rules[{index}].key 必须是无空白的非空字符串")
        if key in seen_keys:
            raise UpstreamTypeShortCircuitError("rule_key_duplicate", f"规则标识重复：{key}")
        seen_keys.add(key)
        level = _text(rule.get("level"))
        if level not in _LEVELS:
            raise UpstreamTypeShortCircuitError("rule_level_invalid", f"rules[{index}].level 只能是 L1-L5")
        match_any = rule.get("match_any")
        if not isinstance(match_any, list) or not match_any:
            raise UpstreamTypeShortCircuitError("rule_match_any_invalid", f"rules[{index}].match_any 必须是非空数组")
        values: list[str] = []
        for value in match_any:
            normalized = normalize_type_value(value)
            if not normalized:
                raise UpstreamTypeShortCircuitError("rule_match_value_invalid", f"rules[{index}].match_any 含空值")
            if normalized in seen_values and seen_values[normalized] != key:
                raise UpstreamTypeShortCircuitError(
                    "rule_match_value_conflict",
                    f"素材类型「{value}」同时属于规则 {seen_values[normalized]} 与 {key}",
                )
            seen_values[normalized] = key
            if normalized not in values:
                values.append(normalized)
        rule_enabled = rule.get("enabled", True)
        if not isinstance(rule_enabled, bool):
            raise UpstreamTypeShortCircuitError("rule_enabled_invalid", f"rules[{index}].enabled 必须是布尔值")
        normalized_rules.append(
            {
                "key": key,
                "label": _text(rule.get("label")) or key,
                "match_any": values,
                "level": level,
                "enabled": rule_enabled,
                "reason": _text(rule.get("reason")),
            }
        )
    content["field_path"] = field_path
    content["skip_roles"] = [_text(role) for role in skip_roles]
    content["rules"] = normalized_rules


def _get_path(payload: Mapping[str, Any], path: str) -> Any:
    current: Any = payload
    for part in path.split("."):
        if not isinstance(current, Mapping) or part not in current:
            return None
        current = current[part]
    return current


def read_upstream_type(content: Mapping[str, Any], input_manifest: Mapping[str, Any]) -> tuple[Any, str]:
    """返回 (原始值, 规范化值)。"""
    raw = _get_path(input_manifest, _text(content.get("field_path")) or DEFAULT_FIELD_PATH)
    return raw, normalize_type_value(raw)


def rule_key_for(rule: Mapping[str, Any]) -> str:
    return _RULE_KEY_PREFIX + _text(rule.get("key"))


def resolve_upstream_type(
    content: Mapping[str, Any], input_manifest: Mapping[str, Any]
) -> dict[str, Any] | None:
    """按内容对冻结输入求值：命中返回 {rule_key, level, label, matched_value}，否则 None。"""
    if content.get("enabled", True) is not True:
        return None
    _raw, normalized = read_upstream_type(content, input_manifest)
    if not normalized:
        return None
    for rule in content.get("rules", []) or []:
        if not isinstance(rule, Mapping) or rule.get("enabled", True) is not True:
            continue
        values = [normalize_type_value(v) for v in rule.get("match_any", []) or []]
        if normalized in values:
            return {
                "rule_key": rule_key_for(rule),
                "level": _text(rule.get("level")),
                "label": _text(rule.get("label")) or _text(rule.get("key")),
                "matched_value": normalized,
            }
    return None


def short_circuit_rules_from_upstream_type(
    content: Mapping[str, Any],
    *,
    output_field: str = NORMALIZED_OUTPUT_FIELD,
) -> list[dict[str, Any]]:
    """由模块内容生成节点短路规则：匹配本节点输出里的规范化素材类型。"""
    if content.get("enabled", True) is not True:
        return []
    priority = content.get("priority", DEFAULT_PRIORITY)
    skip_roles = list(content.get("skip_roles") or DEFAULT_SKIP_ROLES)
    rules: list[dict[str, Any]] = []
    for rule in content.get("rules", []) or []:
        if not isinstance(rule, Mapping) or rule.get("enabled", True) is not True:
            continue
        values = []
        for value in rule.get("match_any", []) or []:
            normalized = normalize_type_value(value)
            if normalized and normalized not in values:
                values.append(normalized)
        level = _text(rule.get("level"))
        if not values or not level:
            continue
        rules.append(
            {
                "rule_key": rule_key_for(rule),
                "rule_version": RULE_VERSION,
                "priority": int(priority),
                "skip_roles": skip_roles,
                "target_level": level,
                "when": {"path": f"output.{output_field}", "op": "in", "value": values},
                "derived_from": UPSTREAM_TYPE_SHORT_CIRCUIT_MODULE_TYPE,
            }
        )
    return rules


def frozen_node_content(node: Mapping[str, Any]) -> dict[str, Any] | None:
    """从冻结节点的 component_snapshot 取模块内容；不是本模块或损坏则 None。"""
    import json

    config = node.get("config")
    if not isinstance(config, Mapping) or node.get("kind") != "rule_module":
        return None
    if config.get("module_type_key") != UPSTREAM_TYPE_SHORT_CIRCUIT_MODULE_TYPE:
        return None
    snapshot = config.get("component_snapshot")
    raw = snapshot.get("content_json") if isinstance(snapshot, Mapping) else None
    if not isinstance(raw, str):
        return None
    try:
        content = json.loads(raw)
    except ValueError:
        return None
    return content if isinstance(content, dict) else None


def effective_short_circuit_rules(node: Mapping[str, Any]) -> list[dict[str, Any]] | None:
    """节点生效的短路规则：显式配置优先；上游类型前置短路节点按冻结内容生成。"""
    config = node.get("config") if isinstance(node.get("config"), Mapping) else {}
    explicit = config.get("short_circuit_rules")
    if isinstance(explicit, list) and explicit:
        return list(explicit)
    content = frozen_node_content(node)
    if content is None:
        return None
    derived = short_circuit_rules_from_upstream_type(content)
    return derived or None

===== FILE backend/app/user_preferences.py =====
from __future__ import annotations

import json
import re
from typing import Any

from fastapi import APIRouter, Depends, HTTPException
from pydantic import BaseModel, Field
from sqlalchemy import select, update
from sqlalchemy.orm import Session

from .database import get_db
from .models import User, UserPreference, utcnow

FAVORITE_ROUTES = frozenset({
    "/workflow/dashboard", "/workflow/operations", "/workflow/materials/packages",
    "/workflow/materials/assets", "/workflow/incremental", "/workflow/incremental/correction",
    "/workflow/incremental/correction-analysis", "/workflow/materials/jobs", "/workflow/failures",
    "/workflow/optimization/baseline-regression", "/workflow/optimization/paired-regression",
    "/workflow/quality-assets", "/workflow/governance/workflow-manager",
    "/workflow/governance/model-registry", "/workflow/governance/doubao-endpoints",
    "/workflow/governance/model-cost", "/workflow/governance/audit",
})
PAGE_FILTERS = {
    "incremental-corrections": {"category_key", "state", "level", "upstream_class_id", "corrected_by", "published_from", "published_to", "search", "workflow_version_id"},
    "baseline-regression": {"category_key", "outcome", "level", "search"},
    "operations": {"category_key", "status", "source_type", "queue_class", "search"},
    "assets": {"category_key", "status", "search", "uploaded_from", "uploaded_to"},
}
PAGE_COLUMNS = {
    "incremental-corrections": {"image", "material", "category", "level", "state", "workflow", "corrected_by", "published_at"},
    "baseline-regression": {"image", "material", "notes", "expected_level", "predicted_level", "outcome", "score"},
    "operations": {"image", "run", "category", "workflow", "step", "checkpoint", "owner", "blocker", "status", "actions", "progress", "delivery", "created_at"},
    "assets": {"image", "name", "category", "size", "created_at"},
}


def validate_preferences(scope: str, values: dict[str, Any]) -> dict[str, Any]:
    if len(json.dumps(values, ensure_ascii=False, allow_nan=False).encode()) > 8192:
        raise ValueError("个人设置过大")
    if scope == "global":
        if set(values) - {"favorites"}:
            raise ValueError("不支持的个人设置")
        favorites = values.get("favorites", [])
        if not isinstance(favorites, list) or len(favorites) > 12 or any(type(v) is not str or v not in FAVORITE_ROUTES for v in favorites):
            raise ValueError("常用入口无效")
        return {"favorites": list(dict.fromkeys(favorites))}
    if scope not in PAGE_FILTERS or set(values) - {"filters", "table"}:
        raise ValueError("不支持的页面设置")
    filters = values.get("filters", {})
    if not isinstance(filters, dict) or set(filters) - PAGE_FILTERS[scope]:
        raise ValueError("不支持的筛选条件")
    if any(type(v) not in {str, int, bool, type(None)} or (isinstance(v, str) and len(v) > 200) for v in filters.values()):
        raise ValueError("筛选条件无效")
    table = values.get("table", {})
    if not isinstance(table, dict) or set(table) - {"density", "page_size", "columns", "sort"}:
        raise ValueError("不支持的表格设置")
    if table.get("density", "comfortable") not in {"comfortable", "compact"}:
        raise ValueError("表格密度无效")
    if type(table.get("page_size", 50)) is not int or table.get("page_size", 50) not in {10, 20, 25, 50, 100, 200}:
        raise ValueError("分页大小无效")
    columns = table.get("columns", [])
    if not isinstance(columns, list) or len(columns) > (100 if scope == "baseline-regression" else len(PAGE_COLUMNS[scope])):
        raise ValueError("表格列无效")
    seen: set[str] = set()
    for column in columns:
        if not isinstance(column, dict) or set(column) - {"key", "visible", "width"}:
            raise ValueError("表格列无效")
        key = column.get("key")
        known = type(key) is str and (key in PAGE_COLUMNS[scope] or (scope == "baseline-regression" and re.fullmatch(r"field:[\w.:/\[\]-]{1,180}", key)))
        if not known or key in seen:
            raise ValueError("表格列无效")
        if type(column.get("visible", True)) is not bool or type(column.get("width", 160)) is not int or not 80 <= column.get("width", 160) <= 600:
            raise ValueError("表格列设置无效")
        seen.add(column["key"])
    sort = table.get("sort")
    if sort is not None and (not isinstance(sort, dict) or set(sort) != {"key", "direction"} or sort["key"] not in PAGE_COLUMNS[scope] or sort["direction"] not in {"asc", "desc"}):
        raise ValueError("表格排序无效")
    return {"filters": filters, "table": table}


class PreferenceUpdate(BaseModel):
    scope: str = Field(min_length=1, max_length=100)
    expected_revision: int = Field(ge=0)
    values: dict[str, Any]


def build_preferences_router(require_user):
    router = APIRouter(prefix="/api/me/preferences", tags=["personal-settings"])

    @router.get("")
    def get_preferences(user: User = Depends(require_user), db: Session = Depends(get_db)):
        items = []
        for row in db.scalars(select(UserPreference).where(UserPreference.user_id == user.id)):
            try:
                values = validate_preferences(row.scope, json.loads(row.values_json))
            except (ValueError, TypeError, KeyError):
                values = {}
            items.append({"scope": row.scope, "revision": row.revision, "values": values})
        return {"items": items}

    @router.put("")
    def save_preferences(payload: PreferenceUpdate, user: User = Depends(require_user), db: Session = Depends(get_db)):
        try:
            values = validate_preferences(payload.scope, payload.values)
        except (ValueError, TypeError):
            raise HTTPException(422, "个人设置包含不支持的值") from None
        row = db.get(UserPreference, (user.id, payload.scope))
        if (row.revision if row else 0) != payload.expected_revision:
            raise HTTPException(409, "这页设置已在其他设备更新，请刷新后重试")
        encoded = json.dumps(values, ensure_ascii=False, allow_nan=False)
        revision = payload.expected_revision + 1
        if row is None:
            db.add(UserPreference(user_id=user.id, scope=payload.scope, revision=revision, values_json=encoded))
        else:
            changed = db.execute(update(UserPreference).where(UserPreference.user_id == user.id,
                UserPreference.scope == payload.scope, UserPreference.revision == payload.expected_revision)
                .values(revision=revision, values_json=encoded, updated_at=utcnow())).rowcount
            if changed != 1:
                raise HTTPException(409, "这页设置已在其他设备更新，请刷新后重试")
        db.commit()
        return {"scope": payload.scope, "revision": revision, "values": values}

    return router

===== FILE backend/app/v3_review_dimensions.py =====
"""Frozen V3 grade contracts for human-review display and recalculation.

The review UI historically read only the legacy strategy dimension snapshot.
V3 authoritative evaluations instead freeze their executable contract on the
evaluation job.  This module projects that frozen contract into the small UI
schema used by human correction and replays dimension corrections through the
same pure ``evaluate_one`` scoring chain used by the worker.
"""

from __future__ import annotations

import json
from typing import Any

from .dimension_composition import validate_subcategory_dimensions
from .dimension_grade_bridge import resolve_dimension_weight_scale
from .dimension_schema_registry import canonical_hash
from .inspiration_category_seed import evaluate_one


STRICT_GRADE_OUTPUT_CONTRACT = {
    "format_version": "dimension-grade-output-v1",
    "require_exact_keys": True,
    "evidence_required": True,
}


class V3ReviewDimensionError(ValueError):
    """The frozen V3 review contract cannot be interpreted safely."""


def _json_object(raw: Any, *, field: str) -> dict[str, Any]:
    if isinstance(raw, dict):
        return raw
    if not isinstance(raw, str):
        raise V3ReviewDimensionError(f"{field} 必须是 JSON 对象")
    parsed = json.loads(raw or "{}")
    if not isinstance(parsed, dict):
        raise V3ReviewDimensionError(f"{field} 必须是 JSON 对象")
    return parsed


def _frozen_context(evaluation: Any) -> dict[str, Any] | None:
    raw_scoring = getattr(evaluation, "scoring_json", None)
    if raw_scoring is None:
        return None
    scoring = _json_object(
        raw_scoring, field="scoring_json"
    )
    if (
        scoring.get("scoring_mode") != "v3_authoritative"
        or scoring.get("dimension_scoring_mode") != "grade_fallback"
    ):
        return None

    job = getattr(evaluation, "job", None)
    if job is None:
        raise V3ReviewDimensionError("V3 评测缺少冻结 Job")
    profile = _json_object(
        getattr(job, "category_profile_snapshot_json", None),
        field="category_profile_snapshot_json",
    )
    bundle = profile.get("v3_authoritative_bundle")
    if not isinstance(bundle, dict):
        raise V3ReviewDimensionError("V3 评测缺少冻结权威合同")

    contract = bundle.get("contract")
    classification_map = bundle.get("classification_map")
    subcategory_dimensions = bundle.get("subcategory_dimensions")
    if not isinstance(contract, dict):
        raise V3ReviewDimensionError("冻结 V3 contract 无效")
    if not isinstance(classification_map, dict):
        raise V3ReviewDimensionError("冻结 V3 classification_map 无效")
    if not isinstance(subcategory_dimensions, dict):
        raise V3ReviewDimensionError("冻结 V3 subcategory_dimensions 无效")

    track_key = scoring.get("track_key")
    if not isinstance(track_key, str) or not track_key:
        raise V3ReviewDimensionError("V3 评分缺少 track_key")
    track_config = subcategory_dimensions.get(track_key)
    if not isinstance(track_config, dict):
        raise V3ReviewDimensionError(f"冻结 V3 合同缺少赛道 {track_key}")

    grade_contract = track_config.get("grade_output_contract")
    if grade_contract is None:
        return None
    if grade_contract != STRICT_GRADE_OUTPUT_CONTRACT:
        raise V3ReviewDimensionError("冻结 V3 grade_output_contract 无效")
    validate_subcategory_dimensions(track_config)

    track_classification = contract.get("track_classification")
    if not isinstance(track_classification, dict):
        raise V3ReviewDimensionError("冻结 V3 track_classification 无效")
    tracks = track_classification.get("tracks")
    track = next(
        (
            item
            for item in tracks or []
            if isinstance(item, dict) and item.get("key") == track_key
        ),
        None,
    )
    if not isinstance(track, dict):
        raise V3ReviewDimensionError(f"V3 contract 未定义赛道 {track_key}")
    return {
        "scoring": scoring,
        "bundle": bundle,
        "contract": contract,
        "classification_map": classification_map,
        "subcategory_dimensions": subcategory_dimensions,
        "track_key": track_key,
        "track_config": track_config,
        "track": track,
    }


def _groups(track_config: dict[str, Any]) -> list[tuple[dict[str, Any], list[dict[str, Any]]]]:
    groups: list[tuple[dict[str, Any], list[dict[str, Any]]]] = []
    for group_name in ("common_group", "specific_group"):
        group = track_config.get(group_name)
        if not isinstance(group, dict):
            continue
        schema = group.get("schema_definition")
        dimensions = schema.get("dimensions") if isinstance(schema, dict) else None
        if isinstance(dimensions, list) and dimensions:
            groups.append((group, dimensions))
    if not groups:
        raise V3ReviewDimensionError("冻结 V3 赛道没有可纠偏维度")
    return groups


def _grade_points(
    dimension: dict[str, Any], schema: dict[str, Any]
) -> dict[str, float]:
    candidates = [dimension.get("grade_points")]
    aggregation = schema.get("aggregation")
    if isinstance(aggregation, dict):
        candidates.append(aggregation.get("grade_points"))
    candidates.append(schema.get("grade_points"))
    raw = next((item for item in candidates if item is not None), None)
    if not isinstance(raw, dict):
        raise V3ReviewDimensionError(
            f"维度 {dimension.get('key')} 缺少 grade_points"
        )
    points: dict[str, float] = {}
    for grade in range(1, 6):
        value = raw.get(str(grade))
        if isinstance(value, bool) or not isinstance(value, (int, float)):
            raise V3ReviewDimensionError(
                f"维度 {dimension.get('key')} 的 grade_points 无效"
            )
        points[str(grade)] = float(value)
    if points["5"] <= points["1"]:
        raise V3ReviewDimensionError(
            f"维度 {dimension.get('key')} 的 grade_points 方向无效"
        )
    return points


def v3_review_dimension_schema_payload(evaluation: Any) -> dict[str, Any] | None:
    """Return a frontend review schema for strict static-grade V3 results."""
    context = _frozen_context(evaluation)
    if context is None:
        return None
    contract = context["contract"]
    track = context["track"]
    track_config = context["track_config"]
    groups = _groups(track_config)
    dimension_max = float(track["dimension_max"])
    group_weight_total = sum(float(group["group_weight"]) for group, _ in groups)
    if group_weight_total <= 0:
        raise V3ReviewDimensionError("V3 非空维度组权重之和必须大于 0")

    projected_dimensions: list[dict[str, Any]] = []
    schema_keys: list[str] = []
    seen: set[str] = set()
    for group, dimensions in groups:
        schema = group["schema_definition"]
        schema_key = schema.get("schema_key")
        if isinstance(schema_key, str) and schema_key:
            schema_keys.append(schema_key)
        effective_max = (
            float(group["group_weight"]) / group_weight_total
        ) * dimension_max
        weight_scale, _ = resolve_dimension_weight_scale(dimensions, effective_max)
        for dimension in dimensions:
            key = dimension.get("key")
            label = dimension.get("label")
            if not isinstance(key, str) or not key or key in seen:
                raise V3ReviewDimensionError("V3 维度 key 缺失或重复")
            if not isinstance(label, str) or not label:
                raise V3ReviewDimensionError(f"维度 {key} 缺少展示名称")
            weight = dimension.get("weight")
            if isinstance(weight, bool) or not isinstance(weight, (int, float)):
                raise V3ReviewDimensionError(f"维度 {key} 的 weight 无效")
            seen.add(key)
            projected_dimensions.append(
                {
                    "key": key,
                    "label": label,
                    "weight": (
                        float(weight) * weight_scale / dimension_max
                        if dimension_max > 0
                        else 0.0
                    ),
                    "grade_points": _grade_points(dimension, schema),
                }
            )

    base_schema_key = schema_keys[0] if schema_keys else str(
        contract.get("category_key") or "v3_review"
    )
    schema_key = f"{base_schema_key}:{context['track_key']}"
    level_scale = contract.get("level_scale")
    levels = level_scale.get("levels") if isinstance(level_scale, dict) else None
    if not isinstance(levels, list) or not levels:
        raise V3ReviewDimensionError("冻结 V3 合同缺少 level_scale")
    definition = {
        "format_version": "v3-review-dimension-schema-v1",
        "schema_key": schema_key,
        "version": str(contract.get("spec_version") or "v3-frozen"),
        "dimensions": projected_dimensions,
        "aggregation": {
            "preview_mode": "v3_grade_bridge",
            "score_round_digits": 0,
            "base_score": track["base_score"],
            "dimension_max": track["dimension_max"],
            "track_cap": track["track_cap"],
            "level_scale": levels,
        },
        "output_contract": {
            "dimension_output_keys": [item["key"] for item in projected_dimensions],
            "unknown_key_policy": "reject",
        },
    }
    return {
        "status": "resolved",
        "schema_id": None,
        "schema_key": schema_key,
        "version": definition["version"],
        "canonical_hash": canonical_hash(definition),
        "legacy_derived": False,
        "dimension_keys": list(definition["output_contract"]["dimension_output_keys"]),
        "dimension_selection": None,
        "dimension_mode": "all",
        "definition": definition,
        "error": None,
    }


def calculate_v3_review_corrected_score(
    evaluation: Any,
    corrections: list[dict[str, Any]],
) -> dict[str, Any] | None:
    """Replay corrected V3 grades through the frozen pure scoring engine."""
    context = _frozen_context(evaluation)
    if context is None:
        return None
    groups = _groups(context["track_config"])
    expected_keys = [
        str(dimension["key"])
        for _, dimensions in groups
        for dimension in dimensions
    ]
    aesthetic = _json_object(
        getattr(evaluation, "aesthetic_json", None), field="aesthetic_json"
    )
    raw_dimensions = aesthetic.get("dimensions")
    if not isinstance(raw_dimensions, dict):
        raise V3ReviewDimensionError("V3 aesthetic.dimensions 必须是对象")
    if set(raw_dimensions) != set(expected_keys):
        raise V3ReviewDimensionError("V3 结果维度与冻结合同不一致")

    grades: dict[str, int] = {}
    for key in expected_keys:
        item = raw_dimensions.get(key)
        grade = item.get("grade") if isinstance(item, dict) else None
        if isinstance(grade, bool) or not isinstance(grade, int) or not 1 <= grade <= 5:
            raise V3ReviewDimensionError(f"维度 {key} 的原始 grade 无效")
        grades[key] = grade

    for correction in corrections:
        key = correction.get("field_key")
        if key not in grades:
            raise V3ReviewDimensionError(f"人工纠偏包含未知维度：{key}")
        grade = correction.get("human_value")
        if isinstance(grade, bool) or not isinstance(grade, int) or not 1 <= grade <= 5:
            raise V3ReviewDimensionError(f"维度 {key} 的人工 grade 必须是 1 至 5")
        grades[key] = grade

    common_group = context["track_config"].get("common_group")
    specific_group = context["track_config"].get("specific_group")
    common_schema = (
        common_group.get("schema_definition", {})
        if isinstance(common_group, dict)
        else {}
    )
    specific_schema = (
        specific_group.get("schema_definition", {})
        if isinstance(specific_group, dict)
        else {}
    )
    common_keys = [
        item["key"] for item in common_schema.get("dimensions", [])
    ] if isinstance(common_schema, dict) else []
    specific_keys = [
        item["key"] for item in specific_schema.get("dimensions", [])
    ] if isinstance(specific_schema, dict) else []
    precheck = _json_object(
        getattr(evaluation, "precheck_json", None), field="precheck_json"
    )
    try:
        outcome = evaluate_one(
            contract=context["contract"],
            classification_map=context["classification_map"],
            subcategory_dimensions=context["subcategory_dimensions"],
            precheck=precheck,
            common_grades_by_track={
                context["track_key"]: {key: grades[key] for key in common_keys}
            } if common_keys else {},
            specific_grades_by_track={
                context["track_key"]: {key: grades[key] for key in specific_keys}
            } if specific_keys else {},
        )
    except (KeyError, TypeError, ValueError) as exc:
        raise V3ReviewDimensionError(f"V3 人工纠偏重算失败：{exc}") from exc
    result = outcome.get("result")
    if not isinstance(result, dict):
        raise V3ReviewDimensionError("V3 人工纠偏重算未返回结果")
    if not result.get("hard_reject") and result.get("track_key") != context["track_key"]:
        raise V3ReviewDimensionError("V3 人工纠偏重算赛道与原评测不一致")
    score = result.get("score")
    level = result.get("level")
    if (
        isinstance(score, bool)
        or not isinstance(score, (int, float))
        or not isinstance(level, str)
    ):
        raise V3ReviewDimensionError("V3 人工纠偏无法计算正式分数与等级")
    return {"score": score, "level": level}

===== FILE backend/app/vision_input_policy.py =====
"""Provider-aware, role-based vision input policy contracts.

The types in this module are intentionally pure and immutable.  They are used at
configuration, publication, execution and audit boundaries, so no caller-owned
mapping is retained and no provider fallback is inferred at runtime.
"""

from __future__ import annotations

import hashlib
import math
import re
from collections.abc import Mapping, Sequence
from dataclasses import dataclass, field
from types import MappingProxyType
from typing import Any, Literal, TypeAlias

from .audit import canonical_json


MediaRole: TypeAlias = Literal["target", "anchor"]
DetailValue: TypeAlias = Literal["low", "high", "xhigh"]

_POLICY_VERSION = "vision-input-policy-v1"
_CAPABILITY_VERSION = "vision-capability-profile-v1"
_LEGACY_VERSION = "legacy-default"
_MEDIA_ROLES: tuple[MediaRole, ...] = ("target", "anchor")
_DETAIL_VALUES = frozenset({"low", "high", "xhigh"})
_PROFILE_KEY = re.compile(r"^[A-Za-z0-9][A-Za-z0-9._-]{0,127}$")
_ISO_DATE = re.compile(r"^\d{4}-\d{2}-\d{2}$")

_CAPABILITY_KEYS = frozenset(
    {
        "version",
        "profile_key",
        "detail_modes",
        "pixel_limit_supported",
        "min_source_pixels",
        "max_source_pixels",
        "min_aspect_ratio",
        "max_aspect_ratio",
        "min_dimension_pixels",
        "allowed_mime_types",
        "max_frames",
        "max_single_image_bytes",
        "single_image_safety_margin_bytes",
        "max_request_body_bytes",
        "request_body_safety_margin_bytes",
        "token_formula",
        "source_document_url",
        "observed_revision_date",
    }
)

SEED_20_PROFILE: Mapping[str, Any] = MappingProxyType(
    {
        "version": _CAPABILITY_VERSION,
        "profile_key": "volcengine-seed-2.0-v20260901",
        "detail_modes": ["low", "high", "xhigh"],
        "pixel_limit_supported": True,
        "min_source_pixels": 196,
        "max_source_pixels": 36_000_000,
        "min_aspect_ratio": 1 / 150,
        "max_aspect_ratio": 150,
        "min_dimension_pixels": 15,
        "allowed_mime_types": ["image/jpeg", "image/png", "image/webp"],
        "max_frames": 1,
        "max_single_image_bytes": 10_000_000,
        "single_image_safety_margin_bytes": 262_144,
        "max_request_body_bytes": 64_000_000,
        "request_body_safety_margin_bytes": 1_048_576,
        "token_formula": "seed-2.0-v1",
        "source_document_url": (
            "https://docs.volcengine.com/docs/82379/1362931?lang=zh"
        ),
        "observed_revision_date": "2026-09-01",
    }
)

# Deployment-specific profile: model label is qwen3.8-27b, actual config is
# Qwen3_5 + Qwen3VLProcessor. All seven .205 instances use patch16/merge2 and
# mm_processor_kwargs min_pixels=38416,max_pixels=1048576. Source originals
# remain untouched; the source-size ceiling below is our bounded admission
# guard, not a claim about an upstream API's maximum. See verification receipt.
QWEN_205_PROFILE: Mapping[str, Any] = MappingProxyType({
    **dict(SEED_20_PROFILE),
    "profile_key": "local-qwen205-p16-m2-1mp-v20260921",
    "detail_modes": ["high"],
    "pixel_limit_supported": False,
    "max_source_pixels": 60_000_000,
    "max_single_image_bytes": 32_000_000,
    "max_request_body_bytes": 256 * 1024 * 1024,
    "token_formula": "qwen205-p16-m2-min38416-max1048576-v1",
    "source_document_url": "https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/blob/main/vllm/model_executor/models/qwen3_vl.py",
    "observed_revision_date": "2026-09-21",
})


def _qwen_205_resized_pixels(width: int, height: int) -> int:
    """Frozen installed smart_resize, factor32; no local image transformation."""
    factor, minimum, maximum = 32, 38416, 1048576
    h, w = round(height / factor) * factor, round(width / factor) * factor
    if h * w > maximum:
        beta = math.sqrt(height * width / maximum)
        h = max(factor, math.floor(height / beta / factor) * factor)
        w = max(factor, math.floor(width / beta / factor) * factor)
    elif h * w < minimum:
        beta = math.sqrt(minimum / (height * width))
        h = math.ceil(height * beta / factor) * factor
        w = math.ceil(width * beta / factor) * factor
    return h * w


class VisionInputPolicyError(ValueError):
    """Stable, machine-readable policy validation failure."""

    def __init__(
        self,
        code: str,
        message: str,
        *,
        evidence: Mapping[str, Any] | None = None,
    ) -> None:
        super().__init__(message)
        self.code = code
        self.evidence = MappingProxyType(dict(evidence or {}))


def _error(
    code: str,
    message: str,
    *,
    evidence: Mapping[str, Any] | None = None,
) -> VisionInputPolicyError:
    return VisionInputPolicyError(code, message, evidence=evidence)


def _is_int(value: Any) -> bool:
    return isinstance(value, int) and not isinstance(value, bool)


def _is_number(value: Any) -> bool:
    return (
        isinstance(value, (int, float))
        and not isinstance(value, bool)
        and math.isfinite(value)
    )


def _require_exact_keys(
    value: Mapping[str, Any],
    expected: frozenset[str],
    *,
    code: str,
    path: str,
) -> None:
    actual = frozenset(value)
    if actual == expected:
        return
    missing = sorted(expected - actual)
    extra = sorted(actual - expected)
    details: list[str] = []
    if missing:
        details.append(f"缺少字段 {', '.join(missing)}")
    if extra:
        details.append(f"存在未知字段 {', '.join(extra)}")
    raise _error(code, f"{path} 字段不合法：{'；'.join(details)}")


@dataclass(frozen=True)
class DetailFidelity:
    value: DetailValue = "high"
    mode: Literal["detail"] = field(default="detail", init=False)

    def __post_init__(self) -> None:
        if self.value not in _DETAIL_VALUES:
            raise _error(
                "vision_fidelity_invalid",
                "detail 只支持 low、high 或 xhigh",
            )

    def as_dict(self) -> dict[str, Any]:
        return {"mode": self.mode, "value": self.value}


@dataclass(frozen=True)
class PixelLimitFidelity:
    min_pixels: int
    max_pixels: int
    mode: Literal["pixel_limit"] = field(default="pixel_limit", init=False)

    def __post_init__(self) -> None:
        if (
            not _is_int(self.min_pixels)
            or not _is_int(self.max_pixels)
            or self.min_pixels < 196
            or self.max_pixels > 36_000_000
            or self.min_pixels > self.max_pixels
        ):
            raise _error(
                "vision_pixel_limit_invalid",
                "像素上限必须满足 196 <= min_pixels <= max_pixels <= 36000000",
            )

    def as_dict(self) -> dict[str, Any]:
        return {
            "mode": self.mode,
            "min_pixels": self.min_pixels,
            "max_pixels": self.max_pixels,
        }


VisionFidelity: TypeAlias = DetailFidelity | PixelLimitFidelity


@dataclass(frozen=True)
class OriginalPreprocess:
    mode: Literal["original"] = field(default="original", init=False)

    def as_dict(self) -> dict[str, Any]:
        return {"mode": self.mode}


@dataclass(frozen=True)
class ShortEdgePreprocess:
    pixels: int
    mode: Literal["short_edge"] = field(default="short_edge", init=False)

    def __post_init__(self) -> None:
        if not _is_int(self.pixels) or self.pixels <= 0:
            raise _error(
                "vision_preprocess_invalid",
                "短边像素必须为正整数",
            )

    def as_dict(self) -> dict[str, Any]:
        return {"mode": self.mode, "pixels": self.pixels}


@dataclass(frozen=True)
class CustomPreprocess:
    max_width: int
    max_height: int
    allow_upscale: Literal[False] = False
    mode: Literal["custom"] = field(default="custom", init=False)

    def __post_init__(self) -> None:
        if (
            not _is_int(self.max_width)
            or not _is_int(self.max_height)
            or self.max_width <= 0
            or self.max_height <= 0
            or self.allow_upscale is not False
        ):
            raise _error(
                "vision_preprocess_invalid",
                "自定义预处理尺寸必须为正整数且禁止放大",
            )

    def as_dict(self) -> dict[str, Any]:
        return {
            "mode": self.mode,
            "max_width": self.max_width,
            "max_height": self.max_height,
            "allow_upscale": self.allow_upscale,
        }


VisionPreprocess: TypeAlias = (
    OriginalPreprocess | ShortEdgePreprocess | CustomPreprocess
)


@dataclass(frozen=True)
class FirstFrameAnimation:
    """显式声明「取首帧送审」，把丢帧变成可审计的冻结决定。"""

    mode: Literal["first_frame"] = field(default="first_frame", init=False)

    def as_dict(self) -> dict[str, Any]:
        return {"mode": self.mode}


@dataclass(frozen=True)
class RejectAnimation:
    """默认：拒绝动画输入，绝不静默丢帧。"""

    mode: Literal["reject"] = field(default="reject", init=False)

    def as_dict(self) -> dict[str, Any]:
        return {"mode": self.mode}


VisionAnimation: TypeAlias = RejectAnimation | FirstFrameAnimation

@dataclass(frozen=True)
class VisionRolePolicy:
    fidelity: VisionFidelity
    preprocess: VisionPreprocess
    # 默认与 legacy-default 的 ModelMediaPolicy.target_max_frames=1 对齐：
    # 「GIF 取首帧送审」是既定业务规则，显式策略路径默认拒绝反而是能力回退。
    animation: VisionAnimation = field(default_factory=FirstFrameAnimation)

    def as_dict(self) -> dict[str, Any]:
        payload: dict[str, Any] = {
            "fidelity": self.fidelity.as_dict(),
            "preprocess": self.preprocess.as_dict(),
        }
        # 默认策略不落盘：避免已冻结工作流的 policy hash 因新增字段而改变
        if not isinstance(self.animation, FirstFrameAnimation):
            payload["animation"] = self.animation.as_dict()
        return payload


@dataclass(frozen=True)
class LosslessTransportPolicy:
    mode: Literal["lossless"] = field(default="lossless", init=False)
    size_limit: Literal["provider_profile"] = "provider_profile"

    def __post_init__(self) -> None:
        if self.size_limit != "provider_profile":
            raise _error(
                "vision_transport_invalid",
                "图片传输大小门禁必须来自供应商能力档案",
            )

    def as_dict(self) -> dict[str, Any]:
        return {"mode": self.mode, "size_limit": self.size_limit}


@dataclass(frozen=True)
class VisionCapabilityProfile:
    version: Literal["vision-capability-profile-v1"]
    profile_key: str
    detail_modes: tuple[DetailValue, ...]
    pixel_limit_supported: bool
    min_source_pixels: int
    max_source_pixels: int
    min_aspect_ratio: float
    max_aspect_ratio: float
    min_dimension_pixels: int
    allowed_mime_types: tuple[str, ...]
    max_frames: int
    max_single_image_bytes: int
    single_image_safety_margin_bytes: int
    max_request_body_bytes: int
    request_body_safety_margin_bytes: int
    token_formula: str
    source_document_url: str
    observed_revision_date: str
    provider: str = field(compare=True, repr=False)
    model_id: str = field(compare=True, repr=False)

    @property
    def operational_single_image_bytes(self) -> int:
        return self.max_single_image_bytes - self.single_image_safety_margin_bytes

    @property
    def operational_request_body_bytes(self) -> int:
        return self.max_request_body_bytes - self.request_body_safety_margin_bytes

    def as_dict(self) -> dict[str, Any]:
        return {
            "version": self.version,
            "profile_key": self.profile_key,
            "detail_modes": list(self.detail_modes),
            "pixel_limit_supported": self.pixel_limit_supported,
            "min_source_pixels": self.min_source_pixels,
            "max_source_pixels": self.max_source_pixels,
            "min_aspect_ratio": self.min_aspect_ratio,
            "max_aspect_ratio": self.max_aspect_ratio,
            "min_dimension_pixels": self.min_dimension_pixels,
            "allowed_mime_types": list(self.allowed_mime_types),
            "max_frames": self.max_frames,
