            "max_single_image_bytes": self.max_single_image_bytes,
            "single_image_safety_margin_bytes": (
                self.single_image_safety_margin_bytes
            ),
            "max_request_body_bytes": self.max_request_body_bytes,
            "request_body_safety_margin_bytes": (
                self.request_body_safety_margin_bytes
            ),
            "token_formula": self.token_formula,
            "source_document_url": self.source_document_url,
            "observed_revision_date": self.observed_revision_date,
        }

    def supports_detail(self, value: str) -> bool:
        return value in self.detail_modes

    def validate_media(
        self,
        *,
        mime_type: str,
        width: int,
        height: int,
        frame_count: int = 1,
    ) -> None:
        normalized_mime = mime_type.strip().lower() if isinstance(mime_type, str) else ""
        if normalized_mime not in self.allowed_mime_types:
            raise _error(
                "media_mime_type_unsupported",
                "图片 MIME 不在冻结供应商能力档案中",
                evidence={"mime_type": normalized_mime},
            )
        if (
            not _is_int(width)
            or not _is_int(height)
            or width < self.min_dimension_pixels
            or height < self.min_dimension_pixels
        ):
            raise _error(
                "media_pixel_constraints_invalid",
                "图片宽高低于供应商能力档案下限",
            )
        pixels = width * height
        aspect_ratio = width / height
        if (
            pixels < self.min_source_pixels
            or pixels > self.max_source_pixels
            or aspect_ratio < self.min_aspect_ratio
            or aspect_ratio > self.max_aspect_ratio
        ):
            raise _error(
                "media_pixel_constraints_invalid",
                "图片像素或宽高比超出供应商能力档案",
                evidence={
                    "width": width,
                    "height": height,
                    "pixels": pixels,
                },
            )
        if (
            not _is_int(frame_count)
            or frame_count < 1
            or frame_count > self.max_frames
        ):
            raise _error(
                "media_frame_limit_exceeded",
                "图片帧数超出供应商能力档案",
                evidence={"frame_count": frame_count, "max_frames": self.max_frames},
            )

    def effective_pixels(
        self,
        fidelity: VisionFidelity,
        width: int,
        height: int,
    ) -> int:
        _validate_estimate_dimensions(width, height)
        if self.token_formula == QWEN_205_PROFILE["token_formula"]:
            if not isinstance(fidelity, DetailFidelity) or fidelity.value != "high":
                raise _error("vision_fidelity_invalid", "该Qwen部署仅支持冻结high图片处理策略")
            return _qwen_205_resized_pixels(width, height)
        pixels = width * height
        if isinstance(fidelity, PixelLimitFidelity):
            return min(max(pixels, fidelity.min_pixels), fidelity.max_pixels)
        if fidelity.value == "low":
            return min(pixels, 2_257_920)
        if fidelity.value == "high":
            return 2_257_920
        return min(max(pixels, 2_257_920), 9_031_680)

    def max_image_tokens(self, fidelity: VisionFidelity) -> int:
        if self.token_formula == QWEN_205_PROFILE["token_formula"]:
            return 1024
        if isinstance(fidelity, PixelLimitFidelity):
            return 5120
        return {"low": 1280, "high": 1280, "xhigh": 5120}[fidelity.value]


@dataclass(frozen=True)
class VisionInputPolicy:
    version: Literal["vision-input-policy-v1", "legacy-default"]
    provider_profile: str
    roles: Mapping[MediaRole, VisionRolePolicy]
    transport: LosslessTransportPolicy = field(default_factory=LosslessTransportPolicy)

    def __post_init__(self) -> None:
        if self.version not in {_POLICY_VERSION, _LEGACY_VERSION}:
            raise _error(
                "vision_input_policy_invalid",
                "图片输入策略版本不受支持",
            )
        copied = dict(self.roles)
        if set(copied) != set(_MEDIA_ROLES) or not all(
            isinstance(copied[role], VisionRolePolicy) for role in _MEDIA_ROLES
        ):
            raise _error(
                "vision_input_policy_invalid",
                "图片输入策略必须且只能包含 target 和 anchor",
            )
        object.__setattr__(self, "roles", MappingProxyType(copied))

    @property
    def is_legacy_default(self) -> bool:
        return self.version == _LEGACY_VERSION

    def as_dict(self) -> dict[str, Any]:
        payload: dict[str, Any] = {
            "version": self.version,
            "provider_profile": self.provider_profile,
            "roles": {
                role: self.roles[role].as_dict()
                for role in _MEDIA_ROLES
            },
        }
        if not self.is_legacy_default:
            payload["transport"] = self.transport.as_dict()
        return payload


def _recognized_seed_20(provider: str, model_id: str) -> bool:
    normalized_provider = provider.strip().lower().replace("_", "-")
    normalized_model = model_id.strip().lower().replace("_", "-").replace(".", "-")
    return normalized_provider in {
        "doubao",
        "volcengine",
        "volcengine-ark",
        "ark",
    } and "doubao-seed-2-0" in normalized_model


def same_vision_capability(
    left: Any,
    right: Any,
    *,
    provider: str,
    model_id: str,
) -> bool:
    """比较两份图片能力档案是否等价（按解析后的规范形态，不看字面量）。

    2026-09-03：设计案例工作流点「检查流程」报「冻结的图片能力档案与当前所选
    模型不一致」，实际两边数值完全相同——模型注册表里 max_aspect_ratio 存的是
    整数 150，解析后是 float 150.0，canonical_json 逐字符比较就判成了漂移。
    档案是否等价必须按解析后的规范形态判断。
    """

    def normalize(value: Any) -> str:
        if value is None:
            return "<none>"
        source = value.as_dict() if hasattr(value, "as_dict") else value
        try:
            parsed = parse_vision_capability_profile(
                source, provider=provider, model_id=model_id
            )
        except VisionInputPolicyError:
            return canonical_json(source)
        return canonical_json(parsed.as_dict())

    return normalize(left) == normalize(right)


def parse_vision_capability_profile(
    value: Mapping[str, Any] | None,
    *,
    provider: str,
    model_id: str,
) -> VisionCapabilityProfile:
    """Parse an exact, versioned provider capability profile.

    The built-in profile is selected only for a recognized Volcengine/Doubao
    Seed 2.0 provider/model pair.  Every other model must supply a complete
    profile explicitly.
    """

    if not isinstance(provider, str) or not provider.strip():
        raise _error(
            "vision_capability_profile_invalid",
            "provider 不能为空",
        )
    if not isinstance(model_id, str) or not model_id.strip():
        raise _error(
            "vision_capability_profile_invalid",
            "model_id 不能为空",
        )
    if value is None or value == {}:
        if not _recognized_seed_20(provider, model_id):
            raise _error(
                "vision_capability_profile_required",
                "未知视觉模型必须显式配置版本化图片能力档案",
            )
        value = SEED_20_PROFILE
    if not isinstance(value, Mapping):
        raise _error(
            "vision_capability_profile_invalid",
            "图片能力档案必须是对象",
        )
    _require_exact_keys(
        value,
        _CAPABILITY_KEYS,
        code="vision_capability_profile_invalid",
        path="vision_capability_profile",
    )

    version = value["version"]
    profile_key = value["profile_key"]
    detail_modes_raw = value["detail_modes"]
    allowed_mime_raw = value["allowed_mime_types"]
    if version != _CAPABILITY_VERSION:
        raise _error(
            "vision_capability_profile_invalid",
            "图片能力档案版本必须是 vision-capability-profile-v1",
        )
    if not isinstance(profile_key, str) or not _PROFILE_KEY.fullmatch(profile_key):
        raise _error(
            "vision_capability_profile_invalid",
            "图片能力档案 profile_key 不合法",
        )
    if (
        not isinstance(detail_modes_raw, Sequence)
        or isinstance(detail_modes_raw, (str, bytes, bytearray))
        or not detail_modes_raw
        or any(item not in _DETAIL_VALUES for item in detail_modes_raw)
        or len(detail_modes_raw) != len(set(detail_modes_raw))
    ):
        raise _error(
            "vision_capability_profile_invalid",
            "detail_modes 必须是 low/high/xhigh 的非空无重复数组",
        )
    if not isinstance(value["pixel_limit_supported"], bool):
        raise _error(
            "vision_capability_profile_invalid",
            "pixel_limit_supported 必须是布尔值",
        )

    int_fields = (
        "min_source_pixels",
        "max_source_pixels",
        "min_dimension_pixels",
        "max_frames",
        "max_single_image_bytes",
        "single_image_safety_margin_bytes",
        "max_request_body_bytes",
        "request_body_safety_margin_bytes",
    )
    if any(not _is_int(value[name]) for name in int_fields):
        raise _error(
            "vision_capability_profile_invalid",
            "图片能力档案的像素、帧数与字节字段必须是整数",
        )
    if (
        value["min_source_pixels"] < 196
        or value["max_source_pixels"] > (
            60_000_000 if profile_key == QWEN_205_PROFILE["profile_key"] else 36_000_000
        )
        or value["min_source_pixels"] > value["max_source_pixels"]
        or value["min_dimension_pixels"] < 15
        or value["min_dimension_pixels"] ** 2 < value["min_source_pixels"]
        or value["max_frames"] < 1
    ):
        raise _error(
            "vision_capability_profile_invalid",
            "图片能力档案的像素、尺寸或帧数边界不合法",
        )
    min_aspect = value["min_aspect_ratio"]
    max_aspect = value["max_aspect_ratio"]
    if (
        not _is_number(min_aspect)
        or not _is_number(max_aspect)
        or min_aspect <= 0
        or min_aspect > max_aspect
        or max_aspect > 150
    ):
        raise _error(
            "vision_capability_profile_invalid",
            "图片能力档案的宽高比边界不合法",
        )
    if (
        not isinstance(allowed_mime_raw, Sequence)
        or isinstance(allowed_mime_raw, (str, bytes, bytearray))
        or not allowed_mime_raw
        or any(
            not isinstance(item, str)
            or not item.startswith("image/")
            or item != item.strip().lower()
            for item in allowed_mime_raw
        )
        or len(allowed_mime_raw) != len(set(allowed_mime_raw))
    ):
        raise _error(
            "vision_capability_profile_invalid",
            "allowed_mime_types 必须是规范化且无重复的图片 MIME 数组",
        )
    if (
        value["max_single_image_bytes"] <= 0
        or value["single_image_safety_margin_bytes"] < 0
        or value["max_single_image_bytes"]
        <= value["single_image_safety_margin_bytes"]
        or value["max_request_body_bytes"] <= 0
        or value["request_body_safety_margin_bytes"] < 0
        or value["max_request_body_bytes"]
        <= value["request_body_safety_margin_bytes"]
    ):
        raise _error(
            "vision_capability_profile_invalid",
            "供应商字节上限必须大于对应安全余量",
        )
    token_formula = value["token_formula"]
    source_document_url = value["source_document_url"]
    observed_revision_date = value["observed_revision_date"]
    if not isinstance(token_formula, str) or not token_formula.strip():
        raise _error(
            "vision_capability_profile_invalid",
            "token_formula 不能为空",
        )
    if (
        not isinstance(source_document_url, str)
        or not source_document_url.startswith("https://")
    ):
        raise _error(
            "vision_capability_profile_invalid",
            "能力来源必须是 HTTPS 文档地址",
        )
    if (
        not isinstance(observed_revision_date, str)
        or not _ISO_DATE.fullmatch(observed_revision_date)
    ):
        raise _error(
            "vision_capability_profile_invalid",
            "observed_revision_date 必须是 YYYY-MM-DD",
        )

    builtin = next((p for p in (SEED_20_PROFILE, QWEN_205_PROFILE)
                    if p["profile_key"] == profile_key), None)
    if token_formula == QWEN_205_PROFILE["token_formula"] and builtin is not QWEN_205_PROFILE:
        raise _error("vision_capability_profile_invalid", "Qwen部署公式必须绑定其冻结能力档案")
    if builtin is QWEN_205_PROFILE and provider.strip() != "local_qwen":
        raise _error("vision_capability_profile_invalid", "Qwen部署档案仅用于local_qwen")
    if builtin is not None:
        # A parsed snapshot serializes the aspect limits as floats.  JSON
        # ``150`` and ``150.0`` are the same capability value and must survive
        # freeze -> replay, while every substantive built-in field remains
        # immutable.
        candidate_identity = dict(value)
        builtin_identity = dict(builtin)
        for field_name in ("min_aspect_ratio", "max_aspect_ratio"):
            candidate_identity[field_name] = float(
                candidate_identity[field_name]
            )
            builtin_identity[field_name] = float(builtin_identity[field_name])
        if canonical_json(candidate_identity) != canonical_json(
            builtin_identity
        ):
            raise _error(
                "vision_capability_profile_invalid",
                "内置 profile_key 的能力值不可漂移，变更需创建新版本",
            )

    return VisionCapabilityProfile(
        version=version,
        profile_key=profile_key,
        detail_modes=tuple(detail_modes_raw),
        pixel_limit_supported=value["pixel_limit_supported"],
        min_source_pixels=value["min_source_pixels"],
        max_source_pixels=value["max_source_pixels"],
        min_aspect_ratio=float(min_aspect),
        max_aspect_ratio=float(max_aspect),
        min_dimension_pixels=value["min_dimension_pixels"],
        allowed_mime_types=tuple(allowed_mime_raw),
        max_frames=value["max_frames"],
        max_single_image_bytes=value["max_single_image_bytes"],
        single_image_safety_margin_bytes=value[
            "single_image_safety_margin_bytes"
        ],
        max_request_body_bytes=value["max_request_body_bytes"],
        request_body_safety_margin_bytes=value[
            "request_body_safety_margin_bytes"
        ],
        token_formula=token_formula,
        source_document_url=source_document_url,
        observed_revision_date=observed_revision_date,
        provider=provider.strip(),
        model_id=model_id.strip(),
    )


def seed_20_capability(
    *,
    provider: str = "doubao",
    model_id: str = "doubao-seed-2-0-lite-260215",
) -> VisionCapabilityProfile:
    return parse_vision_capability_profile(
        SEED_20_PROFILE,
        provider=provider,
        model_id=model_id,
    )


def default_vision_input_policy(
    capability: VisionCapabilityProfile | None = None,
) -> VisionInputPolicy:
    resolved = capability or seed_20_capability()
    return VisionInputPolicy(
        version=_POLICY_VERSION,
        provider_profile=resolved.profile_key,
        roles={
            "target": VisionRolePolicy(
                fidelity=DetailFidelity("high"),
                preprocess=OriginalPreprocess(),
            ),
            # 锚点图默认降像素（老大 2026-09-02 定），目标图保持原图 high
            "anchor": VisionRolePolicy(
                fidelity=(PixelLimitFidelity(196 * 196, 1024 * 1024)
                          if resolved.pixel_limit_supported else DetailFidelity("high")),
                preprocess=ShortEdgePreprocess(1024),
            ),
        },
    )


def _legacy_default_policy(capability: VisionCapabilityProfile) -> VisionInputPolicy:
    return VisionInputPolicy(
        version=_LEGACY_VERSION,
        provider_profile=capability.profile_key,
        roles={
            "target": VisionRolePolicy(
                fidelity=DetailFidelity("high"),
                preprocess=ShortEdgePreprocess(600),
            ),
            "anchor": VisionRolePolicy(
                fidelity=DetailFidelity("high"),
                preprocess=ShortEdgePreprocess(600),
            ),
        },
    )


def _parse_fidelity(
    value: Any,
    *,
    capability: VisionCapabilityProfile,
    path: str,
) -> VisionFidelity:
    if not isinstance(value, Mapping):
        raise _error(
            "vision_fidelity_invalid",
            f"{path} 必须是对象",
        )
    mode = value.get("mode")
    if mode == "detail":
        extra = set(value) - {"mode", "value"}
        if extra & {"min_pixels", "max_pixels"}:
            raise _error(
                "vision_fidelity_fields_conflict",
                f"{path} 不能同时配置 detail 和 image_pixel_limit",
            )
        _require_exact_keys(
            value,
            frozenset({"mode", "value"}),
            code="vision_fidelity_invalid",
            path=path,
        )
        detail = DetailFidelity(value["value"])
        if not capability.supports_detail(detail.value):
            raise _error(
                "vision_fidelity_unsupported",
                f"{path} 的 {detail.value} 不在模型能力档案中",
            )
        return detail
    if mode == "pixel_limit":
        extra = set(value) - {"mode", "min_pixels", "max_pixels"}
        if "value" in extra:
            raise _error(
                "vision_fidelity_fields_conflict",
                f"{path} 不能同时配置 detail 和 image_pixel_limit",
            )
        _require_exact_keys(
            value,
            frozenset({"mode", "min_pixels", "max_pixels"}),
            code="vision_fidelity_invalid",
            path=path,
        )
        if not capability.pixel_limit_supported:
            raise _error(
                "vision_pixel_limit_unsupported",
                f"{path} 所选模型不支持 image_pixel_limit",
            )
        fidelity = PixelLimitFidelity(
            min_pixels=value["min_pixels"],
            max_pixels=value["max_pixels"],
        )
        if (
            fidelity.min_pixels < capability.min_source_pixels
            or fidelity.max_pixels > capability.max_source_pixels
        ):
            raise _error(
                "vision_pixel_limit_invalid",
                f"{path} 像素上限超出模型能力档案",
            )
        return fidelity
    raise _error(
        "vision_fidelity_invalid",
        f"{path}.mode 必须是 detail 或 pixel_limit",
    )


def _parse_preprocess(
    value: Any,
    *,
    capability: VisionCapabilityProfile,
    path: str,
) -> VisionPreprocess:
    if not isinstance(value, Mapping):
        raise _error(
            "vision_preprocess_invalid",
            f"{path} 必须是对象",
        )
    mode = value.get("mode")
    if mode == "original":
        _require_exact_keys(
            value,
            frozenset({"mode"}),
            code="vision_preprocess_invalid",
            path=path,
        )
        return OriginalPreprocess()
    if mode == "short_edge":
        _require_exact_keys(
            value,
            frozenset({"mode", "pixels"}),
            code="vision_preprocess_invalid",
            path=path,
        )
        pixels = value["pixels"]
        maximum = math.isqrt(capability.max_source_pixels)
        if (
            not _is_int(pixels)
            or pixels < capability.min_dimension_pixels
            or pixels > maximum
        ):
            raise _error(
                "vision_preprocess_invalid",
                f"{path}.pixels 必须位于 {capability.min_dimension_pixels}～{maximum}",
            )
        return ShortEdgePreprocess(pixels)
    if mode == "custom":
        _require_exact_keys(
            value,
            frozenset(
                {"mode", "max_width", "max_height", "allow_upscale"}
            ),
            code="vision_preprocess_invalid",
            path=path,
        )
        width = value["max_width"]
        height = value["max_height"]
        allow_upscale = value["allow_upscale"]
        if (
            not _is_int(width)
            or not _is_int(height)
            or width < capability.min_dimension_pixels
            or height < capability.min_dimension_pixels
            or width * height > capability.max_source_pixels
            or not (
                capability.min_aspect_ratio
                <= width / height
                <= capability.max_aspect_ratio
            )
            or allow_upscale is not False
        ):
            raise _error(
                "vision_preprocess_invalid",
                f"{path} 的尺寸、宽高比或放大设置不合法",
            )
        return CustomPreprocess(
            max_width=width,
            max_height=height,
            allow_upscale=False,
        )
    raise _error(
        "vision_preprocess_invalid",
        f"{path}.mode 必须是 original、short_edge 或 custom",
    )


def _parse_role(
    value: Any,
    *,
    capability: VisionCapabilityProfile,
    role: MediaRole,
) -> VisionRolePolicy:
    if not isinstance(value, Mapping):
        raise _error(
            "vision_input_policy_invalid",
            f"roles.{role} 必须是对象",
        )
    allowed = set(value) - {"fidelity", "preprocess", "animation"}
    if allowed or not {"fidelity", "preprocess"} <= set(value):
        _require_exact_keys(
            value,
            frozenset({"fidelity", "preprocess"}),
            code="vision_input_policy_invalid",
            path=f"roles.{role}",
        )
    return VisionRolePolicy(
        fidelity=_parse_fidelity(
            value["fidelity"],
            capability=capability,
            path=f"roles.{role}.fidelity",
        ),
        preprocess=_parse_preprocess(
            value["preprocess"],
            capability=capability,
            path=f"roles.{role}.preprocess",
        ),
        animation=_parse_animation(
            value.get("animation"),
            path=f"roles.{role}.animation",
        ),
    )


def _parse_animation(value: Any, *, path: str) -> VisionAnimation:
    if value is None:
        # 存量冻结策略普遍没有该键，必须自动获得既定的首帧能力
        return FirstFrameAnimation()
    if not isinstance(value, Mapping):
        raise _error("vision_animation_invalid", f"{path} 必须是对象")
    _require_exact_keys(
        value,
        frozenset({"mode"}),
        code="vision_animation_invalid",
        path=path,
    )
    mode = value.get("mode")
    if mode == "reject":
        return RejectAnimation()
    if mode == "first_frame":
        return FirstFrameAnimation()
    raise _error(
        "vision_animation_invalid",
        f"{path}.mode 必须是 reject 或 first_frame",
    )


def parse_vision_input_policy(
    value: Mapping[str, Any] | None,
    *,
    capability: VisionCapabilityProfile,
    legacy_if_missing: bool,
) -> VisionInputPolicy:
    if not isinstance(legacy_if_missing, bool):
        raise _error(
            "vision_input_policy_invalid",
            "legacy_if_missing 必须是布尔值",
        )
    if value is None:
        if legacy_if_missing:
            return _legacy_default_policy(capability)
        return default_vision_input_policy(capability)
    if not isinstance(value, Mapping):
        raise _error(
            "vision_input_policy_invalid",
            "vision_input_policy 必须是对象",
        )
    _require_exact_keys(
        value,
        frozenset({"version", "provider_profile", "roles", "transport"}),
        code="vision_input_policy_invalid",
        path="vision_input_policy",
    )
    if value["version"] != _POLICY_VERSION:
        raise _error(
            "vision_input_policy_invalid",
            "vision_input_policy.version 必须是 vision-input-policy-v1",
        )
    if value["provider_profile"] != capability.profile_key:
        raise _error(
            "vision_provider_profile_mismatch",
            "图片策略引用的能力档案与所选模型不一致",
        )
    roles_raw = value["roles"]
    if not isinstance(roles_raw, Mapping) or set(roles_raw) != set(_MEDIA_ROLES):
        raise _error(
            "vision_input_policy_invalid",
            "roles 必须且只能包含 target 和 anchor",
        )
    transport_raw = value["transport"]
    if not isinstance(transport_raw, Mapping):
        raise _error(
            "vision_transport_invalid",
            "transport 必须是对象",
        )
    _require_exact_keys(
        transport_raw,
        frozenset({"mode", "size_limit"}),
        code="vision_transport_invalid",
        path="transport",
    )
    if (
        transport_raw["mode"] != "lossless"
        or transport_raw["size_limit"] != "provider_profile"
    ):
        raise _error(
            "vision_transport_invalid",
            "新图片策略必须启用 lossless/provider_profile 传输门禁",
        )

    return VisionInputPolicy(
        version=_POLICY_VERSION,
        provider_profile=capability.profile_key,
        roles={
            role: _parse_role(
                roles_raw[role],
                capability=capability,
                role=role,
            )
            for role in _MEDIA_ROLES
        },
        transport=LosslessTransportPolicy(),
    )


def _validate_estimate_dimensions(width: int, height: int) -> None:
    if not _is_int(width) or not _is_int(height) or width <= 0 or height <= 0:
        raise _error(
            "media_pixel_constraints_invalid",
            "Token 估算宽高必须为正整数",
        )


def estimate_image_tokens(
    capability: VisionCapabilityProfile,
    role_policy: VisionRolePolicy,
    width: int,
    height: int,
) -> int:
    """Return the frozen official estimate, never provider actual usage."""

    _validate_estimate_dimensions(width, height)
    if capability.token_formula == QWEN_205_PROFILE["token_formula"]:
        return capability.effective_pixels(role_policy.fidelity, width, height) // 1024
    if capability.token_formula != "seed-2.0-v1":
        raise _error(
            "vision_token_formula_unsupported",
            "当前运行时不支持该能力档案的图片 Token 公式",
        )
    fidelity = role_policy.fidelity
    if isinstance(fidelity, DetailFidelity) and fidelity.value == "high":
        return 1280
    effective_pixels = capability.effective_pixels(fidelity, width, height)
    estimate = math.ceil(effective_pixels / 1764)
    if isinstance(fidelity, DetailFidelity) and fidelity.value == "xhigh":
        estimate = max(1280, estimate)
    return max(1, min(estimate, capability.max_image_tokens(fidelity)))


def vision_policy_hash(
    policy: VisionInputPolicy,
    capability: VisionCapabilityProfile,
) -> str:
    payload = {
        "policy": policy.as_dict(),
        "capability": capability.as_dict(),
    }
    return hashlib.sha256(canonical_json(payload).encode("utf-8")).hexdigest()


__all__ = [
    "SEED_20_PROFILE",
    "QWEN_205_PROFILE",
    "CustomPreprocess",
    "DetailFidelity",
    "LosslessTransportPolicy",
    "MediaRole",
    "OriginalPreprocess",
    "PixelLimitFidelity",
    "ShortEdgePreprocess",
    "VisionCapabilityProfile",
    "VisionInputPolicy",
    "VisionInputPolicyError",
    "VisionRolePolicy",
    "default_vision_input_policy",
    "estimate_image_tokens",
    "parse_vision_capability_profile",
    "parse_vision_input_policy",
    "seed_20_capability",
    "vision_policy_hash",
]

===== FILE backend/app/volcengine_ark_openapi.py =====
"""火山方舟管控面 OpenAPI 客户端：只读 AK/SK + HMAC-SHA256 签名。

只封装本平台需要的三个动作：
- ``GetInferenceUsage``：按接入点、按小时查询推理用量（token / 请求数）；
- ``GetEndpoint``：读取接入点状态与底层模型，用于验收；
- ``CreateEndpoint``：按类目工作流自动创建带标签的接入点（先 DryRun 预检再创建）。

不提供更新、停止或删除接入点的能力；管控 AK/SK 与推理 API Key 分离保存。
签名算法与官方 volc-openapi-demos/signature/python/sign.py 一致
（Credential=AK/短日期/Region/Service/request，SignedHeaders=content-type;host;x-content-sha256;x-date）。
"""
from __future__ import annotations

import hashlib
import hmac
import json
from dataclasses import dataclass
from datetime import datetime, timezone
from typing import Any, Callable, Mapping
from urllib.parse import quote

import httpx

ARK_SERVICE = "ark"
ARK_API_VERSION = "2024-01-01"
DEFAULT_REGION = "cn-beijing"
_ALLOWED_REGIONS = {"cn-beijing"}
_ALLOWED_ACTIONS = {"GetInferenceUsage", "GetEndpoint", "CreateEndpoint"}

# transport(method, url, headers, body) -> (status_code, response_bytes)
Transport = Callable[[str, str, Mapping[str, str], bytes], tuple[int, bytes]]


class VolcengineOpenApiError(RuntimeError):
    def __init__(self, code: str, message: str, *, status_code: int | None = None):
        super().__init__(message)
        self.code = code
        self.status_code = status_code


def ark_host(region: str) -> str:
    if region not in _ALLOWED_REGIONS:
        raise VolcengineOpenApiError("volcengine_region_unsupported", f"不支持的火山区域：{region}")
    return f"ark.{region}.volcengineapi.com"


def _hmac_sha256(key: bytes, content: str) -> bytes:
    return hmac.new(key, content.encode("utf-8"), hashlib.sha256).digest()


def _sha256_hex(content: bytes) -> str:
    return hashlib.sha256(content).hexdigest()


def _canonical_query(params: Mapping[str, str]) -> str:
    return "&".join(
        f"{quote(key, safe='-_.~')}={quote(params[key], safe='-_.~')}"
        for key in sorted(params)
    ).replace("+", "%20")


@dataclass(frozen=True)
class SignedRequest:
    method: str
    url: str
    headers: dict[str, str]
    body: bytes
    canonical_request: str


def sign_ark_request(
    *,
    access_key: str,
    secret_key: str,
    region: str,
    action: str,
    body: Mapping[str, Any],
    signed_at: datetime,
) -> SignedRequest:
    """构造带签名的 POST 请求；纯函数，便于用固定时间做回归测试。"""

    if action not in _ALLOWED_ACTIONS:
        raise VolcengineOpenApiError("volcengine_action_forbidden", f"不允许调用火山动作：{action}")
    if not access_key or not secret_key:
        raise VolcengineOpenApiError("volcengine_credentials_missing", "尚未配置火山账单只读 AK/SK")
    host = ark_host(region)
    if signed_at.tzinfo is None:
        signed_at = signed_at.replace(tzinfo=timezone.utc)
    x_date = signed_at.astimezone(timezone.utc).strftime("%Y%m%dT%H%M%SZ")
    short_date = x_date[:8]
    payload = json.dumps(dict(body), ensure_ascii=False, separators=(",", ":")).encode("utf-8")
    content_sha256 = _sha256_hex(payload)
    content_type = "application/json"
    query = {"Action": action, "Version": ARK_API_VERSION}
    signed_headers = "content-type;host;x-content-sha256;x-date"
    canonical_request = "\n".join(
        [
            "POST",
            "/",
            _canonical_query(query),
            "\n".join(
                [
                    f"content-type:{content_type}",
                    f"host:{host}",
                    f"x-content-sha256:{content_sha256}",
                    f"x-date:{x_date}",
                ]
            ),
            "",
            signed_headers,
            content_sha256,
        ]
    )
    credential_scope = "/".join([short_date, region, ARK_SERVICE, "request"])
    string_to_sign = "\n".join(
        [
            "HMAC-SHA256",
            x_date,
            credential_scope,
            _sha256_hex(canonical_request.encode("utf-8")),
        ]
    )
    k_date = _hmac_sha256(secret_key.encode("utf-8"), short_date)
    k_region = _hmac_sha256(k_date, region)
    k_service = _hmac_sha256(k_region, ARK_SERVICE)
    k_signing = _hmac_sha256(k_service, "request")
    signature = _hmac_sha256(k_signing, string_to_sign).hex()
    headers = {
        "Content-Type": content_type,
        "Host": host,
        "X-Content-Sha256": content_sha256,
        "X-Date": x_date,
        "Authorization": (
            f"HMAC-SHA256 Credential={access_key}/{credential_scope}, "
            f"SignedHeaders={signed_headers}, Signature={signature}"
        ),
    }
    url = f"https://{host}/?{_canonical_query(query)}"
    return SignedRequest(
        method="POST",
        url=url,
        headers=headers,
        body=payload,
        canonical_request=canonical_request,
    )


def httpx_transport(timeout_seconds: float = 30.0) -> Transport:
    def transport(method: str, url: str, headers: Mapping[str, str], body: bytes) -> tuple[int, bytes]:
        try:
            with httpx.Client(timeout=httpx.Timeout(timeout_seconds)) as client:
                response = client.request(method, url, headers=dict(headers), content=body)
        except httpx.TimeoutException as exc:
            raise VolcengineOpenApiError("volcengine_timeout", "火山管控面请求超时") from exc
        except httpx.HTTPError as exc:
            raise VolcengineOpenApiError("volcengine_network_error", f"火山管控面网络错误：{type(exc).__name__}") from exc
        return response.status_code, response.content

    return transport


@dataclass(frozen=True)
class InferenceUsageRow:
    endpoint_id: str
    usage_date: str
    usage_hour: int
    input_tokens: int
    output_tokens: int
    total_tokens: int
    image_count: int
    request_count: int
    raw: dict[str, Any]


def _int_field(row: Mapping[str, Any], name: str) -> int:
    value = row.get(name)
    if value is None or value == "":
        return 0
    try:
        parsed = int(str(value))
    except (TypeError, ValueError) as exc:
        raise VolcengineOpenApiError(
            "volcengine_usage_field_invalid", f"火山用量字段 {name} 不是整数：{value!r}"
        ) from exc
    if parsed < 0:
        raise VolcengineOpenApiError("volcengine_usage_field_invalid", f"火山用量字段 {name} 为负数")
    return parsed


def parse_inference_usage(result: Mapping[str, Any]) -> list[InferenceUsageRow]:
    """按 Fields 名动态映射 Data 行；未知列原样保留在 raw 中。"""

    fields = result.get("Fields")
    data = result.get("Data")
    if not isinstance(fields, list) or not isinstance(data, list):
        raise VolcengineOpenApiError("volcengine_usage_shape_invalid", "火山用量响应缺少 Fields 或 Data")
    names: list[str] = []
    for item in fields:
        name = item.get("Name") if isinstance(item, Mapping) else None
        if not isinstance(name, str) or not name:
            raise VolcengineOpenApiError("volcengine_usage_shape_invalid", "火山用量列定义不合法")
        names.append(name)
    required = {"ModelEndpoint", "Day", "Hour", "InputTokens", "OutputTokens"}
    missing = sorted(required - set(names))
    if missing:
        raise VolcengineOpenApiError(
            "volcengine_usage_shape_invalid", "火山用量响应缺少列：" + "、".join(missing)
        )
    rows: list[InferenceUsageRow] = []
    for values in data:
        if not isinstance(values, list) or len(values) != len(names):
            raise VolcengineOpenApiError("volcengine_usage_shape_invalid", "火山用量数据行与列定义长度不一致")
        row = {name: value for name, value in zip(names, values)}
        endpoint_id = str(row.get("ModelEndpoint") or "").strip()
        usage_date = str(row.get("Day") or "").strip()[:10]
        if not endpoint_id or len(usage_date) != 10:
            raise VolcengineOpenApiError("volcengine_usage_shape_invalid", "火山用量行缺少接入点或日期")
        hour = _int_field(row, "Hour")
        if hour > 23:
            raise VolcengineOpenApiError("volcengine_usage_field_invalid", f"火山用量小时超界：{hour}")
        input_tokens = _int_field(row, "InputTokens")
        output_tokens = _int_field(row, "OutputTokens")
        total_tokens = _int_field(row, "TotalTokens") if "TotalTokens" in row else input_tokens + output_tokens
        rows.append(
            InferenceUsageRow(
                endpoint_id=endpoint_id,
                usage_date=usage_date,
                usage_hour=hour,
                input_tokens=input_tokens,
                output_tokens=output_tokens,
                total_tokens=total_tokens,
                image_count=_int_field(row, "ImageCount") if "ImageCount" in row else 0,
                request_count=_int_field(row, "ReqCnt") if "ReqCnt" in row else 0,
                raw={name: (value if isinstance(value, (str, int, float, bool)) or value is None else str(value)) for name, value in row.items()},
            )
        )
    return rows


class ArkControlPlaneClient:
    """只读客户端。凭据以明文传入（调用方负责从密钥引用解密），不落日志。"""

    def __init__(
        self,
        *,
        access_key: str,
        secret_key: str,
        region: str = DEFAULT_REGION,
        transport: Transport | None = None,
        now: Callable[[], datetime] = lambda: datetime.now(timezone.utc),
    ) -> None:
        self._access_key = access_key
        self._secret_key = secret_key
        self._region = region
        self._transport = transport or httpx_transport()
        self._now = now

    def _call(self, action: str, body: Mapping[str, Any]) -> dict[str, Any]:
        request = sign_ark_request(
            access_key=self._access_key,
            secret_key=self._secret_key,
            region=self._region,
            action=action,
            body=body,
            signed_at=self._now(),
        )
        status_code, content = self._transport(request.method, request.url, request.headers, request.body)
        try:
            document = json.loads(content.decode("utf-8") or "{}")
        except (UnicodeDecodeError, ValueError) as exc:
            raise VolcengineOpenApiError(
                "volcengine_response_invalid", f"火山管控面返回非 JSON（HTTP {status_code}）", status_code=status_code
            ) from exc
        metadata = document.get("ResponseMetadata") if isinstance(document, Mapping) else None
        error = metadata.get("Error") if isinstance(metadata, Mapping) else None
        if status_code >= 400 or isinstance(error, Mapping):
            code = str((error or {}).get("Code") or f"http_{status_code}")
            message = str((error or {}).get("Message") or "火山管控面请求失败")
            raise VolcengineOpenApiError(
                f"volcengine_{code}", f"{action} 失败：{message}", status_code=status_code
            )
        result = document.get("Result") if isinstance(document, Mapping) else None
        if not isinstance(result, Mapping):
            raise VolcengineOpenApiError("volcengine_response_invalid", f"{action} 响应缺少 Result", status_code=status_code)
        return dict(result)

    def get_inference_usage(
        self,
        *,
        start_date: str,
        end_date: str,
        endpoint_ids: list[str],
        project_name: str = "default",
    ) -> list[InferenceUsageRow]:
        body: dict[str, Any] = {
            "QueryInterval": "Hour",
            "StartTime": start_date,
            "EndTime": end_date,
            "ProjectName": project_name or "default",
        }
        if endpoint_ids:
            body["Filters"] = [{"Key": "ModelEndpoint", "Values": list(endpoint_ids)}]
        return parse_inference_usage(self._call("GetInferenceUsage", body))

    def get_endpoint(self, endpoint_id: str) -> dict[str, Any]:
        return self._call("GetEndpoint", {"Id": endpoint_id})

    def create_endpoint(
        self,
        *,
        name: str,
        foundation_model: str,
        model_version: str,
        description: str = "",
        tags: Mapping[str, str] | None = None,
        rpm: int | None = None,
        tpm: int | None = None,
        project_name: str = "default",
        dry_run: bool = False,
    ) -> str | None:
        """创建接入点；``dry_run`` 只预检参数不创建。返回接入点 ID（预检时为 None）。"""

        if not name.strip():
            raise VolcengineOpenApiError("volcengine_endpoint_name_invalid", "接入点名称不能为空")
        if not foundation_model.strip():
            raise VolcengineOpenApiError("volcengine_endpoint_model_invalid", "底层模型不能为空")
        body: dict[str, Any] = {
            "Name": name.strip()[:128],
            "ModelReference": {
                "FoundationModel": {
                    "Name": foundation_model.strip(),
                    **({"ModelVersion": model_version.strip()} if model_version.strip() else {}),
                }
            },
            "ProjectName": project_name or "default",
            "DryRun": bool(dry_run),
        }
        if description:
            body["Description"] = description[:500]
        if tags:
            body["Tags"] = [{"Key": str(key)[:64], "Value": str(value)[:256]} for key, value in tags.items()]
        rate_limit = {}
        if rpm:
            rate_limit["Rpm"] = int(rpm)
        if tpm:
            rate_limit["Tpm"] = int(tpm)
        if rate_limit:
            body["RateLimit"] = rate_limit
        result = self._call("CreateEndpoint", body)
        endpoint_id = result.get("Id")
        if dry_run:
            return endpoint_id if isinstance(endpoint_id, str) and endpoint_id else None
        if not isinstance(endpoint_id, str) or not endpoint_id:
            raise VolcengineOpenApiError("volcengine_response_invalid", "CreateEndpoint 未返回接入点 ID")
        return endpoint_id

===== FILE backend/app/volcengine_billing.py =====
"""火山账单同步与本地成本对账。

- 只读 AK/SK 每小时（可配置）拉取 ``GetInferenceUsage``，按接入点、按小时落表；
- 按接入点 × 业务日（北京时间）汇总本地调用流水与火山用量，写对账行；
- 业务日结束后（次日）且本次同步成功时冻结对账行；同步失败不冻结、不阻断工作流；
- 火山侧只返回 token 与请求数，参考费用按登记模型的首档单价折算并明确标注。
"""
from __future__ import annotations

import json
import logging
from datetime import date, datetime, timedelta, timezone
from typing import Any, Callable, Mapping

from sqlalchemy import case, func, select
from sqlalchemy.orm import Session

from .api_datetime_encoding import platform_isoformat
from .audit import append_audit_event
from .doubao_endpoint_models import (
    DoubaoInferenceEndpoint,
    ModelCostReconciliation,
    VolcengineBillingSettings,
    VolcengineBillingSyncRun,
    VolcengineUsageWindow,
)
from .doubao_endpoints import billing_settings
from .model_pricing import (
    ModelPricingError,
    doubao_seed_2_0_lite_pricing,
    is_volcengine_ark_provider,
    parse_pricing_contract,
)
from .models import ModelRegistryEntry, WorkflowModelInvocation
from .security import SecretStorageError, protect_secret, unprotect_secret
from .volcengine_ark_openapi import (
    ArkControlPlaneClient,
    Transport,
    VolcengineOpenApiError,
)

logger = logging.getLogger(__name__)

BEIJING = timezone(timedelta(hours=8))
AUDIT_CATEGORY = "volcengine_billing"
ACCESS_KEY_ACCOUNT = "volcengine-billing-access-key"
SECRET_KEY_ACCOUNT = "volcengine-billing-secret-key"
TOKEN_TOLERANCE_MIN = 1_000
TOKEN_TOLERANCE_RATIO = 0.005
REQUEST_TOLERANCE_MIN = 2
REQUEST_TOLERANCE_RATIO = 0.01
MAX_ENDPOINTS_PER_QUERY = 50


class VolcengineBillingError(RuntimeError):
    def __init__(self, code: str, message: str, *, status_code: int = 409):
        super().__init__(message)
        self.code = code
        self.status_code = status_code


def _utc(value: datetime | None) -> datetime | None:
    if value is None:
        return None
    return value if value.tzinfo else value.replace(tzinfo=timezone.utc)


def beijing_date(moment: datetime) -> str:
    return _utc(moment).astimezone(BEIJING).date().isoformat()


def day_bounds_utc(business_date: str) -> tuple[datetime, datetime]:
    day = date.fromisoformat(business_date)
    start = datetime(day.year, day.month, day.day, tzinfo=BEIJING)
    return start.astimezone(timezone.utc), (start + timedelta(days=1)).astimezone(timezone.utc)


# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
# 设置
# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------

def _mask(reference: str | None, plain_prefix: str | None = None) -> str | None:
    if not reference:
        return None
    if plain_prefix:
        return plain_prefix[:4] + "****"
    return "已保存"


def settings_payload(row: VolcengineBillingSettings) -> dict[str, Any]:
    return {
        "sync_enabled": bool(row.sync_enabled),
        "endpoint_routing_enabled": bool(row.endpoint_routing_enabled),
        "has_access_key": bool(row.encrypted_access_key),
        "has_secret_key": bool(row.encrypted_secret_key),
        "credentials_ready": bool(row.encrypted_access_key and row.encrypted_secret_key),
        "region": row.region,
        "project_name": row.project_name,
        "sync_interval_seconds": int(row.sync_interval_seconds),
        "lookback_days": int(row.lookback_days),
        "last_sync_started_at": _utc(row.last_sync_started_at),
        "last_sync_succeeded_at": _utc(row.last_sync_succeeded_at),
        "last_sync_error": row.last_sync_error,
        "consecutive_failures": int(row.consecutive_failures),
        "revision": int(row.revision),
        "updated_by": row.updated_by,
        "updated_at": _utc(row.updated_at),
    }


def update_settings(
    db: Session,
    *,
    actor: str,
    expected_revision: int,
    sync_enabled: bool | None = None,
    endpoint_routing_enabled: bool | None = None,
    access_key: str | None = None,
    secret_key: str | None = None,
    region: str | None = None,
    project_name: str | None = None,
    sync_interval_seconds: int | None = None,
    lookback_days: int | None = None,
    reason: str = "",
) -> VolcengineBillingSettings:
    row = billing_settings(db)
    assert row is not None
    if row.revision != expected_revision:
        raise VolcengineBillingError("volcengine_billing_revision_conflict", "账单设置已被其他人修改，请刷新后重试")
    changes: dict[str, Any] = {}
    if sync_enabled is not None:
        changes["sync_enabled"] = bool(sync_enabled)
    if endpoint_routing_enabled is not None:
        changes["endpoint_routing_enabled"] = bool(endpoint_routing_enabled)
    if region is not None:
        if region != "cn-beijing":
            raise VolcengineBillingError("volcengine_region_unsupported", "目前只支持 cn-beijing 区域", status_code=422)
        changes["region"] = region
    if project_name is not None:
        changes["project_name"] = (project_name.strip() or "default")[:120]
    if sync_interval_seconds is not None:
        if sync_interval_seconds < 300 or sync_interval_seconds > 86_400:
            raise VolcengineBillingError("volcengine_interval_invalid", "同步间隔必须在 300 到 86400 秒之间", status_code=422)
        changes["sync_interval_seconds"] = int(sync_interval_seconds)
    if lookback_days is not None:
        if lookback_days < 1 or lookback_days > 7:
            raise VolcengineBillingError("volcengine_lookback_invalid", "回看天数必须在 1 到 7 之间", status_code=422)
        changes["lookback_days"] = int(lookback_days)
    rotated: list[str] = []
    try:
        if access_key is not None and access_key.strip():
            changes["encrypted_access_key"] = protect_secret(access_key.strip(), account=ACCESS_KEY_ACCOUNT)
            rotated.append("access_key")
        if secret_key is not None and secret_key.strip():
            changes["encrypted_secret_key"] = protect_secret(secret_key.strip(), account=SECRET_KEY_ACCOUNT)
            rotated.append("secret_key")
    except SecretStorageError as exc:
        raise VolcengineBillingError("volcengine_secret_storage_failed", "账单凭据无法安全保存", status_code=503) from exc
    if (changes.get("sync_enabled") or (sync_enabled is None and row.sync_enabled)) and not (
        (changes.get("encrypted_access_key") or row.encrypted_access_key)
        and (changes.get("encrypted_secret_key") or row.encrypted_secret_key)
    ):
        if sync_enabled:
            raise VolcengineBillingError("volcengine_credentials_missing", "开启账单同步前请先保存只读 AK/SK", status_code=422)
    for key, value in changes.items():
        setattr(row, key, value)
    row.revision += 1
    row.updated_by = actor
    db.flush()
    safe_changes = {key: value for key, value in changes.items() if not key.startswith("encrypted_")}
    append_audit_event(
        db,
        category=AUDIT_CATEGORY,
        action="settings_updated",
        subject_type="volcengine_billing_settings",
        subject_id=1,
        actor=actor,
        payload={"changes": safe_changes, "rotated": rotated, "reason": reason.strip(), "revision": row.revision},
        event_key=f"volcengine-billing:settings:{row.revision}",
    )
    return row


def _credentials(row: VolcengineBillingSettings) -> tuple[str, str]:
    if not row.encrypted_access_key or not row.encrypted_secret_key:
        raise VolcengineBillingError("volcengine_credentials_missing", "尚未配置火山账单只读 AK/SK", status_code=422)
    try:
        return (
            unprotect_secret(row.encrypted_access_key),
            unprotect_secret(row.encrypted_secret_key),
        )
    except SecretStorageError as exc:
        raise VolcengineBillingError("volcengine_credentials_unavailable", "火山账单凭据在当前环境不可解密", status_code=503) from exc


def build_client(
    row: VolcengineBillingSettings,
    *,
    transport: Transport | None = None,
    now: Callable[[], datetime] | None = None,
) -> ArkControlPlaneClient:
    access_key, secret_key = _credentials(row)
    return ArkControlPlaneClient(
        access_key=access_key,
        secret_key=secret_key,
        region=row.region or "cn-beijing",
        transport=transport,
        now=now or (lambda: datetime.now(timezone.utc)),
    )


# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
# 参考计价
# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------

def reference_pricing_for_endpoint(db: Session, endpoint: DoubaoInferenceEndpoint) -> dict[str, Any] | None:
    """找一份可用于折算火山 token 的计价合同：优先登记表里同模型的合同，其次官方预设。"""

    model_key = (endpoint.foundation_model or "").strip().lower()
    entries = db.scalars(
        select(ModelRegistryEntry).where(ModelRegistryEntry.active.is_(True)).order_by(ModelRegistryEntry.id.desc())
    ).all()
    for entry in entries:
        if not is_volcengine_ark_provider(entry.provider):
            continue
        entry_model = (entry.model_id or "").strip().lower()
        if model_key and not (entry_model.startswith(model_key) or model_key.startswith(entry_model)):
            continue
        try:
            contract = json.loads(entry.pricing_contract_json or "{}")
        except ValueError:
            continue
        if isinstance(contract, dict) and contract:
            try:
                return parse_pricing_contract(contract).to_dict()
            except ModelPricingError:
                continue
    if "seed-2-0-lite" in model_key or "seed-2.0-lite" in model_key:
        return doubao_seed_2_0_lite_pricing()
    return None


def reference_cost_micros(contract: Mapping[str, Any] | None, *, input_tokens: int, output_tokens: int) -> int | None:
    """按首档普通输入价与输出价折算（无缓存、不分档），只作参考上界。"""

    if not contract:
        return None
    tiers = contract.get("tiers") or []
    if not tiers:
        return None
    first = tiers[0]
    unit = int(contract.get("unit_tokens") or 1000)
    input_rate = int(first.get("input_micros_per_1k") or 0)
    output_rate = int(first.get("output_micros_per_1k") or 0)

    def component(tokens: int, rate: int) -> int:
        return (tokens * rate + unit - 1) // unit

    return component(input_tokens, input_rate) + component(output_tokens, output_rate)


# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
# 汇总
# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------

def local_day_aggregate(db: Session, endpoint_id: str, business_date: str) -> dict[str, int]:
    start, end = day_bounds_utc(business_date)
    scope = (
        WorkflowModelInvocation.endpoint_id == endpoint_id,
        WorkflowModelInvocation.created_at >= start,
        WorkflowModelInvocation.created_at < end,
    )
    # 调用次数：排除未发出（rejected）与供应商明确 429 拒绝（确认未计费）的行。
    # 费用：所有已结算的行（succeeded 与带实际用量结算的 failed）都计入，与 token 口径一致；
    # 未结算的 failed 行 actual_cost 为 0，reserved 行尚未结算不计。
    billed_call = (WorkflowModelInvocation.status != "rejected") & (
        WorkflowModelInvocation.upstream_status_code.is_(None)
        | (WorkflowModelInvocation.upstream_status_code != 429)
    )
    calls, input_tokens, output_tokens, cached, cost, unresolved = db.execute(
        select(
            func.count(case((billed_call, WorkflowModelInvocation.id))),
            func.coalesce(func.sum(WorkflowModelInvocation.input_tokens), 0),
            func.coalesce(func.sum(WorkflowModelInvocation.output_tokens), 0),
            func.coalesce(func.sum(WorkflowModelInvocation.cached_input_tokens), 0),
            func.coalesce(
                func.sum(case((WorkflowModelInvocation.status.in_(("succeeded", "failed")),
                               WorkflowModelInvocation.actual_cost_micros), else_=0)),
                0,
            ),
            func.count(case(((WorkflowModelInvocation.status == "reserved")
                             | (WorkflowModelInvocation.error_code == "model_invocation_outcome_unknown"),
                             WorkflowModelInvocation.id))),
        ).where(*scope)
    ).one()
    return {
        "calls": int(calls or 0),
        "input_tokens": int(input_tokens or 0),
        "output_tokens": int(output_tokens or 0),
        "cached_input_tokens": int(cached or 0),
        "cost_micros": int(cost or 0),
        "unresolved_calls": int(unresolved or 0),
    }


def remote_day_aggregate(db: Session, endpoint_id: str, business_date: str) -> dict[str, Any]:
    rows, requests, input_tokens, output_tokens, total_tokens, last_fetched = db.execute(
        select(
            func.count(VolcengineUsageWindow.id),
            func.coalesce(func.sum(VolcengineUsageWindow.request_count), 0),
            func.coalesce(func.sum(VolcengineUsageWindow.input_tokens), 0),
            func.coalesce(func.sum(VolcengineUsageWindow.output_tokens), 0),
            func.coalesce(func.sum(VolcengineUsageWindow.total_tokens), 0),
            func.max(VolcengineUsageWindow.fetched_at),
        ).where(
            VolcengineUsageWindow.endpoint_id == endpoint_id,
            VolcengineUsageWindow.usage_date == business_date,
        )
    ).one()
    return {
        "rows": int(rows or 0),
        "requests": int(requests or 0),
        "input_tokens": int(input_tokens or 0),
        "output_tokens": int(output_tokens or 0),
        "total_tokens": int(total_tokens or 0),
        "last_fetched_at": _utc(last_fetched),
    }


def _match_status(local: Mapping[str, int], remote: Mapping[str, Any]) -> tuple[str, dict[str, Any]]:
    local_total = int(local["input_tokens"]) + int(local["output_tokens"])
    remote_total = int(remote["input_tokens"]) + int(remote["output_tokens"])
    token_diff = local_total - remote_total
    request_diff = int(local["calls"]) - int(remote["requests"])
    token_tolerance = max(TOKEN_TOLERANCE_MIN, int(remote_total * TOKEN_TOLERANCE_RATIO))
    request_tolerance = max(REQUEST_TOLERANCE_MIN, int(int(remote["requests"]) * REQUEST_TOLERANCE_RATIO))
    difference = {
        "token_diff": token_diff,
        "token_tolerance": token_tolerance,
        "input_token_diff": int(local["input_tokens"]) - int(remote["input_tokens"]),
        "output_token_diff": int(local["output_tokens"]) - int(remote["output_tokens"]),
        "request_diff": request_diff,
        "request_tolerance": request_tolerance,
        "remote_rows": int(remote["rows"]),
    }
    if int(remote["rows"]) == 0 and int(local["calls"]) > 0:
        return "remote_missing", difference
    if int(local["calls"]) == 0 and int(remote["requests"]) > 0:
        return "local_missing", difference
    if abs(token_diff) <= token_tolerance and abs(request_diff) <= request_tolerance:
        return "matched", difference
    return "mismatch", difference


def _endpoint_ids_in_scope(db: Session, dates: list[str]) -> list[tuple[str, DoubaoInferenceEndpoint | None]]:
    registered = {row.endpoint_id: row for row in db.scalars(select(DoubaoInferenceEndpoint)).all()}
    seen: dict[str, DoubaoInferenceEndpoint | None] = dict(registered)
    if dates:
        start, _ = day_bounds_utc(dates[0])
        _, end = day_bounds_utc(dates[-1])
        for (endpoint_id,) in db.execute(
            select(WorkflowModelInvocation.endpoint_id)
            .where(
                WorkflowModelInvocation.endpoint_id.is_not(None),
                WorkflowModelInvocation.created_at >= start,
                WorkflowModelInvocation.created_at < end,
            )
            .distinct()
        ):
            if endpoint_id and endpoint_id not in seen:
                seen[endpoint_id] = None
    return sorted(seen.items(), key=lambda item: item[0])


def _dates_in_window(now: datetime, lookback_days: int) -> list[str]:
    today = _utc(now).astimezone(BEIJING).date()
    return [(today - timedelta(days=offset)).isoformat() for offset in range(lookback_days, -1, -1)]


def reconcile_days(
    db: Session,
    *,
    now: datetime,
    lookback_days: int,
    freeze: bool,
) -> dict[str, int]:
    """刷新窗口内每个接入点 × 业务日的对账行；``freeze`` 为真时冻结已结束的业务日。"""

    current = _utc(now)
    today = beijing_date(current)
    dates = _dates_in_window(current, lookback_days)
    summary = {"refreshed": 0, "frozen": 0, "skipped_frozen": 0}
    contracts: dict[str, dict[str, Any] | None] = {}
    for endpoint_id, endpoint in _endpoint_ids_in_scope(db, dates):
        for business_date in dates:
            row = db.scalar(
                select(ModelCostReconciliation).where(
                    ModelCostReconciliation.endpoint_id == endpoint_id,
                    ModelCostReconciliation.business_date == business_date,
                )
            )
            if row is not None and row.status == "frozen":
                summary["skipped_frozen"] += 1
                continue
            local = local_day_aggregate(db, endpoint_id, business_date)
            remote = remote_day_aggregate(db, endpoint_id, business_date)
            if row is None and local["calls"] == 0 and remote["rows"] == 0:
                continue
            if row is None:
                row = ModelCostReconciliation(endpoint_id=endpoint_id, business_date=business_date)
                db.add(row)
            if endpoint_id not in contracts:
                contracts[endpoint_id] = reference_pricing_for_endpoint(db, endpoint) if endpoint else None
            match_status, difference = _match_status(local, remote)
            row.local_calls = local["calls"]
            row.local_input_tokens = local["input_tokens"]
            row.local_output_tokens = local["output_tokens"]
            row.local_cached_input_tokens = local["cached_input_tokens"]
            row.local_cost_micros = local["cost_micros"]
            row.local_unresolved_calls = local["unresolved_calls"]
            row.remote_requests = remote["requests"]
            row.remote_input_tokens = remote["input_tokens"]
            row.remote_output_tokens = remote["output_tokens"]
            row.remote_total_tokens = remote["total_tokens"]
            row.remote_reference_cost_micros = reference_cost_micros(
                contracts[endpoint_id],
                input_tokens=remote["input_tokens"],
                output_tokens=remote["output_tokens"],
            )
            row.match_status = match_status if remote["rows"] or local["calls"] else "pending"
            row.difference_json = json.dumps(
                {**difference, "reference_cost_basis": "first_tier_no_cache" if contracts[endpoint_id] else None},
                ensure_ascii=False,
                sort_keys=True,
            )
            row.last_synced_at = remote["last_fetched_at"]
            summary["refreshed"] += 1
            if freeze and business_date < today:
                row.status = "frozen"
                row.frozen_at = current
                summary["frozen"] += 1
    db.flush()
    return summary


# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
# 同步
# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------

def _upsert_usage_rows(db: Session, rows: list[Any], *, sync_run_id: int, fetched_at: datetime) -> int:
    count = 0
    for item in rows:
        existing = db.scalar(
            select(VolcengineUsageWindow).where(
                VolcengineUsageWindow.endpoint_id == item.endpoint_id,
                VolcengineUsageWindow.usage_date == item.usage_date,
                VolcengineUsageWindow.usage_hour == item.usage_hour,
            )
        )
        if existing is None:
            existing = VolcengineUsageWindow(
                endpoint_id=item.endpoint_id,
                usage_date=item.usage_date,
                usage_hour=item.usage_hour,
            )
            db.add(existing)
        existing.input_tokens = item.input_tokens
        existing.output_tokens = item.output_tokens
        existing.total_tokens = item.total_tokens
        existing.image_count = item.image_count
        existing.request_count = item.request_count
        existing.raw_json = json.dumps(item.raw, ensure_ascii=False, sort_keys=True)
        existing.sync_run_id = sync_run_id
        existing.fetched_at = fetched_at
        count += 1
    db.flush()
    return count


def run_usage_sync(
    db: Session,
    *,
    actor: str,
    trigger: str = "scheduled",
    now: datetime | None = None,
    transport: Transport | None = None,
) -> VolcengineBillingSyncRun:
    """执行一次拉取 + 对账。失败时记录到设置与同步记录上，不抛出。"""

    current = _utc(now or datetime.now(timezone.utc))
    settings = billing_settings(db)
    assert settings is not None
    run = VolcengineBillingSyncRun(trigger=trigger, actor=actor, status="running", started_at=current)
    db.add(run)
    settings.last_sync_started_at = current
    db.flush()
    dates = _dates_in_window(current, int(settings.lookback_days or 2))
    run.window_start_date = dates[0]
    run.window_end_date = dates[-1]
    endpoints = [row.endpoint_id for row in db.scalars(select(DoubaoInferenceEndpoint)).all()]
    run.endpoint_count = len(endpoints)
    try:
        client = build_client(settings, transport=transport, now=lambda: current)
        query_end = (date.fromisoformat(dates[-1]) + timedelta(days=1)).isoformat()
        upserted = 0
        batches = [endpoints[index:index + MAX_ENDPOINTS_PER_QUERY] for index in range(0, len(endpoints), MAX_ENDPOINTS_PER_QUERY)] or [[]]
        for batch in batches:
            rows = client.get_inference_usage(
                start_date=dates[0],
                end_date=query_end,
                endpoint_ids=batch,
                project_name=settings.project_name or "default",
            )
            upserted += _upsert_usage_rows(db, rows, sync_run_id=run.id, fetched_at=current)
        run.rows_upserted = upserted
        reconciled = reconcile_days(db, now=current, lookback_days=int(settings.lookback_days or 2), freeze=True)
        run.reconciliations_frozen = reconciled["frozen"]
        run.status = "succeeded"
        run.finished_at = current
        settings.last_sync_succeeded_at = current
        settings.last_sync_error = ""
        settings.consecutive_failures = 0
    except (VolcengineOpenApiError, VolcengineBillingError) as exc:
        run.status = "failed"
        run.error_code = exc.code[:80]
        run.error_message = str(exc)
        run.finished_at = current
        settings.last_sync_error = f"{exc.code}: {exc}"[:2000]
        settings.consecutive_failures = int(settings.consecutive_failures or 0) + 1
        logger.warning("火山账单同步失败：%s %s", exc.code, exc)
        # 同步失败：不冻结，但仍刷新本地视角，保证看板实时值可用。
        try:
            reconcile_days(db, now=current, lookback_days=int(settings.lookback_days or 2), freeze=False)
        except Exception:  # pragma: no cover - 刷新失败不应掩盖原始错误
            logger.exception("同步失败后刷新本地对账视角失败")
    db.flush()
    return run


def sync_due_usage_windows(
    db: Session,
    *,
    actor: str,
    now: datetime | None = None,
    transport: Transport | None = None,
) -> dict[str, Any]:
    """Worker 辅助 tick 入口：只在开关打开且间隔到期时拉取。"""

    current = _utc(now or datetime.now(timezone.utc))
    settings = db.get(VolcengineBillingSettings, 1)
    if settings is None or not settings.sync_enabled:
        return {"ran": False, "reason": "disabled"}
    last = _utc(settings.last_sync_started_at)
    interval = timedelta(seconds=int(settings.sync_interval_seconds or 3600))
    if last is not None and last + interval > current:
        return {"ran": False, "reason": "not_due", "next_due_at": last + interval}
    run = run_usage_sync(db, actor=actor, trigger="scheduled", now=current, transport=transport)
    return {
        "ran": True,
        "status": run.status,
        "rows_upserted": run.rows_upserted,
        "frozen": run.reconciliations_frozen,
        "error_code": run.error_code,
    }


# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
# 查询与调整
# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------

def reconciliation_payload(row: ModelCostReconciliation) -> dict[str, Any]:
    try:
        difference = json.loads(row.difference_json or "{}")
    except ValueError:
        difference = {}
    try:
        adjustments = json.loads(row.adjustments_json or "[]")
    except ValueError:
        adjustments = []
    return {
        "id": row.id,
        "endpoint_id": row.endpoint_id,
        "business_date": row.business_date,
        "status": row.status,
        "match_status": row.match_status,
        "local": {
            "calls": row.local_calls,
            "input_tokens": row.local_input_tokens,
            "output_tokens": row.local_output_tokens,
            "cached_input_tokens": row.local_cached_input_tokens,
            "cost_micros": row.local_cost_micros,
            "unresolved_calls": row.local_unresolved_calls,
        },
        "remote": {
            "requests": row.remote_requests,
            "input_tokens": row.remote_input_tokens,
            "output_tokens": row.remote_output_tokens,
            "total_tokens": row.remote_total_tokens,
            "reference_cost_micros": row.remote_reference_cost_micros,
        },
        "difference": difference if isinstance(difference, dict) else {},
        "adjustments": adjustments if isinstance(adjustments, list) else [],
        "last_synced_at": _utc(row.last_synced_at),
        "frozen_at": _utc(row.frozen_at),
        "updated_at": _utc(row.updated_at),
    }


def list_reconciliations(
    db: Session,
    *,
    endpoint_id: str | None = None,
    date_from: str | None = None,
    date_to: str | None = None,
    limit: int = 200,
) -> list[dict[str, Any]]:
    query = select(ModelCostReconciliation)
    if endpoint_id:
        query = query.where(ModelCostReconciliation.endpoint_id == endpoint_id)
    if date_from:
        query = query.where(ModelCostReconciliation.business_date >= date_from)
    if date_to:
        query = query.where(ModelCostReconciliation.business_date <= date_to)
    query = query.order_by(ModelCostReconciliation.business_date.desc(), ModelCostReconciliation.endpoint_id).limit(max(1, min(limit, 1000)))
    return [reconciliation_payload(row) for row in db.scalars(query).all()]


def add_adjustment(
    db: Session,
    reconciliation_id: int,
    *,
    actor: str,
    note: str,
    now: datetime | None = None,
) -> ModelCostReconciliation:
    row = db.get(ModelCostReconciliation, reconciliation_id)
    if row is None:
        raise VolcengineBillingError("volcengine_reconciliation_missing", "对账记录不存在", status_code=404)
    text = (note or "").strip()
    if not text:
        raise VolcengineBillingError("volcengine_adjustment_note_required", "调整说明不能为空", status_code=422)
    current = _utc(now or datetime.now(timezone.utc))
    try:
        adjustments = json.loads(row.adjustments_json or "[]")
    except ValueError:
        adjustments = []
    if not isinstance(adjustments, list):
        adjustments = []
    entry = {"note": text[:2000], "actor": actor, "recorded_at": platform_isoformat(current), "status_at_time": row.status, "match_status_at_time": row.match_status}
    adjustments.append(entry)
    row.adjustments_json = json.dumps(adjustments, ensure_ascii=False)
    db.flush()
    append_audit_event(
        db,
        category=AUDIT_CATEGORY,
        action="reconciliation_adjusted",
        subject_type="model_cost_reconciliation",
        subject_id=row.id,
        actor=actor,
        payload={"endpoint_id": row.endpoint_id, "business_date": row.business_date, **entry},
    )
    return row


def billing_dashboard(db: Session, *, now: datetime | None = None) -> dict[str, Any]:
    current = _utc(now or datetime.now(timezone.utc))
    today = beijing_date(current)
    yesterday = (date.fromisoformat(today) - timedelta(days=1)).isoformat()
    settings = billing_settings(db, create=False)
    last_run = db.scalar(select(VolcengineBillingSyncRun).order_by(VolcengineBillingSyncRun.id.desc()).limit(1))
    endpoints = db.scalars(select(DoubaoInferenceEndpoint).order_by(DoubaoInferenceEndpoint.category_key, DoubaoInferenceEndpoint.id)).all()
    items = []
    for endpoint in endpoints:
        today_local = local_day_aggregate(db, endpoint.endpoint_id, today)
        today_remote = remote_day_aggregate(db, endpoint.endpoint_id, today)
        yesterday_row = db.scalar(
            select(ModelCostReconciliation).where(
                ModelCostReconciliation.endpoint_id == endpoint.endpoint_id,
                ModelCostReconciliation.business_date == yesterday,
            )
        )
        items.append(
            {
                "endpoint_id": endpoint.endpoint_id,
                "display_name": endpoint.display_name,
                "category_key": endpoint.category_key,
                "workflow_definition_id": endpoint.workflow_definition_id,
                "status": endpoint.status,
                "verification_status": endpoint.verification_status,
                "today": {
                    "business_date": today,
                    "local": today_local,
                    "remote": {key: value for key, value in today_remote.items() if key != "last_fetched_at"},
                    "remote_last_fetched_at": today_remote["last_fetched_at"],
                    "cache_hit_ratio": (
                        round(today_local["cached_input_tokens"] / today_local["input_tokens"], 4)
                        if today_local["input_tokens"] else None
                    ),
                },
                "yesterday": reconciliation_payload(yesterday_row) if yesterday_row else None,
            }
        )
    return {
        "generated_at": current,
        "business_today": today,
        "settings": settings_payload(settings) if settings else None,
        "last_sync": None if last_run is None else {
            "id": last_run.id,
            "trigger": last_run.trigger,
            "status": last_run.status,
            "started_at": _utc(last_run.started_at),
            "finished_at": _utc(last_run.finished_at),
            "rows_upserted": last_run.rows_upserted,
            "reconciliations_frozen": last_run.reconciliations_frozen,
            "error_code": last_run.error_code,
            "error_message": last_run.error_message,
        },
        "endpoints": items,
        "notes": [
            "本地值来自模型调用账本的逐次结算；火山值来自 GetInferenceUsage 的按小时用量。",
            "火山参考费用按登记模型首档普通单价折算，不含缓存折扣与账单优惠，仅用于对账参考。",
            "对账行在业务日（北京时间）结束后的下一次成功同步时冻结；同步失败停止冻结并告警。",
        ],
    }


def billing_health(db: Session, *, now: datetime | None = None) -> dict[str, Any]:
    """供运营指标与告警使用的紧凑健康视图。"""

    current = _utc(now or datetime.now(timezone.utc))
    settings = db.get(VolcengineBillingSettings, 1)
    today = beijing_date(current)
    week_ago = (date.fromisoformat(today) - timedelta(days=7)).isoformat()
    mismatched = int(
        db.scalar(
            select(func.count(ModelCostReconciliation.id)).where(
                ModelCostReconciliation.status == "frozen",
                ModelCostReconciliation.match_status.in_(["mismatch", "remote_missing", "local_missing"]),
                ModelCostReconciliation.business_date >= week_ago,
            )
        )
        or 0
    )
    overdue_open = int(
        db.scalar(
            select(func.count(ModelCostReconciliation.id)).where(
                ModelCostReconciliation.status == "open",
                ModelCostReconciliation.business_date < today,
            )
        )
        or 0
    )
    last_success = _utc(settings.last_sync_succeeded_at) if settings else None
    return {
        "configured": bool(settings and settings.encrypted_access_key and settings.encrypted_secret_key),
        "sync_enabled": bool(settings and settings.sync_enabled),
        "endpoint_routing_enabled": bool(settings and settings.endpoint_routing_enabled),
        "sync_interval_seconds": int(settings.sync_interval_seconds) if settings else None,
        "last_sync_succeeded_at": platform_isoformat(last_success) if last_success else None,
        "last_success_age_seconds": int((current - last_success).total_seconds()) if last_success else None,
        "consecutive_failures": int(settings.consecutive_failures) if settings else 0,
        "last_error": (settings.last_sync_error if settings else "") or "",
        "frozen_mismatch_days_7d": mismatched,
        "overdue_open_days": overdue_open,
    }

===== FILE backend/app/wide_material.py =====
"""面向下游的素材大宽表投影（ADR-0111）。

单一真相来源是各内部表；这里只做「从源表重算整行」：

* ``build_material_row``：按 asset_id 把 evaluation_results（最新一条）、production_runs、
  assets/asset_versions/content_records、baseline_truth_revisions（回退 baseline_set_items）、
  downstream_publish_ledger（按 origin_run_key 取最新 revision）、correction_history、
  manual_failure_resolutions 与灵感图身份（下游节点 output.inspiration_task）拼成一行。
* ``refresh_material``：写/更新 current；row_hash（排除 updated_at/recorded_at）与 history
  最后一行不同才追加 history。不 commit；任何异常只记日志并返回 False，绝不让主流程失败。
* ``reconcile``：重算并比对，修正不一致。

写入点钩子（评测收尾、纠偏、人工结论、真值修订、投递结果、运行创建/终态）都在各自的
事务里、原有写库之后调用 ``refresh_material``；每日对账兜底。库内时间全部 UTC naive。
"""

from __future__ import annotations

import hashlib
import struct
import json
import logging
from collections.abc import Iterable, Iterator, Mapping
from contextlib import contextmanager
from datetime import datetime, timezone
from typing import Any

from sqlalchemy import select
from sqlalchemy.exc import IntegrityError
from sqlalchemy.orm import Session

from .wide_material_models import (
    EVENT_KINDS,
    ROW_COLUMNS,
    WIDE_SCHEMA_VERSION,
    WideMaterialCurrent,
    WideMaterialHistory,
)

logger = logging.getLogger(__name__)

# 比较后写入的重试次数：构建期间 current 被别的事务刷新过就重读重算（Astra 全仓复审 F4）。
CAS_ATTEMPTS = 3


class WideMaterialConflict(RuntimeError):
    """写 current 时它已不是构建前读到的样子：另一事务在本次构建期间刷新过这条素材。"""

#: 等级 → 档。ADR：L1-L2 recommend、L3-L4 regular、L5 filter。
TIER_BY_LEVEL = {
    "L1": "recommend",
    "L2": "recommend",
    "L3": "regular",
    "L4": "regular",
    "L5": "filter",
}

#: 维度键 → 列名（3DSU 五维 + 灵感图八维，键名不同，均可空）。
DIMENSION_COLUMNS = {
    "model_detail": "dim_model_detail",
    "material_rendering": "dim_material_rendering",
    "lighting": "dim_lighting",
    "design_trend": "dim_design_trend",
    "visual_composition": "dim_visual_composition",
    "composition_viewpoint": "dim_composition_viewpoint",
    "lighting_atmosphere": "dim_lighting_atmosphere",
    "color_material": "dim_color_material",
    "spatial_design_furnishing": "dim_spatial_design_furnishing",
    "visual_hierarchy": "dim_visual_hierarchy",
    "detail_completion": "dim_detail_completion",
    "inspiration_reference": "dim_inspiration_reference",
    "presentation_integrity": "dim_presentation_integrity",
}

#: row_hash 不含的列：每次重算都会变，不代表结论变化。
HASH_EXCLUDED = frozenset({"updated_at", "recorded_at"})


# --------------------------------------------------------------------------- #
# 小工具
# --------------------------------------------------------------------------- #


def _json_object(text: Any) -> dict[str, Any]:
    if isinstance(text, Mapping):
        return dict(text)
    if not isinstance(text, str) or not text.strip():
        return {}
    try:
        parsed = json.loads(text)
    except (TypeError, ValueError):
        return {}
    return dict(parsed) if isinstance(parsed, Mapping) else {}


def _json_list(text: Any) -> list[Any]:
    if isinstance(text, list):
        return list(text)
    if not isinstance(text, str) or not text.strip():
        return []
    try:
        parsed = json.loads(text)
    except (TypeError, ValueError):
        return []
    return list(parsed) if isinstance(parsed, list) else []


def _utc_naive(value: Any) -> datetime | None:
    """库内一律 UTC naive；带时区的换算到 UTC 后去掉 tzinfo，字符串按 ISO 解析。"""

    if value is None:
        return None
    if isinstance(value, str):
        text = value.strip()
        if not text:
            return None
        try:
            value = datetime.fromisoformat(text.replace("Z", "+00:00"))
        except ValueError:
            return None
    if not isinstance(value, datetime):
        return None
    if value.tzinfo is not None:
        value = value.astimezone(timezone.utc).replace(tzinfo=None)
    return value.replace(microsecond=value.microsecond)


def _now_utc() -> datetime:
    return datetime.now(timezone.utc).replace(tzinfo=None)


def _number(value: Any) -> float | None:
    if isinstance(value, bool) or not isinstance(value, (int, float)):
        return None
    return float(value)


def _positive_int(value: Any) -> int | None:
    if isinstance(value, bool) or not isinstance(value, int):
        return None
    return value if value > 0 else None


def _text(value: Any, limit: int | None = None) -> str | None:
    if value is None:
        return None
    if isinstance(value, str):
        text = value
    elif isinstance(value, (list, tuple)):
        text = "；".join(str(item) for item in value if isinstance(item, str) and item.strip())
    else:
        text = json.dumps(value, ensure_ascii=False, sort_keys=True)
    if limit is not None and len(text) > limit:
        text = text[:limit]
    return text


def _json_text(value: Any) -> str | None:
    if value is None:
        return None
    return json.dumps(value, ensure_ascii=False, sort_keys=True)


def _canonical(value: Any) -> str:
    return json.dumps(value, ensure_ascii=False, sort_keys=True, separators=(",", ":"), default=str)


def compute_row_hash(row: Mapping[str, Any]) -> str:
    """结论指纹：排除 updated_at/recorded_at；时间按 ISO 字符串参与。"""

    payload = {
        key: _hash_value(value)
        for key, value in row.items()
        if key not in HASH_EXCLUDED
    }
    return hashlib.sha256(_canonical(payload).encode("utf-8")).hexdigest()


def _hash_value(value: Any) -> Any:
    """指纹用值：时间转 ISO；浮点取 6 位有效数字（MySQL FLOAT 列只保留单精度，
    0.01721047 存回读出是 0.0172105，不归一每次对账都会误判不一致）。"""

    if isinstance(value, datetime):
        return value.isoformat()
    if isinstance(value, float):
        # 先按单精度截断再取 6 位有效数字，和 MySQL 对 FLOAT 列的文本化一致
        # （0.9116735009 直接取 6 位是 0.911674，库里回读却是 0.911673）。
        single = struct.unpack("f", struct.pack("f", value))[0]
        return float(f"{single:.6g}")
    return value


# --------------------------------------------------------------------------- #
# 源表读取
# --------------------------------------------------------------------------- #


#: 运行路径优先级：正式增量 > 兼容/旧路径（run_mode 非 stock_regression，含 NULL）> 存量回归。
#: 黄金集素材同时有正式增量结果和实验回归结果（候选版本跑 run 488/490 之类），
#: 单纯按 id 取最新会让实验结果覆盖正式结论，下游拿到错等级。
def _run_mode_rank(run_mode: Any) -> int:
    if run_mode == "incremental":
        return 0
    if run_mode == "stock_regression":
        return 2
    return 1


def _latest_result(db: Session, asset_id: int):
    """按路径优先级取当前生效结果：增量 → 非存量回归（含无 run_mode）→ 存量回归；同级按 id 最新。"""

    from .models import EvaluationResult, ProductionRun

    rows = db.execute(
        select(EvaluationResult, ProductionRun.run_mode)
        .outerjoin(ProductionRun, ProductionRun.id == EvaluationResult.production_run_id)
        .where(EvaluationResult.asset_id == asset_id)
        .order_by(EvaluationResult.id.desc())
    ).all()
    if not rows:
        return None
    return min(rows, key=lambda row: (_run_mode_rank(row[1]), -int(row[0].id)))[0]


def _runs_for_asset(db: Session, asset_id: int, *, run_hint: Any = None) -> list[Any]:
    """一条素材关联过的运行：结果行/兼容任务绑定的、接入事件派生的、存量回归项绑定的。

    ``run_hint`` 是钩子手里的那条运行（失败的运行没有结果行，接入事件又可能还没挂上
    content_records.asset_id），一并算进候选。
    """

    from .models import (
        BaselineRegressionItem,
        ContentIngressEvent,
        ContentRecord,
        EvaluationJob,
        EvaluationResult,
        ProductionRun,
    )

    run_ids: set[int] = set()
    if run_hint is not None and getattr(run_hint, "id", None) is not None:
        run_ids.add(int(run_hint.id))
    for value in db.scalars(
        select(EvaluationJob.production_run_id).where(
            EvaluationJob.asset_id == asset_id,
