            EvaluationJob.production_run_id.is_not(None),
        )
    ):
        run_ids.add(int(value))
    for value in db.scalars(
        select(EvaluationResult.production_run_id).where(
            EvaluationResult.asset_id == asset_id,
            EvaluationResult.production_run_id.is_not(None),
        )
    ):
        run_ids.add(int(value))
    for value in db.scalars(
        select(BaselineRegressionItem.production_run_id).where(
            BaselineRegressionItem.asset_id == asset_id,
            BaselineRegressionItem.production_run_id.is_not(None),
        )
    ):
        run_ids.add(int(value))
    record_ids = [
        int(value)
        for value in db.scalars(
            select(ContentRecord.id).where(ContentRecord.asset_id == asset_id)
        )
    ]
    if record_ids:
        # ContentRecord.asset_id 会随接入改指新素材（新版本，或同一版本重新接入新字节），记录的全部接入事件
        # 不都属于当前素材（Astra 全仓复审 F3、复审 R-F3）。逐条运行按 _ingress_run_asset_id 的同一优先级
        # 判断归属：事件冻结的素材绑定 → 运行冻结的 input_manifest → （两者都没有）当前版本兜底。
        events = {
            int(event.id): event
            for event in db.scalars(
                select(ContentIngressEvent).where(
                    ContentIngressEvent.content_record_id.in_(record_ids)
                )
            )
        }
        if events:
            for run_id, source_id in db.execute(
                select(ProductionRun.id, ProductionRun.source_id).where(
                    ProductionRun.source_type == "content_ingress_event",
                    ProductionRun.source_id.in_(list(events)),
                )
            ):
                if int(run_id) in run_ids:
                    continue
                event = events[int(source_id)]
                owner = _event_bound_asset_id(event)  # 快路径：不读运行大快照
                if owner is None:
                    run = db.get(ProductionRun, int(run_id))
                    try:
                        owner = _ingress_run_asset_id(db, event, run) if run is not None else None
                    finally:
                        if run is not None:
                            db.expunge(run)
                if owner == int(asset_id):
                    run_ids.add(int(run_id))
    for value in db.scalars(
        select(ProductionRun.id).where(
            ProductionRun.source_type.in_(["native_evaluation", "asset"]),
            ProductionRun.source_id == asset_id,
        )
    ):
        run_ids.add(int(value))
    if not run_ids:
        return []
    runs = list(
        db.scalars(
            select(ProductionRun)
            .where(ProductionRun.id.in_(sorted(run_ids)))
            .order_by(ProductionRun.id.desc())
        )
    )
    # 与 _latest_result 同一优先级：实验回归运行不能成为 run_state 的「当前运行」。
    runs.sort(key=lambda run: (_run_mode_rank(run.run_mode), -int(run.id)))
    return runs


def resolve_run_asset_id(db: Session, run: Any) -> int | None:
    """从一条运行找到它评的素材。

    接入事件派生的运行与候选发现、运行关联共用 _ingress_run_asset_id 的优先级（复审 R-F3）；
    其余来源：冻结清单 asset_id → source_type 直指 → 存量回归项。
    """

    from .models import Asset, BaselineRegressionItem, ContentIngressEvent

    if run.source_type == "content_ingress_event" and run.source_id is not None:
        event = db.get(ContentIngressEvent, int(run.source_id))
        if event is not None:
            asset_id = _ingress_run_asset_id(db, event, run)
            return asset_id if asset_id is not None and db.get(Asset, asset_id) is not None else None
    candidates: list[Any] = [_frozen_asset_id(run)]
    if run.source_type in {"native_evaluation", "asset"}:
        candidates.append(run.source_id)
    for candidate in candidates:
        asset_id = _positive_int(candidate)
        if asset_id is not None and db.get(Asset, asset_id) is not None:
            return asset_id
    if run.source_type == "baseline_regression_item" and run.source_id is not None:
        item = db.get(BaselineRegressionItem, int(run.source_id))
        if item is not None:
            return int(item.asset_id)
    return None


def _frozen_asset_id(run: Any) -> int | None:
    """运行创建时冻结在 input_manifest 里的素材 id；读不到快照或没有该字段时返回 None。"""

    try:
        from .workflow_snapshot_store import read_run_snapshot_text

        snapshot = _json_object(read_run_snapshot_text(run))
    except Exception:  # noqa: BLE001 - 快照读不出来就当定不下来
        return None
    runtime_context = snapshot.get("runtime_context")
    frozen = runtime_context.get("input_manifest") if isinstance(runtime_context, Mapping) else None
    return _positive_int(frozen.get("asset_id")) if isinstance(frozen, Mapping) else None


def _event_bound_asset_id(event: Any) -> int | None:
    """接入时冻结在事件身份快照里的 ``asset_binding.asset_id``（不可变、轻量）；接入时素材未到则没有。"""

    binding = _json_object(getattr(event, "identity_snapshot_json", None)).get("asset_binding")
    return _positive_int(binding.get("asset_id")) if isinstance(binding, Mapping) else None


def _ingress_run_asset_id(db: Session, event: Any, run: Any) -> int | None:
    """接入事件派生的运行评的是哪张素材：运行关联、钩子与候选发现共用同一优先级（Astra 全仓复审 F3、复审 R-F3）。

    1. 事件接入时冻结的 ``asset_binding``；
    2. 没有绑定（接入时素材未到）：运行创建时冻结的 ``input_manifest.asset_id``；
    3. 两者都没有（没有冻结素材的历史运行）：只有事件仍是记录当前版本，才用记录当前指向的素材。

    ContentRecord.asset_id 会随接入改指，同一版本重新接入新字节也会改指；"版本相等"证明不了归属，
    所以只在没有任何冻结依据时兜底。
    """

    from .models import ContentRecord

    bound = _event_bound_asset_id(event)
    if bound is not None:
        return bound
    frozen = _frozen_asset_id(run) if run is not None else None
    if frozen is not None:
        return frozen
    if event.content_record_id is None:
        return None
    record = db.get(ContentRecord, int(event.content_record_id))
    if record is None or record.asset_id is None:
        return None
    if _json_object(event.payload_json).get("content_version") != record.source_version:
        return None
    return int(record.asset_id)


def _run_snapshot(run: Any) -> dict[str, Any]:
    try:
        from .workflow_snapshot_store import read_run_snapshot_text

        return _json_object(read_run_snapshot_text(run))
    except Exception:  # noqa: BLE001
        return {}


def _frozen_input(snapshot: Mapping[str, Any]) -> dict[str, Any]:
    runtime_context = snapshot.get("runtime_context")
    frozen = runtime_context.get("input_manifest") if isinstance(runtime_context, Mapping) else None
    return dict(frozen) if isinstance(frozen, Mapping) else {}


def _downstream_output(node_outputs: Mapping[str, Any]) -> dict[str, Any]:
    """下游节点的 output（factory-result-v1 / inspiration-factory-result-v1）。"""

    for node_result in node_outputs.values():
        if not isinstance(node_result, Mapping):
            continue
        output = node_result.get("output")
        if not isinstance(output, Mapping):
            continue
        schema = output.get("schema_version")
        if node_result.get("node_kind") == "downstream_projection" or (
            isinstance(schema, str) and schema.endswith("factory-result-v1")
        ):
            return dict(output)
    return {}


def _prompt_outputs(node_outputs: Mapping[str, Any]) -> list[dict[str, Any]]:
    outputs: list[dict[str, Any]] = []
    for node_result in node_outputs.values():
        if not isinstance(node_result, Mapping):
            continue
        output = node_result.get("output")
        if isinstance(output, Mapping) and output:
            outputs.append(dict(output))
    return outputs


def _production_fields(
    downstream: Mapping[str, Any],
    prompt_outputs: Iterable[Mapping[str, Any]],
    precheck: Mapping[str, Any],
) -> dict[str, Any]:
    """业务字段：下游投出去的那份优先（纠偏后与投递一致），其次调用 A 输出。"""

    stage_a = downstream.get("prompt_a_fields")
    if isinstance(stage_a, Mapping) and isinstance(stage_a.get("production_fields"), Mapping):
        return dict(stage_a["production_fields"])
    a_fields = downstream.get("a_fields")
    if isinstance(a_fields, Mapping) and a_fields:
        return dict(a_fields)
    for output in prompt_outputs:
        fields = output.get("production_fields")
        if isinstance(fields, Mapping) and fields:
            return dict(fields)
        nested = output.get("precheck")
        if isinstance(nested, Mapping) and isinstance(nested.get("production_fields"), Mapping):
            return dict(nested["production_fields"])
    fields = precheck.get("production_fields")
    if isinstance(fields, Mapping):
        return dict(fields)
    return {}


def _grade(value: Any) -> float | None:
    if isinstance(value, Mapping):
        for key in ("grade", "score", "value"):
            number = _number(value.get(key))
            if number is not None:
                return number
        return None
    return _number(value)


def _dimension_grades(
    normalized: Mapping[str, Any], prompt_outputs: Iterable[Mapping[str, Any]]
) -> dict[str, float]:
    """维度档位：先看归并结果里的证据，再回退调用 B 原始输出。"""

    grades: dict[str, float] = {}

    def absorb(mapping: Any) -> None:
        if not isinstance(mapping, Mapping):
            return
        for key, value in mapping.items():
            if key in DIMENSION_COLUMNS and key not in grades:
                grade = _grade(value)
                if grade is not None:
                    grades[key] = grade

    absorb(normalized.get("aesthetic_dimensions"))
    evidence = normalized.get("dimension_evidence")
    if isinstance(evidence, Mapping):
        inner = evidence.get("dimension_evidence")
        if isinstance(inner, Mapping):
            for scope in ("common", "specific"):
                block = inner.get(scope)
                if isinstance(block, Mapping):
                    absorb(block.get("evidence"))
        absorb(evidence.get("evidence"))
    for output in prompt_outputs:
        absorb(output.get("dimensions"))
        nested = output.get("dimension_output")
        if isinstance(nested, Mapping):
            absorb(nested.get("dimensions"))
    return grades


def _latest_truth(db: Session, asset_id: int) -> tuple[str | None, int | None, datetime | None]:
    from .models import BaselineSetItem, BaselineTruthRevision

    item = db.scalar(
        select(BaselineSetItem)
        .where(BaselineSetItem.asset_id == asset_id)
        .order_by(BaselineSetItem.id.desc())
        .limit(1)
    )
    if item is None:
        return None, None, None
    revision = db.scalar(
        select(BaselineTruthRevision)
        .where(BaselineTruthRevision.baseline_set_item_id == item.id)
        .order_by(BaselineTruthRevision.revision.desc())
        .limit(1)
    )
    if revision is not None:
        return revision.expected_level, int(revision.revision), _utc_naive(revision.created_at)
    return item.expected_level, 0, _utc_naive(item.created_at)


def _latest_delivery(db: Session, run_key: str | None):
    from .models import DownstreamPublishLedger

    if not run_key:
        return None
    return db.scalar(
        select(DownstreamPublishLedger)
        .where(DownstreamPublishLedger.origin_run_key == run_key)
        .order_by(DownstreamPublishLedger.revision.desc(), DownstreamPublishLedger.id.desc())
        .limit(1)
    )


def _manual_resolution(db: Session, run_ids: Iterable[int]):
    from .failure_resolution_models import ManualFailureResolution

    ids = sorted({int(value) for value in run_ids})
    if not ids:
        return None
    return db.scalar(
        select(ManualFailureResolution)
        .where(
            ManualFailureResolution.production_run_id.in_(ids),
            ManualFailureResolution.status == "published",
        )
        .order_by(ManualFailureResolution.id.desc())
        .limit(1)
    )


def _content_record(db: Session, asset_id: int):
    from .models import ContentRecord

    return db.scalar(
        select(ContentRecord)
        .where(ContentRecord.asset_id == asset_id)
        .order_by(ContentRecord.id.desc())
        .limit(1)
    )


def _asset_version(db: Session, asset_id: int):
    from .models import AssetVersion

    return db.scalar(
        select(AssetVersion)
        .where(AssetVersion.asset_id == asset_id)
        .order_by(AssetVersion.version.desc())
        .limit(1)
    )


# --------------------------------------------------------------------------- #
# 投影
# --------------------------------------------------------------------------- #


def _empty_row() -> dict[str, Any]:
    row: dict[str, Any] = {column: None for column in ROW_COLUMNS}
    row["overridden"] = False
    row["wide_schema_version"] = WIDE_SCHEMA_VERSION
    return row


def build_material_row(db: Session, asset_id: int, *, run_hint: Any = None) -> dict[str, Any] | None:
    """从源表重算一行；素材不存在或既无结果又无运行时返回 None。"""

    from .models import Asset, WorkflowVersion

    asset = db.get(Asset, int(asset_id))
    if asset is None:
        return None
    result = _latest_result(db, asset.id)
    runs = _runs_for_asset(db, asset.id, run_hint=run_hint)
    if result is None and not runs:
        return None

    latest_run = runs[0] if runs else None
    result_run = None
    if result is not None and result.production_run_id is not None:
        result_run = next((run for run in runs if run.id == result.production_run_id), None)
        if result_run is None:
            from .models import ProductionRun

            result_run = db.get(ProductionRun, int(result.production_run_id))
    resolution = _manual_resolution(db, [run.id for run in runs])
    resolution_run = None
    if resolution is not None:
        resolution_run = next((run for run in runs if run.id == resolution.production_run_id), None)
    # 人工结论最高优先级：只要它比结果行绑定的运行更新（或结果行不存在），就以它为准。
    use_resolution = resolution is not None and (
        result_run is None or (resolution_run is not None and resolution_run.id >= result_run.id)
    )
    run = resolution_run if use_resolution else (result_run or latest_run)

    row = _empty_row()
    now = _now_utc()

    # ---- 身份 ----
    record = _content_record(db, asset.id)
    version = _asset_version(db, asset.id)
    snapshot = _run_snapshot(run) if run is not None else {}
    frozen = _frozen_input(snapshot)
    row["category_key"] = (run.category_key if run is not None else None) or asset.category_key
    row["content_key"] = (
        (record.content_key if record is not None else None)
        or (frozen.get("content_key") if isinstance(frozen.get("content_key"), str) else None)
    )
    row["sha256"] = asset.sha256
    row["source_uri"] = asset.source_uri or (version.source_uri if version is not None else None)
    row["width"] = asset.width
    row["height"] = asset.height
    row["source_system"] = (
        (record.source_system if record is not None else None)
        or (version.source_system if version is not None else None)
        or (frozen.get("source_system") if isinstance(frozen.get("source_system"), str) else None)
    )
    factory_task = frozen.get("factory_task") if isinstance(frozen.get("factory_task"), Mapping) else {}
    if run is not None:
        row["upstream_task_id"] = _positive_int(run.upstream_task_id) or _positive_int(factory_task.get("task_id"))
        row["upstream_img_id"] = _positive_int(run.upstream_img_id) or _positive_int(factory_task.get("img_id"))
        row["upstream_ll_id"] = _positive_int(run.upstream_ll_id) or _positive_int(factory_task.get("ll_id"))
        row["upstream_class_id"] = _positive_int(run.upstream_class_id) or _positive_int(
            factory_task.get("class_id_1")
        )

    # ---- 结果与运行 ----
    normalized: dict[str, Any] = {}
    node_outputs: dict[str, Any] = {}
    precheck: dict[str, Any] = {}
    history: list[Any] = []
    if result is not None:
        normalized = _json_object(result.normalized_result_json) or _json_object(result.scoring_json)
        node_outputs = _json_object(result.workflow_node_outputs_json)
        precheck = _json_object(result.precheck_json)
        history = [entry for entry in _json_list(result.correction_history_json) if isinstance(entry, Mapping)]
    downstream = _downstream_output(node_outputs)
    prompt_outputs = _prompt_outputs(node_outputs)

    # 灵感图身份：upstream_* 为 NULL，身份在下游节点 output.inspiration_task（回退冻结输入）。
    insp = downstream.get("inspiration_task")
    if not isinstance(insp, Mapping):
        insp = frozen.get("inspiration_task")
    if isinstance(insp, Mapping):
        row["insp_task_id"] = _positive_int(insp.get("task_id"))
        row["insp_cloud_id"] = _text(insp.get("cloud_id"), 120) if insp.get("cloud_id") is not None else None
        row["insp_project_id"] = (
            _text(insp.get("project_id"), 120) if insp.get("project_id") is not None else None
        )

    if result is not None and not use_resolution:
        row["final_level"] = result.level
        row["final_score"] = _number(result.score)
        row["model_level"] = normalized.get("model_level") or normalized.get("raw_level") or normalized.get("level")
        row["model_score"] = _number(normalized.get("score"))
        row["confidence"] = _number(result.confidence)
        row["track_key"] = _text(normalized.get("track_key"), 80) if normalized.get("track_key") else None
        hard_reject = normalized.get("hard_reject")
        row["hard_reject"] = bool(hard_reject) if hard_reject is not None else None
        row["hard_defect_action"] = (
            _text(normalized.get("hard_defect_action"), 80) if normalized.get("hard_defect_action") else None
        )
        row["hit_rules_json"] = _json_text(normalized.get("hit_rules")) if normalized.get("hit_rules") is not None else None
        calibration = normalized.get("recommendation_calibration")
        if isinstance(calibration, Mapping):
            row["rec_probability"] = _number(calibration.get("probability"))
            recommended = calibration.get("recommended")
            row["rec_recommended"] = bool(recommended) if recommended is not None else None
        row["evaluated_at"] = _utc_naive(result.created_at)
        row["model_id"] = result.model_id
        row["prompt_a_version"] = result.prompt_a_version
        row["prompt_b_version"] = result.prompt_b_version

    # ---- 业务字段 ----
    if use_resolution and resolution is not None:
        fields = _json_object(resolution.fields_json)
    else:
        fields = _production_fields(downstream, prompt_outputs, precheck)
    if fields:
        row["title"] = _text(fields.get("title"), 500)
        row["seo_title"] = _text(fields.get("seo_title", fields.get("seotitle")), 500)
        row["category_text"] = _text(fields.get("class_text", fields.get("category")), 200)
        row["style"] = _text(fields.get("style"), 200)
        row["cons"] = _text(fields.get("cons"))
        row["design"] = _text(fields.get("design"))
        row["tags_json"] = _json_text(fields.get("tags")) if fields.get("tags") is not None else None
        row["reason"] = _text(fields.get("reason"))
        row["hwreason"] = _text(fields.get("hwreason"))
        row["trait"] = _text(fields.get("trait"), 200)
        row["image_defects_json"] = (
            _json_text(fields.get("image_defects")) if fields.get("image_defects") is not None else None
        )

    # ---- 维度 ----
    for key, grade in _dimension_grades(normalized, prompt_outputs).items():
        row[DIMENSION_COLUMNS[key]] = grade

    # ---- 人工 ----
    last_correction = history[-1] if history else None
    human_override = downstream.get("human_override") if isinstance(downstream.get("human_override"), Mapping) else None
    if last_correction is not None:
        row["overridden"] = True
        row["override_field"] = _text(last_correction.get("node_path") or last_correction.get("node_type"), 300)
        if last_correction.get("node_type") == "final_level" and isinstance(last_correction.get("new_value"), str):
            row["override_level"] = last_correction["new_value"]
        row["override_actor"] = _text(last_correction.get("corrector"), 120)
        row["override_reason"] = _text(last_correction.get("reason"))
        row["override_at"] = _utc_naive(last_correction.get("corrected_at"))
        row["last_correction_at"] = row["override_at"]
    elif human_override is not None:
        row["overridden"] = True
        row["override_field"] = _text(human_override.get("field"), 300)
        row["override_level"] = _text(human_override.get("level"), 10)
        row["override_actor"] = _text(human_override.get("actor"), 120)
        row["override_reason"] = _text(human_override.get("reason"))
    if use_resolution and resolution is not None:
        row["final_level"] = resolution.level
        row["final_score"] = _number(resolution.score)
        row["overridden"] = True
        row["override_field"] = "final_level"
        row["override_level"] = resolution.level
        row["override_actor"] = _text(resolution.actor, 120)
        row["override_reason"] = _text(resolution.reason)
        row["override_at"] = _utc_naive(resolution.updated_at or resolution.created_at)
        row["last_correction_at"] = row["override_at"]
        row["manual_resolution"] = resolution.status
    elif resolution is not None:
        row["manual_resolution"] = resolution.status
    row["tier"] = TIER_BY_LEVEL.get(row["final_level"]) if row["final_level"] else None

    # ---- 真值 ----
    row["truth_level"], row["truth_revision"], row["truth_updated_at"] = _latest_truth(db, asset.id)

    # ---- 投递 ----
    delivery = _latest_delivery(db, run.run_key if run is not None else None)
    if delivery is not None:
        row["delivery_status"] = delivery.delivery_status
        row["delivered_at"] = _utc_naive(delivery.delivered_at)
        row["delivery_revision"] = int(delivery.revision)
        row["external_record_key"] = delivery.external_record_key or None
        row["target_contract_key"] = delivery.target_contract_key
        row["delivery_error"] = delivery.rejection_code or None

    # ---- 版本与时间 ----
    if run is not None:
        row["production_run_id"] = int(run.id)
        row["run_key"] = run.run_key
        row["run_mode"] = run.run_mode
        row["run_status"] = run.status
        row["workflow_version_id"] = int(run.workflow_version_id)
        workflow_version = db.get(WorkflowVersion, int(run.workflow_version_id))
        row["mechanism_version_name"] = workflow_version.version if workflow_version is not None else None
    elif result is not None and result.workflow_version_id is not None:
        row["workflow_version_id"] = int(result.workflow_version_id)
        workflow_version = db.get(WorkflowVersion, int(result.workflow_version_id))
        row["mechanism_version_name"] = workflow_version.version if workflow_version is not None else None
    row["updated_at"] = now
    row["wide_schema_version"] = WIDE_SCHEMA_VERSION

    # ---- 明细 ----
    detail: dict[str, Any] = {
        "normalized_result": normalized or None,
        "human_override": human_override,
        "recommendation_calibration": normalized.get("recommendation_calibration"),
        "correction_history": [
            {
                "correction_key": entry.get("correction_key"),
                "node_type": entry.get("node_type"),
                "node_path": entry.get("node_path"),
                "old_value": entry.get("old_value"),
                "new_value": entry.get("new_value"),
                "corrector": entry.get("corrector"),
                "corrected_at": entry.get("corrected_at"),
                "reason": entry.get("reason"),
            }
            for entry in history
        ],
        "manual_resolution": (
            {
                "id": int(resolution.id),
                "level": resolution.level,
                "score": int(resolution.score),
                "actor": resolution.actor,
                "status": resolution.status,
                "production_run_id": int(resolution.production_run_id),
            }
            if resolution is not None
            else None
        ),
        "evaluation_result_id": int(result.id) if result is not None else None,
    }
    row["detail_json"] = _json_text(detail)
    return row


# --------------------------------------------------------------------------- #
# 写入
# --------------------------------------------------------------------------- #


def _apply_row(target: Any, row: Mapping[str, Any]) -> None:
    for column in ROW_COLUMNS:
        setattr(target, column, row.get(column))


def _last_history_hash(db: Session, asset_id: int) -> str | None:
    return db.scalar(
        select(WideMaterialHistory.row_hash)
        .where(WideMaterialHistory.asset_id == asset_id)
        .order_by(WideMaterialHistory.id.desc())
        .limit(1)
    )


def _current_hash(db: Session, asset_id: int) -> str | None:
    """新鲜读 current（不用会话里缓存的旧对象），返回行哈希；还没有这一行返回 None。"""

    current = db.scalar(
        select(WideMaterialCurrent)
        .where(WideMaterialCurrent.asset_id == asset_id)
        .execution_options(populate_existing=True)
    )
    if current is None:
        return None
    return compute_row_hash({column: getattr(current, column) for column in ROW_COLUMNS})


@contextmanager
def _fresh_reader(db: Session) -> Iterator[Session]:
    """构建一行用的新鲜读会话（Astra 复审 R-F4）。

    业务 Session 的 identity map 里可能缓存着旧的源对象（生产 SessionLocal 为 autoflush=False、
    expire_on_commit=False），同一 Session 的 ORM 查询不会用数据库新值覆盖已加载属性：比较后写入冲突后
    重建，会拿"新 current 哈希"给"旧源数据"背书，照样写回旧值。
    做法：先把业务事务里未落库的改动 flush 下去，再在同一连接（同一事务）上开一个空 identity map 的
    只读 Session 构建。它读得到本事务已写入的内容和别的事务已提交的内容（MySQL READ COMMITTED），
    不动业务 Session 里的任何对象；join_transaction_mode="rollback_only"，关闭时不提交也不回滚外层事务。
    """

    db.flush()
    reader = Session(
        bind=db.connection(),
        autoflush=False,
        expire_on_commit=False,
        join_transaction_mode="rollback_only",
    )
    try:
        yield reader
    finally:
        reader.close()


def build_material_row_fresh(db: Session, asset_id: int, *, run_hint: Any = None) -> dict[str, Any] | None:
    """用新鲜读会话重算一行（所有写宽表的路径都应经过这里构建）。"""

    with _fresh_reader(db) as reader:
        return build_material_row(reader, int(asset_id), run_hint=run_hint)


def _write_row(
    db: Session, asset_id: int, row: Mapping[str, Any], *, event_kind: str, expected_hash: str | None
) -> dict[str, bool]:
    """比较后写入 current，并按需追加 history。返回 {'current_changed', 'history_appended'}。

    Astra 全仓复审 F4：原来先算行、再无条件覆盖。算行期间另一事务已把权威结果和宽表刷新成新值时，
    旧行会把 current 写回旧值，history 也追加旧值。现在锁住 current 行（MySQL READ COMMITTED 下
    FOR UPDATE 读到最新已提交值）再核对它是否仍是构建前读到的 ``expected_hash``，不是就抛
    WideMaterialConflict，由调用方重读重算。两个事务都以为还没有这一行时，后插入的一方撞主键，
    同样按冲突重算。
    """

    row_hash = compute_row_hash(row)
    current = db.scalar(
        select(WideMaterialCurrent)
        .where(WideMaterialCurrent.asset_id == asset_id)
        .with_for_update()
        .execution_options(populate_existing=True)
    )
    actual = (
        None
        if current is None
        else compute_row_hash({column: getattr(current, column) for column in ROW_COLUMNS})
    )
    if actual != expected_hash:
        raise WideMaterialConflict(f"wide_material_current changed during build: asset {asset_id}")
    current_changed = actual != row_hash
    if current is None:
        current = WideMaterialCurrent(asset_id=asset_id)
        db.add(current)
    _apply_row(current, row)
    history_appended = False
    if _last_history_hash(db, asset_id) != row_hash:
        entry = WideMaterialHistory(
            asset_id=asset_id,
            event_kind=event_kind,
            recorded_at=row.get("updated_at") or _now_utc(),
            row_hash=row_hash,
        )
        _apply_row(entry, row)
        db.add(entry)
        history_appended = True
    db.flush()
    return {"current_changed": current_changed, "history_appended": history_appended}


def _project(
    db: Session, asset_id: int, *, event_kind: str, run_hint: Any = None
) -> tuple[dict[str, Any] | None, str | None, dict[str, bool] | None]:
    """重算一行并比较后写入；构建期间被别的事务刷新过就重读重算，最多 CAS_ATTEMPTS 次。

    每次尝试都在新鲜读会话里构建（_fresh_reader），不用业务 Session 缓存的旧源对象。

    返回 (row, 构建前的 current 哈希, 写入结果)。row 为 None 表示素材没有可投影的内容，不写。
    重试用尽仍冲突时抛 WideMaterialConflict。
    """

    for _ in range(CAS_ATTEMPTS):
        expected = _current_hash(db, asset_id)
        row = build_material_row_fresh(db, asset_id, run_hint=run_hint)
        if row is None:
            return None, expected, None
        row_hash = compute_row_hash(row)
        if row_hash == expected and _last_history_hash(db, asset_id) == row_hash:
            # 什么都不用写：不锁 current，空刷新不增加锁竞争。
            return row, expected, {"current_changed": False, "history_appended": False}
        try:
            with db.begin_nested():
                outcome = _write_row(db, asset_id, row, event_kind=event_kind, expected_hash=expected)
        except (WideMaterialConflict, IntegrityError):
            continue
        return row, expected, outcome
    raise WideMaterialConflict(f"wide_material_current kept changing: asset {asset_id}")


def refresh_material(
    db: Session, asset_id: int | None, *, event_kind: str, run_hint: Any = None
) -> bool:
    """钩子入口：重算并写 current/history。不 commit；任何异常只记日志并返回 False。"""

    if event_kind not in EVENT_KINDS:
        logger.warning("wide_material: unknown event_kind %s for asset %s", event_kind, asset_id)
    if asset_id is None:
        logger.info("wide_material: skip refresh (%s) — asset_id unresolved", event_kind)
        return False
    try:
        # 构建只读；写入在 _project 里自带 savepoint，冲突或出错只回滚宽表这一步。
        row, _expected, _outcome = _project(db, int(asset_id), event_kind=event_kind, run_hint=run_hint)
        if row is None:
            logger.info("wide_material: asset %s has no result or run; skip (%s)", asset_id, event_kind)
            return False
        return True
    except Exception:  # noqa: BLE001 - 宽表永远不阻断主流程
        logger.exception("wide_material: refresh failed for asset %s (%s)", asset_id, event_kind)
        return False


def refresh_for_run(db: Session, run: Any, *, event_kind: str) -> bool:
    """按运行刷新：先找素材，找不到就跳过并记日志。"""

    try:
        asset_id = resolve_run_asset_id(db, run)
    except Exception:  # noqa: BLE001
        logger.exception("wide_material: resolve asset for run %s failed", getattr(run, "id", None))
        return False
    if asset_id is None:
        logger.info(
            "wide_material: run %s (%s:%s) has no resolvable asset; skip (%s)",
            getattr(run, "id", None), getattr(run, "source_type", None), getattr(run, "source_id", None), event_kind,
        )
        return False
    return refresh_material(db, asset_id, event_kind=event_kind, run_hint=run)


def refresh_for_run_key(db: Session, run_key: str | None, *, event_kind: str) -> bool:
    from .models import ProductionRun

    if not run_key:
        logger.info("wide_material: empty run_key; skip (%s)", event_kind)
        return False
    run = db.scalar(select(ProductionRun).where(ProductionRun.run_key == run_key))
    if run is None:
        logger.info("wide_material: run_key %s not found; skip (%s)", run_key, event_kind)
        return False
    return refresh_for_run(db, run, event_kind=event_kind)


# --------------------------------------------------------------------------- #
# 对账与回填
# --------------------------------------------------------------------------- #


def candidate_asset_ids(db: Session, *, since: datetime | None = None) -> list[int]:
    """需要投影的素材：有结果行或有运行的；since 只看该时间之后有变动的。

    运行只取 id/source_type/source_id 三列：全量时无结果行的运行有两三万条、快照上 GB，
    整对象载入会把应用容器（1 GiB）直接 OOM。能按 source_type 直接解析的不读快照，
    读过快照的运行随手 expunge 掉。
    """

    from .models import EvaluationResult, ProductionRun

    ids: set[int] = set()
    result_query = select(EvaluationResult.asset_id).distinct()
    if since is not None:
        result_query = result_query.where(EvaluationResult.updated_at >= since)
    ids.update(int(value) for value in db.scalars(result_query))
    run_query = select(ProductionRun.id, ProductionRun.source_type, ProductionRun.source_id)
    if since is not None:
        run_query = run_query.where(ProductionRun.updated_at >= since)
    else:
        # 全量时结果行已覆盖绝大多数；只补没有结果行的运行（失败/进行中）。
        from .models import EvaluationResult as _ER

        run_query = run_query.where(
            ~ProductionRun.id.in_(select(_ER.production_run_id).where(_ER.production_run_id.is_not(None)))
        )
    pending: list[int] = []
    for run_id, source_type, source_id in db.execute(run_query):
        asset_id = _resolve_asset_id_by_source(db, source_type, source_id)
        if asset_id is not None:
            ids.add(asset_id)
        else:
            pending.append(int(run_id))
    for run_id in pending:
        run = db.get(ProductionRun, run_id)
        if run is None:
            continue
        try:
            asset_id = resolve_run_asset_id(db, run)
        finally:
            db.expunge(run)
        if asset_id is not None:
            ids.add(asset_id)
    return sorted(ids)


def _resolve_asset_id_by_source(db: Session, source_type: Any, source_id: Any) -> int | None:
    """不读快照、只凭 source_type/source_id 能定下来的素材；定不下来返回 None。"""

    from .models import Asset, BaselineRegressionItem, ContentIngressEvent

    if source_id is None:
        return None
    if source_type in {"native_evaluation", "asset"}:
        asset_id = _positive_int(source_id)
        if asset_id is not None and db.get(Asset, asset_id) is not None:
            return asset_id
        return None
    if source_type == "baseline_regression_item":
        item = db.get(BaselineRegressionItem, int(source_id))
        return int(item.asset_id) if item is not None else None
    if source_type == "content_ingress_event":
        event = db.get(ContentIngressEvent, int(source_id))
        # 快路径只认事件冻结的素材绑定；没有绑定就返回 None，交给调用方按 resolve_run_asset_id
        # （与运行关联同一优先级）读运行快照。
        return _event_bound_asset_id(event) if event is not None else None
    return None


def reconcile(
    db: Session,
    *,
    since: datetime | None = None,
    asset_ids: Iterable[int] | None = None,
    event_kind: str = "run_state",
) -> dict[str, Any]:
    """重算并比对：current 缺失/不一致就修正（history 按 row_hash 去重追加）。不 commit。"""

    targets = sorted({int(value) for value in asset_ids}) if asset_ids is not None else candidate_asset_ids(db, since=since)
    summary: dict[str, Any] = {
        "checked": 0,
        "consistent": 0,
        "missing": 0,
        "mismatched": 0,
        "repaired": 0,
        "skipped": 0,
        "failed": 0,
        "conflicts": 0,
        "history_appended": 0,
        "mismatched_asset_ids": [],
    }
    for asset_id in targets:
        summary["checked"] += 1
        try:
            outcome = _reconcile_one(db, asset_id, event_kind=event_kind)
        except WideMaterialConflict:
            logger.warning("wide_material: reconcile kept conflicting for asset %s", asset_id)
            summary["conflicts"] += 1
            continue
        except Exception:  # noqa: BLE001
            logger.exception("wide_material: reconcile failed for asset %s", asset_id)
            summary["failed"] += 1
            continue
        summary[outcome["verdict"]] += 1
        if outcome["verdict"] == "mismatched" and len(summary["mismatched_asset_ids"]) < 200:
            summary["mismatched_asset_ids"].append(asset_id)
        if outcome["written"]:
            summary["repaired"] += 1
            if outcome["history_appended"]:
                summary["history_appended"] += 1
    return summary


def _reconcile_one(db: Session, asset_id: int, *, event_kind: str) -> dict[str, Any]:
    """单条对账：重算与 current 比较，不一致才比较后写入；构建期间被别人刷新过就重读重算。

    判定只看最后一次成功尝试：别的事务在对账期间已经把它刷新对了，就算 consistent，不算修复。
    """

    for _ in range(CAS_ATTEMPTS):
        expected = _current_hash(db, asset_id)
        row = build_material_row_fresh(db, asset_id)
        if row is None:
            return {"verdict": "skipped", "written": False, "history_appended": False}
        if compute_row_hash(row) == expected:
            return {"verdict": "consistent", "written": False, "history_appended": False}
        try:
            with db.begin_nested():
                outcome = _write_row(db, asset_id, row, event_kind=event_kind, expected_hash=expected)
        except (WideMaterialConflict, IntegrityError):
            continue
        return {
            "verdict": "missing" if expected is None else "mismatched",
            "written": True,
            "history_appended": outcome["history_appended"],
        }
    raise WideMaterialConflict(f"wide_material_current kept changing: asset {asset_id}")

===== FILE backend/app/wide_material_models.py =====
"""面向下游的素材大宽表（ADR-0111）。

两张表同一列集：``wide_material_current`` 每素材一行（asset_id 唯一，当前生效结论），
``wide_material_history`` 每次结论变化追加一行（event_kind + recorded_at + row_hash）。
两表由 ``app.wide_material`` 的同一套投影逻辑写入；下游只读同库视图
``downstream_material_current`` / ``downstream_material_history``（时间列转东八区）。

库内时间一律 UTC naive；只在视图里 ``CONVERT_TZ`` 成东八区。
``Base`` 必须从 ``app.database`` 取——从 ``app.models`` 取会成环。
"""

from __future__ import annotations

from datetime import datetime

from sqlalchemy import (
    BigInteger,
    Boolean,
    DateTime,
    Float,
    Index,
    Integer,
    String,
    Text,
)
from sqlalchemy.orm import Mapped, declarative_mixin, mapped_column

from .database import Base

WIDE_SCHEMA_VERSION = 1

#: history.event_kind 的合法值。
EVENT_KINDS = (
    "evaluated",
    "corrected",
    "manual_resolution",
    "truth_revised",
    "delivered",
    "run_state",
)

#: 视图里要转东八区的时间列（current）；history 另加 recorded_at。
TIME_COLUMNS = (
    "override_at",
    "last_correction_at",
    "truth_updated_at",
    "delivered_at",
    "evaluated_at",
    "updated_at",
)


@declarative_mixin
class WideMaterialColumns:
    """ADR-0111 列集 v1。★ 列建索引；索引名带表名前缀，避免两表撞名。"""

    # ---- 身份 ----
    category_key: Mapped[str | None] = mapped_column(String(40), nullable=True)
    content_key: Mapped[str | None] = mapped_column(String(320), nullable=True)
    sha256: Mapped[str | None] = mapped_column(String(64), nullable=True)
    source_uri: Mapped[str | None] = mapped_column(String(1000), nullable=True)
    width: Mapped[int | None] = mapped_column(Integer, nullable=True)
    height: Mapped[int | None] = mapped_column(Integer, nullable=True)
    source_system: Mapped[str | None] = mapped_column(String(120), nullable=True)
    upstream_task_id: Mapped[int | None] = mapped_column(BigInteger, nullable=True)
    upstream_img_id: Mapped[int | None] = mapped_column(BigInteger, nullable=True)
    upstream_ll_id: Mapped[int | None] = mapped_column(BigInteger, nullable=True)
    upstream_class_id: Mapped[int | None] = mapped_column(Integer, nullable=True)
    insp_task_id: Mapped[int | None] = mapped_column(BigInteger, nullable=True)
    insp_cloud_id: Mapped[str | None] = mapped_column(String(120), nullable=True)
    insp_project_id: Mapped[str | None] = mapped_column(String(120), nullable=True)

    # ---- 结论 ----
    final_level: Mapped[str | None] = mapped_column(String(10), nullable=True)
    final_score: Mapped[float | None] = mapped_column(Float, nullable=True)
    tier: Mapped[str | None] = mapped_column(String(20), nullable=True)
    model_level: Mapped[str | None] = mapped_column(String(10), nullable=True)
    model_score: Mapped[float | None] = mapped_column(Float, nullable=True)
    confidence: Mapped[float | None] = mapped_column(Float, nullable=True)
    track_key: Mapped[str | None] = mapped_column(String(80), nullable=True)
    hard_reject: Mapped[bool | None] = mapped_column(Boolean, nullable=True)
    hard_defect_action: Mapped[str | None] = mapped_column(String(80), nullable=True)
    hit_rules_json: Mapped[str | None] = mapped_column(Text, nullable=True)
    rec_probability: Mapped[float | None] = mapped_column(Float, nullable=True)
    rec_recommended: Mapped[bool | None] = mapped_column(Boolean, nullable=True)

    # ---- 业务字段 ----
    title: Mapped[str | None] = mapped_column(String(500), nullable=True)
    seo_title: Mapped[str | None] = mapped_column(String(500), nullable=True)
    category_text: Mapped[str | None] = mapped_column(String(200), nullable=True)
    style: Mapped[str | None] = mapped_column(String(200), nullable=True)
    cons: Mapped[str | None] = mapped_column(Text, nullable=True)
    design: Mapped[str | None] = mapped_column(Text, nullable=True)
    tags_json: Mapped[str | None] = mapped_column(Text, nullable=True)
    reason: Mapped[str | None] = mapped_column(Text, nullable=True)
    hwreason: Mapped[str | None] = mapped_column(Text, nullable=True)
    trait: Mapped[str | None] = mapped_column(String(200), nullable=True)
    image_defects_json: Mapped[str | None] = mapped_column(Text, nullable=True)

    # ---- 维度（3DSU 五维）----
    dim_model_detail: Mapped[float | None] = mapped_column(Float, nullable=True)
    dim_material_rendering: Mapped[float | None] = mapped_column(Float, nullable=True)
    dim_lighting: Mapped[float | None] = mapped_column(Float, nullable=True)
    dim_design_trend: Mapped[float | None] = mapped_column(Float, nullable=True)
    dim_visual_composition: Mapped[float | None] = mapped_column(Float, nullable=True)
    # ---- 维度（灵感图八维）----
    dim_composition_viewpoint: Mapped[float | None] = mapped_column(Float, nullable=True)
    dim_lighting_atmosphere: Mapped[float | None] = mapped_column(Float, nullable=True)
    dim_color_material: Mapped[float | None] = mapped_column(Float, nullable=True)
    dim_spatial_design_furnishing: Mapped[float | None] = mapped_column(Float, nullable=True)
    dim_visual_hierarchy: Mapped[float | None] = mapped_column(Float, nullable=True)
    dim_detail_completion: Mapped[float | None] = mapped_column(Float, nullable=True)
    dim_inspiration_reference: Mapped[float | None] = mapped_column(Float, nullable=True)
    dim_presentation_integrity: Mapped[float | None] = mapped_column(Float, nullable=True)

    # ---- 人工 ----
    overridden: Mapped[bool] = mapped_column(Boolean, default=False, server_default="0")
    override_field: Mapped[str | None] = mapped_column(String(300), nullable=True)
    override_level: Mapped[str | None] = mapped_column(String(10), nullable=True)
    override_actor: Mapped[str | None] = mapped_column(String(120), nullable=True)
    override_reason: Mapped[str | None] = mapped_column(Text, nullable=True)
    override_at: Mapped[datetime | None] = mapped_column(DateTime, nullable=True)
    manual_resolution: Mapped[str | None] = mapped_column(String(20), nullable=True)
    last_correction_at: Mapped[datetime | None] = mapped_column(DateTime, nullable=True)

    # ---- 真值 ----
    truth_level: Mapped[str | None] = mapped_column(String(10), nullable=True)
    truth_revision: Mapped[int | None] = mapped_column(Integer, nullable=True)
    truth_updated_at: Mapped[datetime | None] = mapped_column(DateTime, nullable=True)

    # ---- 投递 ----
    delivery_status: Mapped[str | None] = mapped_column(String(20), nullable=True)
    delivered_at: Mapped[datetime | None] = mapped_column(DateTime, nullable=True)
    delivery_revision: Mapped[int | None] = mapped_column(Integer, nullable=True)
    external_record_key: Mapped[str | None] = mapped_column(String(240), nullable=True)
    target_contract_key: Mapped[str | None] = mapped_column(String(160), nullable=True)
    delivery_error: Mapped[str | None] = mapped_column(String(80), nullable=True)

    # ---- 版本与时间 ----
    production_run_id: Mapped[int | None] = mapped_column(Integer, nullable=True)
    run_key: Mapped[str | None] = mapped_column(String(160), nullable=True)
    run_mode: Mapped[str | None] = mapped_column(String(40), nullable=True)
    run_status: Mapped[str | None] = mapped_column(String(30), nullable=True)
    workflow_version_id: Mapped[int | None] = mapped_column(Integer, nullable=True)
    mechanism_version_name: Mapped[str | None] = mapped_column(String(80), nullable=True)
    model_id: Mapped[str | None] = mapped_column(String(200), nullable=True)
    prompt_a_version: Mapped[str | None] = mapped_column(String(120), nullable=True)
    prompt_b_version: Mapped[str | None] = mapped_column(String(120), nullable=True)
    evaluated_at: Mapped[datetime | None] = mapped_column(DateTime, nullable=True)
    updated_at: Mapped[datetime] = mapped_column(DateTime)
    wide_schema_version: Mapped[int] = mapped_column(
        Integer, default=WIDE_SCHEMA_VERSION, server_default=str(WIDE_SCHEMA_VERSION)
    )

    # ---- 明细 ----
    detail_json: Mapped[str | None] = mapped_column(Text, nullable=True)


#: ★ 列：两表各自建普通索引。
INDEXED_COLUMNS = (
    "category_key",
    "content_key",
    "sha256",
    "upstream_task_id",
    "upstream_img_id",
    "upstream_class_id",
    "insp_task_id",
    "insp_cloud_id",
    "final_level",
    "tier",
    "track_key",
    "hard_reject",
    "overridden",
    "truth_level",
    "delivery_status",
    "production_run_id",
    "run_mode",
    "run_status",
    "workflow_version_id",
    "evaluated_at",
    "updated_at",
)


def _indexes(table: str) -> tuple[Index, ...]:
    return tuple(Index(f"ix_{table}_{column}", column) for column in INDEXED_COLUMNS)


class WideMaterialCurrent(WideMaterialColumns, Base):
    """每素材一行：当前生效结论。"""

    __tablename__ = "wide_material_current"
    __table_args__ = _indexes("wmc")

    asset_id: Mapped[int] = mapped_column(Integer, primary_key=True, autoincrement=False)


class WideMaterialHistory(WideMaterialColumns, Base):
    """每次结论变化追加一行；同素材相邻两行 row_hash 相同不追加。"""

    __tablename__ = "wide_material_history"
    __table_args__ = (
        Index("ix_wmh_asset_recorded", "asset_id", "recorded_at"),
        Index("ix_wmh_event_kind", "event_kind"),
        Index("ix_wmh_recorded_at", "recorded_at"),
        *_indexes("wmh"),
    )

    id: Mapped[int] = mapped_column(primary_key=True)
    asset_id: Mapped[int] = mapped_column(Integer)
    event_kind: Mapped[str] = mapped_column(String(30))
    recorded_at: Mapped[datetime] = mapped_column(DateTime)
    row_hash: Mapped[str] = mapped_column(String(64))


#: 投影写入的列（不含主键/history 专属列）。
ROW_COLUMNS: tuple[str, ...] = tuple(
    column.name
    for column in WideMaterialCurrent.__table__.columns
    if column.name != "asset_id"
)

===== FILE backend/app/windows_deploy.py =====
from __future__ import annotations

import argparse
import base64
import binascii
import hashlib
import json
import os
import platform
import shutil
import socket
import sqlite3
import stat
import subprocess
import sys
import tempfile
import uuid
from contextlib import contextmanager
from dataclasses import dataclass
from datetime import datetime, timezone
from pathlib import Path, PurePosixPath
from typing import Callable, Iterator, Mapping, Sequence

from .migrations.runner import MIGRATIONS
from .security import (
    DPAPI_MACHINE_REFERENCE_PREFIX,
    DPAPI_REFERENCE_PREFIX,
    DPAPI_SCOPE_CURRENT_USER,
    DPAPI_SCOPE_LOCAL_MACHINE,
    SecretStorageError,
    probe_windows_dpapi,
)


BACKUP_SCHEMA = "3d66-label-system-windows-backup"
BACKUP_SCHEMA_VERSION = 1
SUPPORTED_SCHEMA_VERSION = max(migration.version for migration in MIGRATIONS)
PYTHON_MIN = (3, 11)
PYTHON_MAX_EXCLUSIVE = (3, 13)
NODE_MIN_MAJOR = 20
NODE_MAX_EXCLUSIVE = 27
NPM_MIN_MAJOR = 10
NPM_MAX_EXCLUSIVE = 12
MAX_MANIFEST_BYTES = 2 * 1024 * 1024
REPARSE_POINT_FLAG = getattr(stat, "FILE_ATTRIBUTE_REPARSE_POINT", 0x400)
CREDENTIAL_NOTICE = (
    "DPAPI、Keychain、API Key 与登录会话均未备份；恢复后必须由目标机当前用户重新填写凭据。"
)
_WINDOWS_RESERVED_NAMES = frozenset(
    {
        "CON",
        "PRN",
        "AUX",
        "NUL",
        *(f"COM{index}" for index in range(1, 10)),
        *(f"LPT{index}" for index in range(1, 10)),
    }
)


class DeployError(RuntimeError):
    def __init__(self, code: str, message: str) -> None:
        super().__init__(message)
        self.code = code


@dataclass(frozen=True)
class DoctorReport:
    data_dir: Path
    database_exists: bool
    schema_version: int | None
    checks: tuple[str, ...]


@dataclass(frozen=True)
class BackupValidation:
    backup_dir: Path
    database_path: Path
    schema_version: int
    file_count: int
    manifest: dict[str, object]


def _absolute_path(path: Path, *, label: str) -> Path:
    expanded = path.expanduser()
    if not expanded.is_absolute():
        raise DeployError("PATH_NOT_ABSOLUTE", f"{label}必须是绝对路径：{path}")
    return Path(os.path.abspath(expanded))


def _is_reparse_point(path: Path) -> bool:
    try:
        metadata = os.lstat(path)
    except FileNotFoundError:
        return False
    return stat.S_ISLNK(metadata.st_mode) or bool(
        getattr(metadata, "st_file_attributes", 0) & REPARSE_POINT_FLAG
    )


def _reject_reparse_components(path: Path, label: str) -> None:
    absolute = _absolute_path(path, label=label)
    candidates = [absolute, *absolute.parents]
    for candidate in reversed(candidates):
        if _is_reparse_point(candidate):
            raise DeployError(
                "REPARSE_POINT_REJECTED",
                f"{label}路径不得包含符号链接、junction 或其他重解析点：{candidate}",
            )


def _reject_reparse_tree(root: Path, label: str) -> None:
    _reject_reparse_components(root, label)
    if not root.exists() or not root.is_dir():
        return
    pending = [root]
    while pending:
        current = pending.pop()
        try:
            entries = list(os.scandir(current))
        except OSError as exc:
            raise DeployError(
                "PATH_INSPECTION_FAILED",
                f"无法检查 {label}：{current}",
            ) from exc
        for entry in entries:
            path = Path(entry.path)
            if _is_reparse_point(path):
                raise DeployError(
                    "REPARSE_POINT_REJECTED",
                    f"{label}不得包含符号链接、junction 或其他重解析点：{path}",
                )
            if entry.is_dir(follow_symlinks=False):
                pending.append(path)


def _normalized_path(path: Path, *, label: str) -> Path:
    absolute = _absolute_path(path, label=label)
    _reject_reparse_components(absolute, label)
    return absolute.resolve(strict=False)


def _casefolded_parts(path: Path) -> tuple[str, ...]:
    return tuple(part.casefold() for part in path.parts)


def _is_same_or_descendant(path: Path, parent: Path) -> bool:
    child_parts = _casefolded_parts(path)
    parent_parts = _casefolded_parts(parent)
    return (
        len(child_parts) >= len(parent_parts)
        and child_parts[: len(parent_parts)] == parent_parts
    )


def _require_outside_repo(path: Path, repo_root: Path, *, label: str) -> None:
    candidate = _normalized_path(path, label=label)
    repository = _normalized_path(repo_root, label="代码仓库")
    if _is_same_or_descendant(candidate, repository):
        raise DeployError(
            "PATH_INSIDE_REPOSITORY",
            f"{label}不得位于代码仓库内：{path}",
        )


def _require_backup_outside_data(backup_root: Path, data_dir: Path) -> None:
    backup = _normalized_path(backup_root, label="备份目录")
    data = _normalized_path(data_dir, label="DATA_DIR")
    if _is_same_or_descendant(backup, data) or _is_same_or_descendant(data, backup):
        raise DeployError("BACKUP_DATA_OVERLAP", "备份目录与数据目录不得互相包含")


def _env_file_value(repo_root: Path, key: str) -> str | None:
    env_file = repo_root / ".env"
    _reject_reparse_components(env_file, ".env")
    if not env_file.is_file():
        return None
    try:
        lines = env_file.read_text(encoding="utf-8").splitlines()
    except OSError as exc:
        raise DeployError("ENV_READ_FAILED", "无法安全读取 .env") from exc
    for raw_line in lines:
        line = raw_line.strip()
        if not line or line.startswith("#") or "=" not in line:
            continue
        candidate_key, value = line.split("=", 1)
        if candidate_key.strip() != key:
            continue
        value = value.strip()
        if len(value) >= 2 and value[0] == value[-1] and value[0] in {"'", '"'}:
            value = value[1:-1]
        return value
    return None


def _safe_env_value(repo_root: Path, key: str, env: Mapping[str, str]) -> str | None:
    direct = env.get(key)
    if direct is not None and direct.strip():
        return direct.strip()
    return _env_file_value(repo_root, key)


def default_windows_data_dir(env: Mapping[str, str] | None = None) -> Path:
    effective_env = os.environ if env is None else env
    local_app_data = effective_env.get("LOCALAPPDATA", "").strip()
    if not local_app_data:
        raise DeployError(
            "LOCALAPPDATA_MISSING",
            "未检测到 LOCALAPPDATA，无法确定 Windows 默认 DATA_DIR",
        )
    return _absolute_path(Path(local_app_data) / "3d66-label-system", label="DATA_DIR")


def resolve_data_dir(
    repo_root: Path,
    *,
    explicit: Path | None = None,
    env: Mapping[str, str] | None = None,
) -> Path:
    effective_env = os.environ if env is None else env
    if explicit is not None:
        return _normalized_path(explicit, label="DATA_DIR")
    configured = _safe_env_value(repo_root, "DATA_DIR", effective_env)
    if configured:
        return _normalized_path(Path(configured), label="DATA_DIR")
    return _normalized_path(
        default_windows_data_dir(effective_env),
        label="DATA_DIR",
    )


def default_backup_root(env: Mapping[str, str] | None = None) -> Path:
    effective_env = os.environ if env is None else env
    user_profile = effective_env.get("USERPROFILE", "").strip()
    if not user_profile:
        raise DeployError(
            "USERPROFILE_MISSING",
            "未检测到 USERPROFILE；请使用 --backup-dir 指定仓库外备份目录",
        )
    return _absolute_path(
        Path(user_profile) / "Documents" / "3d66-label-system-backups",
        label="备份目录",
    )


def _parse_major(raw: str, label: str) -> int:
    cleaned = raw.strip().lstrip("v")
    try:
        return int(cleaned.split(".", 1)[0])
    except (TypeError, ValueError) as exc:
        raise DeployError("VERSION_UNREADABLE", f"无法解析 {label} 版本") from exc


def _command_version(command: str) -> str:
    try:
        result = subprocess.run(
            [command, "--version"],
            check=True,
            capture_output=True,
            text=True,
            encoding="utf-8",
            errors="replace",
            timeout=10,
        )
    except (FileNotFoundError, subprocess.SubprocessError) as exc:
        raise DeployError("DEPENDENCY_MISSING", f"未检测到 {command}") from exc
    return (result.stdout or result.stderr).strip()


def _validate_runtime_versions(
    *,
    python_version: tuple[int, int, int],
    node_version: str,
    npm_version: str,
) -> None:
    if not (PYTHON_MIN <= python_version[:2] < PYTHON_MAX_EXCLUSIVE):
        raise DeployError("PYTHON_VERSION_UNSUPPORTED", "Python 必须为 3.11 或 3.12")
    node_major = _parse_major(node_version, "Node.js")
    if not (NODE_MIN_MAJOR <= node_major < NODE_MAX_EXCLUSIVE):
        raise DeployError("NODE_VERSION_UNSUPPORTED", "Node.js 必须为 20.x 至 26.x")
    npm_major = _parse_major(npm_version, "npm")
    if not (NPM_MIN_MAJOR <= npm_major < NPM_MAX_EXCLUSIVE):
        raise DeployError("NPM_VERSION_UNSUPPORTED", "npm 必须为 10.x 或 11.x")


def _existing_writable_ancestor(path: Path) -> Path:
    candidate = path
    while not candidate.exists() and candidate.parent != candidate:
        candidate = candidate.parent
    return candidate


@contextmanager
def _sqlite_connect_readonly(path: Path) -> Iterator[sqlite3.Connection]:
    uri = path.resolve(strict=True).as_uri() + "?mode=ro"
    connection = sqlite3.connect(uri, uri=True, timeout=10)
    try:
        connection.execute("PRAGMA busy_timeout=10000")
        yield connection
    finally:
        connection.close()


def _integrity_check(connection: sqlite3.Connection) -> None:
    if connection.execute("PRAGMA integrity_check").fetchall() != [("ok",)]:
        raise DeployError("SQLITE_INTEGRITY_FAILED", "SQLite integrity_check 未通过")


def _table_exists(connection: sqlite3.Connection, table: str) -> bool:
    return (
        connection.execute(
            "SELECT 1 FROM sqlite_master WHERE type='table' AND name=?",
            (table,),
        ).fetchone()
        is not None
    )


def _database_schema_version(connection: sqlite3.Connection) -> int:
    if not _table_exists(connection, "schema_migrations"):
        return 0
    row = connection.execute("SELECT COALESCE(MAX(version), 0) FROM schema_migrations").fetchone()
    return int(row[0]) if row else 0


def _validate_supported_database(connection: sqlite3.Connection) -> int:
    _integrity_check(connection)
    version = _database_schema_version(connection)
    if version > SUPPORTED_SCHEMA_VERSION:
        raise DeployError(
            "FUTURE_SCHEMA_REJECTED",
            f"备份数据库迁移版本 {version} 高于当前代码支持版本 {SUPPORTED_SCHEMA_VERSION}",
        )
    return version


def _decode_dpapi_reference(reference: str) -> bytes:
    if reference.startswith("keychain:"):
        raise DeployError("CREDENTIAL_REFERENCE_UNSAFE", "Windows 数据库含 macOS Keychain 引用")
    if reference.startswith(DPAPI_REFERENCE_PREFIX):
        payload = reference.removeprefix(DPAPI_REFERENCE_PREFIX)
    elif reference.startswith(DPAPI_MACHINE_REFERENCE_PREFIX):
        payload = reference.removeprefix(DPAPI_MACHINE_REFERENCE_PREFIX)
    elif ":" not in reference:
        payload = reference
    else:
        raise DeployError("CREDENTIAL_REFERENCE_UNSAFE", "Windows 数据库含未知凭据引用")
    try:
        decoded = base64.b64decode(payload.encode("ascii"), validate=True)
    except (UnicodeEncodeError, binascii.Error, ValueError) as exc:
        raise DeployError("CREDENTIAL_REFERENCE_UNSAFE", "Windows 数据库含无效 DPAPI 密文") from exc
    if not decoded:
        raise DeployError("CREDENTIAL_REFERENCE_UNSAFE", "Windows 数据库含空 DPAPI 密文")
    return decoded


def _validate_windows_credential_references(connection: sqlite3.Connection) -> None:
    for table in ("model_configs", "optimizer_configs"):
        if not _table_exists(connection, table):
            continue
        rows = connection.execute(
            f"SELECT encrypted_api_key FROM {table} "
            "WHERE encrypted_api_key IS NOT NULL AND trim(encrypted_api_key) <> ''"
        ).fetchall()
        for row in rows:
            _decode_dpapi_reference(str(row[0]))


def doctor(
    repo_root: Path,
    *,
    data_dir: Path | None = None,
    env: Mapping[str, str] | None = None,
    platform_name: str | None = None,
    python_version: tuple[int, int, int] | None = None,
    node_version: str | None = None,
    npm_version: str | None = None,
    dpapi_probe: Callable[[], str] | None = None,
) -> DoctorReport:
    if (platform_name or platform.system()).casefold() != "windows":
        raise DeployError("PLATFORM_UNSUPPORTED", "该工具只允许在 Windows 上运行")
    root = _normalized_path(repo_root, label="代码仓库")
    required_files = (
        root / "backend" / "requirements.txt",
        root / "frontend" / "package-lock.json",
        root / "backend" / "app" / "launcher.py",
        root / "scripts" / "windows" / "start.ps1",
    )
    for required in required_files:
        _reject_reparse_components(required, "仓库必要文件")
        if not required.is_file():
            raise DeployError("REPOSITORY_INCOMPLETE", f"仓库缺少必要文件：{required}")
    venv_python = root / ".venv" / "Scripts" / "python.exe"
    _reject_reparse_tree(root / ".venv", "仓库内 .venv")
    _reject_reparse_components(venv_python, "仓库内 Python")
    if not venv_python.is_file():
        raise DeployError("VENV_MISSING", "仓库内 .venv 不存在，请先运行 install.ps1")
    frontend_index = root / "frontend" / "dist" / "index.html"
    _reject_reparse_tree(root / "frontend" / "dist", "前端构建")
    _reject_reparse_components(frontend_index, "前端构建")
    if not frontend_index.is_file():
        raise DeployError("FRONTEND_BUILD_MISSING", "前端生产构建不存在，请先运行 install.ps1")
    _validate_runtime_versions(
        python_version=python_version or tuple(sys.version_info[:3]),
        node_version=node_version or _command_version("node.exe"),
        npm_version=npm_version or _command_version("npm.cmd"),
    )
    effective_env = os.environ if env is None else env
    effective_data = resolve_data_dir(root, explicit=data_dir, env=effective_env)
    _require_outside_repo(effective_data, root, label="DATA_DIR")
    ancestor = _existing_writable_ancestor(effective_data)
    _reject_reparse_components(ancestor, "DATA_DIR 现有父目录")
    if not ancestor.is_dir() or not os.access(ancestor, os.W_OK):
        raise DeployError("DATA_DIR_NOT_WRITABLE", "DATA_DIR 的现有父目录不可写")
    if effective_data.exists() and not effective_data.is_dir():
        raise DeployError("DATA_DIR_INVALID", "DATA_DIR 不是目录")
    database = effective_data / "database" / "app.db"
    schema_version: int | None = None
    if database.exists():
        _reject_reparse_components(database, "数据库")
        if not database.is_file():
            raise DeployError("DATABASE_INVALID", "数据库路径不是普通文件")
        with _sqlite_connect_readonly(database) as connection:
            schema_version = _validate_supported_database(connection)
            _validate_windows_credential_references(connection)
    try:
        dpapi_scope = (dpapi_probe or probe_windows_dpapi)()
    except SecretStorageError as exc:
        system_error = (
            f"，系统错误 {exc.system_error}"
            if exc.system_error is not None
            else ""
        )
        raise DeployError(
            "DPAPI_ROUND_TRIP_FAILED",
            f"Windows DPAPI 回环失败（{exc.reason}{system_error}）",
        ) from exc
    if dpapi_scope not in {
        DPAPI_SCOPE_CURRENT_USER,
        DPAPI_SCOPE_LOCAL_MACHINE,
    }:
        raise DeployError(
            "DPAPI_SCOPE_INVALID",
            "Windows DPAPI 回环返回了未知存储范围",
        )
    checks = (
        "Windows 平台",
        "Python 3.11/3.12",
        "Node.js 20-26 与 npm 10/11",
        "仓库必要文件、仓库内 .venv 与前端生产构建",
        "仓库外 DATA_DIR 与 Windows 优先级",
        "路径不含符号链接、junction 或其他重解析点",
        "SQLite 完整性、迁移版本与 DPAPI 引用" if database.exists() else "首次启动数据目录（未创建）",
        f"Windows DPAPI {dpapi_scope} 内存回环",
    )
    return DoctorReport(effective_data, database.exists(), schema_version, checks)


def _hash_file(path: Path) -> str:
    digest = hashlib.sha256()
    with path.open("rb") as handle:
        for chunk in iter(lambda: handle.read(1024 * 1024), b""):
            digest.update(chunk)
    return digest.hexdigest()


def _copy_regular_file(source: Path, destination: Path) -> None:
    _reject_reparse_components(source, "源文件")
    source_before = os.stat(source, follow_symlinks=False)
    if not stat.S_ISREG(source_before.st_mode):
        raise DeployError("NON_REGULAR_FILE", f"只允许备份普通文件：{source}")
    destination.parent.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
    shutil.copyfile(source, destination, follow_symlinks=False)
    source_after = os.stat(source, follow_symlinks=False)
    if (
        source_before.st_dev,
        source_before.st_ino,
        source_before.st_size,
        source_before.st_mtime_ns,
    ) != (
        source_after.st_dev,
        source_after.st_ino,
        source_after.st_size,
        source_after.st_mtime_ns,
    ):
        raise DeployError("SOURCE_CHANGED_DURING_BACKUP", f"备份期间文件发生变化：{source}")


def _copy_images(source: Path, destination: Path) -> None:
    destination.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
    _reject_reparse_components(source, "images")
    if not source.exists():
        return
    if not source.is_dir():
        raise DeployError("IMAGES_INVALID", "images 不是目录")
    for current, directories, files in os.walk(source, followlinks=False):
        current_path = Path(current)
        _reject_reparse_components(current_path, "图片目录")
        relative = current_path.relative_to(source)
        target_dir = destination / relative
        target_dir.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
        for directory in directories:
            _reject_reparse_components(current_path / directory, "图片目录")
        for filename in files:
            _copy_regular_file(current_path / filename, target_dir / filename)


def _sqlite_backup(source: Path, destination: Path) -> None:
    _reject_reparse_components(source, "数据库")
    if not source.is_file():
        raise DeployError("DATABASE_MISSING", f"数据库不存在：{source}")
    destination.parent.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
    with _sqlite_connect_readonly(source) as source_connection:
        destination_connection = sqlite3.connect(destination)
        try:
            source_connection.backup(destination_connection)
            destination_connection.commit()
        finally:
            destination_connection.close()


def _sanitize_backup_database(database: Path) -> int:
    connection = sqlite3.connect(database)
    try:
        _integrity_check(connection)
        with connection:
            if _table_exists(connection, "session_tokens"):
                connection.execute("DELETE FROM session_tokens")
            for table in ("model_configs", "optimizer_configs"):
                if _table_exists(connection, table):
                    connection.execute(f"UPDATE {table} SET encrypted_api_key = NULL")
        connection.execute("VACUUM")
        version = _validate_supported_database(connection)
        _validate_sanitized_database(connection)
        return version
    finally:
        connection.close()


def _validate_sanitized_database(connection: sqlite3.Connection) -> None:
    if _table_exists(connection, "session_tokens"):
        row = connection.execute("SELECT COUNT(*) FROM session_tokens").fetchone()
        if row and int(row[0]) != 0:
            raise DeployError("SESSION_TOKEN_PRESENT", "备份数据库仍包含登录会话")
    for table in ("model_configs", "optimizer_configs"):
        if not _table_exists(connection, table):
            continue
        row = connection.execute(
            f"SELECT COUNT(*) FROM {table} "
            "WHERE encrypted_api_key IS NOT NULL AND trim(encrypted_api_key) <> ''"
        ).fetchone()
        if row and int(row[0]) != 0:
            raise DeployError("CREDENTIAL_PRESENT", "备份数据库仍包含凭据引用")


def _git_commit(repo_root: Path | None) -> str | None:
    if repo_root is None:
        return None
    try:
        result = subprocess.run(
            ["git", "-C", str(repo_root), "rev-parse", "HEAD"],
            check=True,
            capture_output=True,
            text=True,
            encoding="utf-8",
            errors="replace",
            timeout=10,
        )
    except (FileNotFoundError, subprocess.SubprocessError):
        return None
    commit = result.stdout.strip().lower()
    if len(commit) == 40 and all(character in "0123456789abcdef" for character in commit):
        return commit
    return None


def _manifest_files(backup_dir: Path) -> list[dict[str, object]]:
    files: list[dict[str, object]] = []
    for base in (backup_dir / "database", backup_dir / "images"):
        if not base.exists():
            continue
        for current, directories, filenames in os.walk(base, followlinks=False):
            current_path = Path(current)
            _reject_reparse_components(current_path, "备份目录")
            for directory in directories:
                _reject_reparse_components(current_path / directory, "备份目录")
            for filename in filenames:
                path = current_path / filename
                _reject_reparse_components(path, "备份文件")
                files.append(
                    {
                        "path": path.relative_to(backup_dir).as_posix(),
                        "sha256": _hash_file(path),
                        "size": path.stat().st_size,
                    }
                )
    return sorted(files, key=lambda item: str(item["path"]))


def _timestamp(now: datetime | None = None) -> tuple[str, str]:
    current = (now or datetime.now(timezone.utc)).astimezone(timezone.utc)
    return current.isoformat(timespec="seconds").replace("+00:00", "Z"), current.strftime(
        "%Y%m%dT%H%M%SZ"
    )


def _unique_backup_destination(root: Path, compact_timestamp: str) -> Path:
    base = root / f"3d66-backup-v{BACKUP_SCHEMA_VERSION}-{compact_timestamp}"
    if not base.exists():
        return base
    for index in range(1, 1000):
        candidate = root / f"{base.name}-{index:03d}"
        if not candidate.exists():
            return candidate
    raise DeployError("BACKUP_NAME_EXHAUSTED", "无法生成唯一备份目录名")


def create_backup(
    data_dir: Path,
    *,
    backup_root: Path | None = None,
    repo_root: Path | None = None,
    env: Mapping[str, str] | None = None,
    now: datetime | None = None,
) -> Path:
    source = _normalized_path(data_dir, label="DATA_DIR")
    root = _normalized_path(
        backup_root or default_backup_root(env),
        label="备份目录",
    )
    if repo_root is not None:
        _require_outside_repo(source, repo_root, label="DATA_DIR")
        _require_outside_repo(root, repo_root, label="备份目录")
    _require_backup_outside_data(root, source)
    if not source.is_dir():
        raise DeployError("DATA_DIR_MISSING", "DATA_DIR 不存在")
    database = source / "database" / "app.db"
    _reject_reparse_components(_existing_writable_ancestor(root), "备份目录现有父目录")
    root.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
    _reject_reparse_components(root, "备份目录")
    created_at, compact_timestamp = _timestamp(now)
    destination = _unique_backup_destination(root, compact_timestamp)
    temporary = Path(tempfile.mkdtemp(prefix=".3d66-backup-tmp-", dir=root))
    try:
        copied_database = temporary / "database" / "app.db"
        _sqlite_backup(database, copied_database)
        schema_version = _sanitize_backup_database(copied_database)
        _copy_images(source / "images", temporary / "images")
        files = _manifest_files(temporary)
        manifest: dict[str, object] = {
            "schema": BACKUP_SCHEMA,
            "schema_version": BACKUP_SCHEMA_VERSION,
            "created_at": created_at,
            "source": {
                "application": "3d66-label-system",
                "platform": "windows",
                "database_schema_version": schema_version,
                "git_commit": _git_commit(repo_root),
            },
            "files": files,
            "exclusions": {
                "logs_excluded": True,
                "environment_files_excluded": True,
                "session_tokens_cleared": True,
                "model_credentials_cleared": True,
                "keychain_content_excluded": True,
                "dpapi_content_excluded": True,
            },
            "restore_notice": CREDENTIAL_NOTICE,
        }
        (temporary / "manifest.json").write_text(
            json.dumps(manifest, ensure_ascii=False, indent=2, sort_keys=True) + "\n",
            encoding="utf-8",
        )
        os.replace(temporary, destination)
        return destination
    except Exception:
        shutil.rmtree(temporary, ignore_errors=True)
        raise


def _validated_relative_path(raw: object) -> PurePosixPath:
    if not isinstance(raw, str) or not raw or "\\" in raw:
        raise DeployError("MANIFEST_PATH_INVALID", "manifest 文件路径无效")
    path = PurePosixPath(raw)
    if path.is_absolute() or path.as_posix() != raw or any(
        part in {"", ".", ".."} for part in path.parts
    ):
        raise DeployError("PATH_TRAVERSAL_REJECTED", f"拒绝不安全相对路径：{raw}")
    for part in path.parts:
        if (
            any(ord(character) < 32 or character in '<>:"|?*' for character in part)
            or part.rstrip(" .") != part
            or part.split(".", 1)[0].rstrip(" ").upper() in _WINDOWS_RESERVED_NAMES
        ):
            raise DeployError("MANIFEST_PATH_INVALID", f"拒绝不安全 Windows 文件名：{raw}")
    if raw != "database/app.db" and not raw.startswith("images/"):
        raise DeployError("MANIFEST_PATH_FORBIDDEN", f"备份含未授权路径：{raw}")
    if raw.startswith("images/") and len(path.parts) < 2:
        raise DeployError("MANIFEST_PATH_INVALID", f"图片路径无效：{raw}")
    return path


def _enumerate_backup_files(root: Path) -> set[str]:
    found: set[str] = set()
    for base_name in ("database", "images"):
        base = root / base_name
        if not base.exists():
            continue
        _reject_reparse_components(base, "备份目录")
        if not base.is_dir():
            raise DeployError("BACKUP_LAYOUT_INVALID", f"{base_name} 不是目录")
        for current, directories, filenames in os.walk(base, followlinks=False):
            current_path = Path(current)
            _reject_reparse_components(current_path, "备份目录")
            for directory in directories:
                _reject_reparse_components(current_path / directory, "备份目录")
            for filename in filenames:
                path = current_path / filename
                _reject_reparse_components(path, "备份文件")
                if not stat.S_ISREG(os.stat(path, follow_symlinks=False).st_mode):
                    raise DeployError("NON_REGULAR_FILE", f"备份含非普通文件：{path}")
                found.add(path.relative_to(root).as_posix())
    return found


def _validate_backup_root_layout(root: Path) -> None:
    allowed = {"manifest.json", "database", "images"}
    for child in root.iterdir():
        _reject_reparse_components(child, "备份根目录入口")
        if child.name not in allowed:
            raise DeployError(
                "BACKUP_LAYOUT_INVALID",
                f"备份根目录含未授权入口：{child.name}",
            )


def validate_backup(
    backup_dir: Path,
    *,
    supported_schema_version: int = SUPPORTED_SCHEMA_VERSION,
) -> BackupValidation:
    root = _normalized_path(backup_dir, label="备份目录")
    if not root.is_dir():
        raise DeployError("BACKUP_MISSING", "备份目录不存在")
    _validate_backup_root_layout(root)
    manifest_path = root / "manifest.json"
    _reject_reparse_components(manifest_path, "manifest")
    if not manifest_path.is_file():
        raise DeployError("MANIFEST_MISSING", "备份缺少 manifest.json")
    if manifest_path.stat().st_size > MAX_MANIFEST_BYTES:
        raise DeployError("MANIFEST_TOO_LARGE", "manifest 超过大小上限")
    try:
        manifest = json.loads(manifest_path.read_text(encoding="utf-8"))
    except (OSError, UnicodeDecodeError, json.JSONDecodeError) as exc:
        raise DeployError("MANIFEST_INVALID", "manifest 不是有效 UTF-8 JSON") from exc
    if not isinstance(manifest, dict):
        raise DeployError("MANIFEST_INVALID", "manifest 顶层必须是对象")
    if (
        manifest.get("schema") != BACKUP_SCHEMA
        or manifest.get("schema_version") != BACKUP_SCHEMA_VERSION
    ):
        raise DeployError("MANIFEST_SCHEMA_UNSUPPORTED", "manifest schema/version 不受支持")
    exclusions = manifest.get("exclusions")
    required_exclusions = {
        "logs_excluded",
        "environment_files_excluded",
        "session_tokens_cleared",
        "model_credentials_cleared",
        "keychain_content_excluded",
        "dpapi_content_excluded",
    }
    if not isinstance(exclusions, dict) or any(
        exclusions.get(key) is not True for key in required_exclusions
    ):
        raise DeployError("MANIFEST_EXCLUSIONS_INVALID", "manifest 未证明敏感数据已排除")
    entries = manifest.get("files")
    if not isinstance(entries, list):
        raise DeployError("MANIFEST_FILES_INVALID", "manifest files 必须是数组")
    declared: set[str] = set()
    declared_casefolded: set[str] = set()
    for entry in entries:
        if not isinstance(entry, dict):
            raise DeployError("MANIFEST_FILES_INVALID", "manifest 文件条目无效")
        relative = _validated_relative_path(entry.get("path"))
        raw_path = relative.as_posix()
        folded = raw_path.casefold()
        if folded in declared_casefolded:
            raise DeployError("MANIFEST_DUPLICATE_PATH", f"manifest 路径重复：{raw_path}")
        declared.add(raw_path)
        declared_casefolded.add(folded)
        digest = entry.get("sha256")
        size = entry.get("size")
        if (
            not isinstance(digest, str)
            or len(digest) != 64
            or any(character not in "0123456789abcdef" for character in digest)
            or not isinstance(size, int)
            or isinstance(size, bool)
            or size < 0
        ):
            raise DeployError("MANIFEST_FILE_METADATA_INVALID", f"文件元数据无效：{raw_path}")
        file_path = root.joinpath(*relative.parts)
        _reject_reparse_components(file_path, "备份文件")
        resolved_file = file_path.resolve(strict=False)
        if not _is_same_or_descendant(resolved_file, root):
            raise DeployError("PATH_TRAVERSAL_REJECTED", f"路径逃逸备份目录：{raw_path}")
        if not file_path.is_file():
            raise DeployError("BACKUP_FILE_MISSING", f"备份文件缺失：{raw_path}")
        if file_path.stat().st_size != size or _hash_file(file_path) != digest:
            raise DeployError("BACKUP_TAMPERED", f"备份文件校验失败：{raw_path}")
    if "database/app.db" not in declared:
        raise DeployError("DATABASE_MISSING", "manifest 未包含 database/app.db")
    if _enumerate_backup_files(root) != declared:
        raise DeployError("BACKUP_UNDECLARED_FILE", "备份目录与 manifest 文件清单不一致")
    database = root / "database" / "app.db"
    with _sqlite_connect_readonly(database) as connection:
        _integrity_check(connection)
        schema_version = _database_schema_version(connection)
        if schema_version > supported_schema_version:
            raise DeployError(
                "FUTURE_SCHEMA_REJECTED",
                f"备份数据库迁移版本 {schema_version} 高于当前代码支持版本 {supported_schema_version}",
            )
        _validate_sanitized_database(connection)
    source = manifest.get("source")
    if (
        not isinstance(source, dict)
        or source.get("platform") != "windows"
        or source.get("database_schema_version") != schema_version
    ):
        raise DeployError("MANIFEST_SCHEMA_MISMATCH", "manifest 来源与数据库迁移版本不一致")
    return BackupValidation(root, database, schema_version, len(declared), manifest)


def _service_may_be_running(host: str, port: int) -> bool:
    bind_host = host.strip() or "127.0.0.1"
    if bind_host in {"0.0.0.0", "localhost"}:
        bind_host = "127.0.0.1"
    family = socket.AF_INET6 if ":" in bind_host else socket.AF_INET
    try:
        with socket.socket(family, socket.SOCK_STREAM) as probe:
            probe.bind((bind_host, port))
    except OSError:
        return True
    return False


def _app_bind(repo_root: Path, env: Mapping[str, str]) -> tuple[str, int]:
    host = _safe_env_value(repo_root, "APP_HOST", env) or "127.0.0.1"
    raw_port = _safe_env_value(repo_root, "APP_PORT", env) or "8080"
    try:
        port = int(raw_port)
    except ValueError as exc:
        raise DeployError("APP_PORT_INVALID", "APP_PORT 必须是整数") from exc
    if not 1 <= port <= 65535:
        raise DeployError("APP_PORT_INVALID", "APP_PORT 超出有效范围")
    return host, port


def _copy_database_exact(source: Path, destination: Path) -> None:
    _sqlite_backup(source, destination)
    with _sqlite_connect_readonly(destination) as connection:
        _integrity_check(connection)


def _create_rollback_snapshot(data_dir: Path) -> tuple[Path, bool, bool]:
    snapshot = Path(tempfile.mkdtemp(prefix=".3d66-rollback-", dir=data_dir.parent))
    database = data_dir / "database" / "app.db"
    images = data_dir / "images"
    had_database = database.exists()
    had_images = images.exists()
    try:
        if had_database:
            _copy_database_exact(database, snapshot / "database" / "app.db")
        if had_images:
            _copy_images(images, snapshot / "images")
        return snapshot, had_database, had_images
    except Exception:
        shutil.rmtree(snapshot, ignore_errors=True)
        raise


def _remove_sqlite_sidecars(database: Path) -> None:
    for suffix in ("-wal", "-shm", "-journal"):
        sidecar = Path(f"{database}{suffix}")
        _reject_reparse_components(sidecar, "SQLite sidecar")
        if sidecar.exists():
            if not sidecar.is_file():
                raise DeployError("DATABASE_INVALID", f"SQLite sidecar 不是普通文件：{sidecar}")
            sidecar.unlink()


def _replace_images(staged: Path, target: Path) -> None:
    displaced = target.parent / f".images-displaced-{uuid.uuid4().hex}"
